Conclusion: 将分子生成过程与可执行的化学验证和反馈引导的精化机制相结合，是提升文本到分子生成质量的有效途径，为未来相关工作提供了新思路。

Abstract: Text-to-molecule generation is typically formulated as a one-shot sequence generation problem, where a model directly maps target descriptions to molecular representations. However, molecular descriptions often contain informative structural constraints, and violating such constraints can change the molecular identity. This makes chemical verification and error correction important but underexplored. To fill this gap, we propose MolGVR, a chemistry-grounded Generator--Verifier--Refiner framework. The Generator infers structural evidence and generates candidate molecules. The Verifier addresses the lack of chemical validation by converting descriptions into chemical constraints and checking candidates against them. The Refiner addresses generation failures by revising candidates rejected by the Verifier. Experiments on ChEBI-20 and PCDes show that MolGVR improves exact-match performance. These results suggest that coupling generation with executable verification and feedback-guided refinement is an effective way to improve text-to-molecule generation.

</details>


### [70] [DreamQAS: Learning a Decision-Useful World Model for VQE-Efficient Quantum Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29491)
*Jiayang Niu,Yan Wang,Jie Li,Ke Deng,Azadeh Alavi,Muhammad Usman,Yongli Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了DreamQAS，一种基于模型的强化学习量子架构搜索框架，通过学习代价高昂的VQE反馈而非电路动力学，在五个分子任务中实现了更低的能量误差和更少的真实VQE调用次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于强化学习的量子架构搜索（RL-QAS）方法在扩展电路后需要重复优化变分量子特征求解器（VQE），尽管电路构建和动作合法性是确定性的且已知的，但仍需大量昂贵的真实VQE计算。这导致了计算效率低下的问题，亟需一种能够保留精确电路动力学的同时避免频繁真实VQE调用的方法。

Method: 提出DreamQAS框架：1）使用循环随机先验集成预测相对于经验能量边界的无预言机分数，仅学习昂贵的后VQE反馈；2）在显式合法电路上支持多步想象策略学习；3）引入基于排序的激活、不确定性感知的悲观主义和截断机制；4）采用选择性真实VQE验证形成可靠性控制的循环学习。

Result: 在15000个episode预算下，DreamQAS在五个分子任务中的四个上取得最低的平均冻结策略能量误差，在一个任务上取得第二低。在所有种子都能达到的精细误差目标下，四个任务使用的真实VQE调用次数减少了1.6x到2.0x，在BeH2-8q上减少了10.6x。反事实动作排序效用平均增加0.346，95%置信区间为[0.185, 0.507]。

Conclusion: DreamQAS建立了一种世界模型设计范式用于量子架构搜索，其价值在于提供决策有用的反馈而非精确的能量预测，证明了基于模型的强化学习在量子电路优化中的有效性，为减少量子算法设计中的计算开销提供了新思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement-learning-based quantum architecture search (RL-QAS) repeatedly optimizes a variational quantum eigensolver (VQE) after extending a circuit, although circuit construction and action legality are deterministic and known. We introduce DreamQAS, a model-based RL framework that preserves these exact circuit dynamics and learns only the expensive post-VQE feedback. A recurrent randomized-prior ensemble predicts an oracle-free score relative to an empirical energy frontier and supports multi-step imagined policy learning over explicit legal circuits. Ranking-based activation, uncertainty-aware pessimism and truncation, and selective real-VQE verification form a reliability-controlled learning loop. Under a common 15,000-episode budget and frozen evaluation for the RL methods, DreamQAS has the lowest mean frozen-policy energy error on four of five molecular tasks and the second-lowest on one. At fine-error targets reached by all seeds of both methods, it uses 1.6x to 2.0x fewer real VQE calls on four tasks and 10.6x fewer on BeH2-8q. Counterfactual action-ranking utility increases across all five tasks, with a mean increase of 0.346 and a 95 percent confidence interval of [0.185, 0.507], while direct greedy and beam use of the same model does not recover the gains of imagined policy learning. Ensemble disagreement also improves risk-coverage over random rejection on all three probed tasks. These results establish a world-model design for QAS whose value lies in decision-useful feedback rather than exact energy prediction.

</details>


### [71] [Adaptive FastOPD: Progress-Aware Rollout Horizon Expansion for Efficient On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29494)
*Qian Tan,Huaifei Liang,Xuanyu Zhu,Lei Jiang,Yuqiang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Adaptive FastOPD，一种进度感知的on-policy蒸馏策略，在保持最高性能的同时将训练时间减少49.1-71.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有on-policy distillation (OPD)的在线rollout过程计算成本高，尤其当少量长响应延迟批处理完成时。现有加速方法使用固定预算或绝对师生协议阈值控制rollout长度，无法反映不同模型和训练阶段的学习进度。

Method: 提出Adaptive FastOPD，一种进度感知策略：当当前边界区域的学习停滞且当前horizon被充分利用时，才扩展rollout horizon。前者通过相对于进入每个horizon时的师生信号值来确定，使扩展响应阶段特定进度而非预定义步数间隔或原始协议信号的绝对阈值；后者防止少量长响应触发rollout成本增加。

Result: 在两组师生对上，Adaptive FastOPD达到最高平均性能，同时相对于OPD 15K减少训练时间49.1-71.2%，且在一系列超参数设置下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Adaptive FastOPD通过进度感知的rollout horizon扩展策略，有效解决了OPD的计算成本问题，在显著降低训练时间的同时保持甚至提升模型性能，证明了进度感知策略在知识蒸馏中的有效性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense teacher supervision along student-generated trajectories, but its online rollout process incurs substantial computational cost, particularly when a few long responses delay batch completion. Existing acceleration methods typically control rollout length using fixed budgets or absolute teacher--student agreement thresholds, which may not reflect learning progress across different models and training stages. We propose Adaptive FastOPD, a progress-aware strategy that expands the rollout horizon only when learning near the current boundary region has plateaued and the current horizon is sufficiently utilized. The former is determined from four teacher--student signals measured relative to their values upon entering each horizon, making expansion responsive to stage-specific progress rather than a predefined step interval or an absolute threshold on the raw agreement signals, while the latter prevents a small number of long responses from triggering increases in rollout cost. Across two teacher--student pairs, Adaptive FastOPD achieves the highest average performance while reducing training time by 49.1--71.2\% relative to OPD 15K, and remains robust across a range of hyperparameter settings.

</details>


### [72] [The Grokked Illusion: True Equilibrium Mitigates Catastrophic Forgetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29503)
*Xiaotian Zhang,Lai Shun Chan,Yue Shang,Entao Yang,Ge Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 高熵神经网络在抗干扰鲁棒性上显著优于AdamW训练模型，完美泛化不等于同等鲁棒性，揭示了“泛化幻觉”现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已发现高熵解在泛化能力上具有优势（高熵优势），但一个关键问题尚未解答：这种优势是否延伸到模型鲁棒性？特别是，当模型需要学习新任务时，能否保持已学知识的能力（即抗灾难性遗忘）？传统训练指标（训练/测试性能）无法揭示学习表征的鲁棒性，这可能导致“泛化幻觉”——即表面完美泛化的模型实际上存在脆弱性。

Method: 1) 使用模运算中的grokking现象作为可控实验设置；
2) 对比AdamW训练的Transformer与通过Wang-Landau分子动力学采样的高熵模型（确保两者饱和性能相同）；
3) 设计噪声注入实验，通过强制两个模型完全记忆带随机标签的新数据来评估鲁棒性差异；
4) 使用奇异值分解（SVD）分析神经网络权重的有效秩，探究注意力层和MLP层的特征表示丰富度。

Result: 1) AdamW训练模型在噪声注入后遭受灾难性遗忘，原始任务测试准确率从100%骤降至75%以下；
2) 高熵模型保持约95%的测试准确率，展现出更强的抗干扰能力；
3) 高熵神经网络在噪声注入前后的注意力层和MLP层中均具有显著更高的有效秩；
4) 更丰富的特征表示可作为抵抗灾难性遗忘的缓冲器。

Conclusion: 完美泛化并不意味着同等鲁棒性，高熵解通过更高有效秩的丰富特征表示提供更强的抗干扰能力，为理解训练模型的鲁棒性机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: While neural networks are typically evaluated by their training and test performance, these metrics do not reveal how robust a learned representation is. Recent studies have shown that solutions occupying larger volumes in parameter space, as quantified by Boltzmann entropy, often exhibit superior generalizability compared to those reached by conventional optimization, a phenomenon known as the high entropy advantage. Here we ask whether this advantage persists beyond generalization. Specifically, we investigate models' robustness, the ability to retain the learned knowledge when the model is subsequently trained to acquire new information. Using grokking in modular arithmetic as a controlled setting, we design a noise injection experiment to evaluate the robustness difference between AdamW-trained transformers and high-entropy model sampled from Wang-Landau Molecular Dynamics with identical saturated performance. By forcing both models to fully remember new data with random labels, we find that AdamW-trained models suffer from catastrophic forgetting, with original task test accuracy dropping from 100% to below 75%, whereas the high-entropy models maintain approximately 95% test accuracy. We term this hidden fragility behind apparent generalization the "grokked illusion." Through singular value decomposition of the neural network weights, we discover that high-entropy neural networks possess significantly higher effective rank in attention and MLP layers both before and after noise injection, indicating richer feature representations can serve as a buffer against catastrophic forgetting. Our findings demonstrate that perfect generalization does not imply equal robustness, offering a new perspective on what makes a trained model robust to interference.

</details>


### [73] [TerraNova: A Foundation Model for the Anthropocene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29527)
*Carlos Rodriguez-Pardo,Massimo Tavoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TerraNova，一个能够同时处理连续网格化物理场和离散国家层面社会数据的基础模型，通过跨模态transformer和双对比学习目标实现地球系统与社会数据的几何耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类世的一个核心挑战是如何将物理地球和人类社会建模为一个耦合系统。现有方法的根本障碍在于几何表示的不匹配：物理地球观测为跨越国界的连续场，而社会数据则按行政单元报告。现有地球系统基础模型仅服务于第一种几何，而将其耦合到第二种几何需要进行有损的边界平均，导致信息损失。

Method: 提出TerraNova基础模型，在原生几何上训练1024个物理和社会记录（512个网格化地球系统场和512个国家指标）。设计专用编码器表示位置、国家、时间和任务，使用跨模态transformer将其融合为共享时空状态，并通过超网络生成查询特定的解码器，其证据头部返回预测分布。采用两个对比学习目标：(1)每个国家与其领土坐标之间的人口加权对齐；(2)与携带图像语义的预训练地理空间嵌入的对齐。

Result: 通过解码器读取的表示在与专门设计的地理空间编码器竞争中表现优异，同时覆盖了这些编码器未能表示的维度（时间、海洋和不确定性），并支持国家层面的能力。冻结的骨干网络能够从稀疏观测重建密集场，并能在消费级硬件上在几分钟内适应未见过的变量。

Conclusion: TerraNova成功实现了物理地球和社会系统在原生几何上的统一建模，为人类世的地球-社会耦合系统研究提供了一个能够处理时空动态、海洋区域和不确定性的基础模型，在保持竞争力的同时扩展了现有地理空间编码器的能力边界。

Abstract: A defining problem of the Anthropocene is to model the physical Earth and human societies as one coupled system, yet no learned representation spans their observational breadth. We argue the obstacle is geometric: the physical Earth is measured as continuous fields that ignore political borders, whereas societies are reported for administrative units. Earth-system foundation models serve the first geometry; coupling it to the second has required lossy averaging over borders. We introduce TerraNova, a foundation model trained on 1,024 physical and societal records in their native geometries: 512 gridded Earth-system fields and 512 national indicators. Dedicated encoders represent location, country, time and task, cross-modal transformers fuse them into a shared spatiotemporal state, and a hypernetwork generates a per-query decoder whose evidential head returns a predictive distribution. Two contrastive objectives couple the representation: a population-weighted alignment between each country and coordinates in its territory, and one to pretrained geospatial embeddings carrying image-derived semantics. Read out through that decoder, the representation is competitive with purpose-built geospatial encoders while spanning axes they do not represent (time, oceans and uncertainty) and supporting country-level capabilities. The frozen backbone reconstructs dense fields from sparse observations and adapts to unseen variables in minutes on consumer hardware.

</details>


### [74] [MOT-SR: Multi-Objective Tool-Augmented Scientific Equation Discovery with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29561)
*Boxiao Wang,Runxiang Wang,Kai Li,Chongming Li,Zhiwei Chen,Yifan Zhang,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MOT-SR框架，通过工具增强的结构先验提取和多目标优化改进符号回归，在40个标准任务和引力波天文轨道建模中取得优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的符号回归方法存在两个关键局限：(1)缺乏数据分析机制来揭示变量依赖关系，降低了方程发现效率；(2)大多采用仅关注拟合误差的单目标评估，忽视结构复杂性和泛化能力，导致模型过早收敛至局部最优。

Method: 提出多目标工具增强符号回归(MOT-SR)框架，包含两个协作LLM模块：元策略生成器负责选择工具并基于帕累托最优方程合成结构优化策略，方程生成器据此产生新候选方程。系统引入外部分析工具提取结构先验，通过维护动态帕累托前沿的多目标评估模块联合优化准确性、复杂性和泛化能力，实现策略与方程结构的闭环持续优化。

Result: 在40个标准任务上，MOT-SR在准确性、泛化性和效率方面均超越现有符号回归方法。在极端质量比旋进(EMRI)轨道建模这一空间引力波天文学关键问题上，所发现的可解释校正实现了最低的轨迹级积分误差。

Conclusion: MOT-SR通过工具增强和多目标优化的有效结合，能够实现长时程科学动力学的可靠建模，证明了该方法在科学发现任务中的实用价值和潜力。

Abstract: Symbolic Regression (SR) aims to discover analytical equations from observational data and plays a central role in scientific modeling. While recent Large Language Model (LLM) based approaches show promise, they face two limitations. First, they lack data analysis mechanisms for uncovering variable dependencies, which reduces the efficiency of equation discovery. Second, most methods rely on single-objective evaluation focused solely on fitting error. This neglect of structural complexity and generalization often causes models to converge prematurely to local optima, limiting their ability to explore the broader equation space. We propose Multi-Objective Tool-augmented Symbolic Regression (MOT-SR), a unified framework that integrates external analytical tools to extract structural priors and guide equation generation, while jointly optimizing for accuracy, complexity, and generalization via a multi-objective evaluation module that maintains a dynamic Pareto front. MOT-SR employs two collaborative LLM modules: a Meta Strategy Generator, which selects tools and synthesizes structural optimization strategies based on Pareto-optimal equations, and an Equation Generator, which produces new candidate equations accordingly. The system operates in a closed-loop manner, continuously refining both strategies and equation structures. Across 40 standard tasks, MOT-SR outperforms existing SR methods in accuracy, generalization, and efficiency. We further validate MOT-SR on extreme mass-ratio inspiral (EMRI) orbital modeling, an important problem in space-based gravitational-wave astronomy where small local errors can accumulate substantially over long-term evolution. The discovered interpretable correction achieves the lowest trajectory-level integration error on held-out configurations. These results demonstrate the potential of MOT-SR to enable reliable modeling of long-horizon scientific dynamics.

</details>


### [75] [A Neurosymbolic Approach for Explainable Early Diagnosis of Alzheimer's Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29530)
*Ranveer Singh,Pranuthi Tenali,Saurabh Mathur,Ameet Soni,Vaishali Phatak,Karla Lynch,Daniel Murman,Matthew Rizzo,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自动化流程从语言流畅性测试音频中提取AD进展指标，利用基础模型和贝叶斯网络成功恢复已知临床知识并发现新关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统阿尔茨海默病(AD)生物标志物识别依赖人工转录和专家分析，过程繁琐且限制规模，需要自动化方法从音频记录中直接提取可靠指标。

Method: 使用预训练基础模型处理原始音频，提取临床相关变量，构建贝叶斯网络(BN)用于推理AD进展标志物及其定性关系。

Result: 系统成功恢复已知临床知识，并发现语言标志物之间的新关系，验证了自动化流程的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了从音频到临床知识的自动化提取，为大规模AD标志物研究提供可行方案，有助于发现新的潜在进展指标。

Abstract: Identifying reliable Alzheimer's disease (AD) markers typically requires manual, labor-intensive transcription and expert analysis, limiting its scale. We introduce an automated pipeline that extracts qualitative knowledge about potential AD progression indicators directly from audio recordings of verbal fluency tests. Our method uses pretrained foundation models to process raw audio and extract clinically relevant variables to construct a Bayesian Network (BN); this BN is used to reason about the AD progression markers and infer their qualitative relationships. Our system successfully recovers known clinical knowledge and identifies novel relationships between linguistic markers.

</details>


### [76] [A Human-Centered Validation of the Explainability-Performance Coefficient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29614)
*Christian Oliva,Luis F. Lago-Fernández*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了模型无关的EPC评分指标，通过平衡特征稀疏性和模型性能来量化解释质量，验证了其能有效揭示网络激活与解释性能的关系，且与人类解释高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在高风险领域的快速应用增强了对可信赖可解释人工智能（XAI）的需求。然而，客观评估解释保真度以及使XAI指标与以人为中心的理解相一致仍然是关键的开放挑战。

Method: 提出了一种模型无关的指标——EPC评分，这是可解释性-性能系数（EPC）的扩展。该指标通过显式平衡特征选择稀疏性和保留模型性能之间的权衡来量化解释质量。研究在表格、文本和图像模态上进行了实证验证。

Result: EPC评分有效揭示了网络激活、数据维度和解释器性能之间的操作依赖关系。通过与独立的人类解释进行验证，证明较高的EPC评分与人类词汇情感判断和空间视觉注释高度一致。

Conclusion: EPC评分作为一种客观的XAI评估指标，不仅能够量化解释质量，还能与人类中心的解释保持一致，为高风险领域中的可信XAI提供了有效的评估工具。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of deep learning models in high-risk domains has intensified the need for trustworthy Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI). However, objectively evaluating explanation fidelity and aligning XAI metrics with human-centered understanding remain critical open challenges. In this work, we propose a model-agnostic metric, the EPC score, which is an extension of the Explainability-Performance Coefficient (EPC), that quantifies explanation quality by explicitly balancing the trade-off between feature selection sparsity and preserved model performance. Through an empirical validation across tabular, text, and image modalities, we show that the EPC score effectively uncovers operational dependencies among network activations, data dimensionality, and explainer performance. Furthermore, we validate the EPC score against independent human-based explanations, proving that higher EPC scores strongly align with human lexical sentiment judgments and spatial visual annotations.

</details>


### [77] [Pyramidal Width Can Increase Under Vertex Insertion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29555)
*Jinze Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过构造一个具体的反例，证明了Lacoste-Julien和Jaggi在2015年提出的关于顶点插入不会增加多面体pyramidal width的猜想是错误的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lacoste-Julien和Jaggi在2015年提出了一个猜想，认为在保持所有旧点仍为顶点的前提下，向多面体添加顶点不会增加其pyramidal width。本文旨在验证这一猜想是否成立。

Method: 作者构造了一个包含六个整数点的三维反例：多面体P有五个顶点（v₀到v₄），多面体Q通过添加顶点v₅得到。利用pyramidal width与facial distance的等价关系，通过整数支撑超平面验证面格结构，并使用有限理性计算评估每个facial distance。

Result: 实验结果表明，P的所有五个顶点在Q中仍为顶点，但PWidth(P)² = 48/353，PWidth(Q)² = 36/133。顶点插入使pyramidal width增加了因子√(1059/532) ≈ 1.4109，从而证伪了原猜想。

Conclusion: 本文成功构造了一个精确的反例，证明顶点插入确实可以增加多面体的pyramidal width，这推翻了Lacoste-Julien和Jaggi的猜想。研究还提供了一个无依赖的精确验证器以供验证。

Abstract: Lacoste-Julien and Jaggi conjectured in 2015 that the pyramidal width of a polytope cannot increase when a vertex is added, provided that every old point remains a vertex. We give an exact counterexample with six integer points in $\R^3$. For \[ P=\conv\{v_0,\ldots,v_4\},\qquad Q=\conv\{v_0,\ldots,v_5\}, \] where \[ \begin{aligned} v_0&=(-1,-3,-1), & v_1&=(3,2,-2), & v_2&=(0,2,1),\\ v_3&=(-1,-3,3), & v_4&=(-2,0,1), & v_5&=(-1,0,-2), \end{aligned} \] all five vertices of $P$ remain vertices of $Q$, but \[ \PWidth(P)^2=\frac{48}{353} \quad\text{and}\quad \PWidth(Q)^2=\frac{36}{133}. \] Thus vertex insertion increases pyramidal width by the factor $\sqrt{1059/532}\approx 1.410886779$. The proof uses the equivalence between pyramidal width and facial distance, certifies both face lattices by integer supporting hyperplanes, and evaluates every facial distance by a finite rational calculation. A dependency-free exact verifier accompanies the paper.

</details>


### [78] [When Does On-Policy Interaction Help? Representational Tradeoffs in Value-Based Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29617)
*Luca Viano,Antoine Moulin,Audrey Huang,Volkan Cevher,Philip Amortila,Dylan J. Foster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了OVI算法，证明了交互式模仿学习只需学习者能表示专家的价值函数，而不需要表示完整的专家策略，并证明了交互的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的模仿学习方法（如行为克隆）在学习者无法完美表示专家策略时会出现累积误差和性能瓶颈。虽然专家交互查询和价值函数估计被经验性地证明能提升性能，但其理论机制和相互作用关系尚不清楚。本文旨在深入理解这些改进的本质及其相互影响。

Method: 提出了OVI（Online Value-based Imitation）算法，这是一个交互式在线模仿学习算法。该算法在学习者自身的轨迹上查询专家，使用价值函数估计来生成策略。算法在能表示专家价值函数时具有统计效率，在获得线性最大化预言机时具有计算效率。同时提供了负面结果证明，展示了交互的必要性。

Result: 证明了专家交互可以放松对学习者表示能力的要求：只需学习者能实现专家的价值函数，而无需实现专家的策略本身。证明了在没有更强假设的情况下，任何离线模仿学习算法的复杂度必须与专家策略类复杂度相关。实验表明OVI优于离线策略方法（BC）、交互式策略方法（DAgger）和离线价值方法，尤其在学习者网络表达能力明显弱于专家时效果最显著。

Conclusion: 专家交互查询在模仿学习中具有关键作用，它能将表示要求从策略层面降低到价值函数层面，使得学习者可以在更低的表示能力要求下达到更好的性能。这为理解模仿学习中的表示差距和算法设计提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Imitation learning (IL)---training an agent to replicate expert behavior from demonstrations---underpins applications from robotics to language model training. Standard approaches such as Behavior Cloning (BC) are known to suffer from compounding errors and performance plateaus, particularly when the learner cannot perfectly represent the expert's policy (as is typical, e.g., in distillation). Two interventions are widely understood empirically to improve performance: querying the expert interactively along the learner's own trajectories, and using value function estimation en route to generating a policy rather than directly fitting the expert's full action distribution.
  We investigate the nature of these improvements and their potentially surprising interplay. Our main finding is that expert interaction relaxes the representational demands on the learner: one only needs a model capable of realizing the expert's value function, bypassing the (often stricter) requirement of realizing the expert's policy itself. Concretely, we introduce OVI, an interactive on-policy IL algorithm that is statistically efficient whenever the learner can represent the expert's value function and computationally efficient given access to a linear maximization oracle. We complement this with a negative result showing that interaction is necessary. Namely, without stronger assumptions beyond expert-value realizability alone, any offline IL algorithm must scale with the complexity of the expert policy class. Our findings bear out empirically. OVI outperforms offline policy-based (BC), interactive policy-based (DAgger), and offline value-based IL methods, with the largest gains when the learner network is substantially less expressive than the expert's.

</details>


### [79] [CENDRe: Concept Extraction with Natural Domain Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29621)
*Antonia Holzapfel,Andres Felipe Posada Moreno,Sebastian Trimpe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了CENDRe方法，用于CNN时间序列分类中的概念提取，解决了现有方法仅限于时域、需预定义概念数量和定位不准确的问题，能同时提取时域和频域的概念，并自动确定概念数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列概念提取方法存在三个局限性：仅在时域操作而忽略频率特征、需要预定义概念数量、生成的定位与模型实际使用的区域不对齐，这在关键领域部署CNN时限制了对其预测驱动因素的理解。

Method: 提出CENDRe方法：1）通过两阶段聚类发现概念，使用轮廓系数引导的聚合自动选择概念数量；2）通过概念存在分数的梯度进行概念定位，生成聚焦于驱动概念区域的掩码；3）通过可微分可逆映射（如傅里叶变换）将梯度传播到频域，实现频域定位；4）为每个概念分配量化其对各类别贡献的相关性分数。

Result: 在合成基准测试中，CENDRe达到了与最先进概念提取方法相当的表示正确性，显著更高的重要性正确性；在真实轴承故障数据上，CENDRe提取了驱动模型预测的频带，定位于故障诊断常用的检查区域，提供了时域方法无法提供的模型评估证据。

Conclusion: CENDRe成功解决了现有时间序列概念提取方法的局限性，能够同时提取时域和频域概念，自动确定概念数量，生成准确的定位，为关键领域的CNN部署提供了更好的可解释性和可信赖性评估手段。

Abstract: Convolutional neural networks (CNNs) are widely used for time-series classification, but their deployment in critical domains requires understanding the temporal and spectral patterns that drive their predictions. Concept extraction (CE) methods identify such patterns by analyzing representations within the models' latent space. However, existing time-series CE methods have three limitations: they operate only in the time domain and overlook frequency features, predefine the number of concepts, and produce localizations misaligned with the regions the model uses. We address these limitations by proposing CENDRe, a concept extraction method for CNNs. It first discovers concepts by clustering per-timestep latent representations in two stages, where silhouette-guided aggregation selects the number of concepts automatically. Then, it localizes each concept through gradients of a presence score that contrasts the latent representations with their prototypes, producing masks that concentrate on the regions driving the concept. These gradients, propagated through a differentiable invertible mapping of the input such as a Fourier transform, yield localizations for the same concepts in the frequency domain. Finally, each concept receives a relevance score that quantifies its contribution to each class. On synthetic benchmarks, CENDRe achieves representation correctness comparable to state-of-the-art CE methods and significantly higher importance correctness. On real bearing-fault data, CENDRe extracts the frequency bands driving the model's predictions, located in regions commonly inspected for fault diagnosis, producing evidence to assess the model that time-domain CE methods cannot.

</details>


### [80] [Convergence and Regret of the Policy Gradient for Multi-Armed Bandits in Diffusion Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29593)
*Yanwei Jia,Du Ouyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩散环境下多臂赌博机问题的策略梯度更新，证明了在任意常数学习率下几乎必然收敛到最优臂，并在学习率低于阈值时获得了O(log T)的遗憾上界。通过构造新的李雅普诺夫函数改进了已有分析，并展示了SDE工具在策略梯度分析中的透明性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究（Lattimore 2026a）对扩散环境下的策略梯度算法分析存在改进空间，需要更透明的分析方法来理解连续时间强化学习框架下的策略梯度更新特性。同时，探索SDE工具在策略梯度分析中的应用价值，以及将连续时间分析扩展到离散时间算法的可能性。

Method: 在连续时间强化学习框架下，采用logit参数化的随机策略研究多臂赌博机问题。核心方法是构造一个新颖的李雅普诺夫函数来分析策略梯度更新的动态行为，利用随机微分方程（SDE）的工具进行理论分析，并将该方法推广到离散时间算法。

Result: 1）证明了策略梯度更新在任意常数学习率下几乎必然收敛到最优臂；2）当常数学习率低于某个时不变阈值时，推导出非渐近遗憾上界，遗憾界为O(log T)；3）该分析方法比Lattimore (2026a)更为精细和透明；4）构造的李雅普诺夫函数同样适用于离散时间策略梯度算法的分析。

Conclusion: 本文成功地将SDE工具应用于策略梯度分析，通过新颖的李雅普诺夫函数实现了比现有文献更优的理论结果。不仅获得了几乎必然收敛性和O(log T)遗憾界，还展示了该方法在连续和离散时间设置下的通用性，为扩散环境下的强化学习算法分析提供了新的方法论。

Abstract: This paper studies the policy gradient update for a multi-arm bandit problem in diffusion environment that is described by a stochastic differential equation (SDE) under the continuous-time reinforcement learning framework by Wang et al. (2020), Jia and Zhou (2022b). With the logit parameterization for the stochastic policy, we show that it converges almost surely to the optimal arm under an arbitrary constant learning rate. Furthermore, we derive the non-asymptotic regret upper bound when the constant learning rate is below a time-invariant threshold; and the regret bound has order $O(\log T)$. We improve the analysis in Lattimore (2026a) for the same SDE by constructing a novel Lyapunov function and demonstrate the transparency of analyzing policy gradient using the tools in SDEs. In addition, the same Lyapunov function is also helpful in analyzing the discrete-time policy gradient algorithm.

</details>


### [81] [The Parts Are Greater Than the Sum: Automated Task Sequencing for Efficient Training of Multi-Policy LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29601)
*Jiajia Tang,Sizhe Yuen,Francisco Gomez Medina,Yali Du,Adam Sobey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了自动多策略PEFT架构，通过优化路径组织解决异构任务序列中的干扰问题，在相同参数预算下性能优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PEFT方法在异构任务序列上使用共享低秩适配器(LoRA)时，单一共享优化空间会产生干扰，导致迁移性能差和灾难性遗忘。现有方法主要通过增加参数容量或组合多个适配器来提升表达能力，但仍依赖共享优化路径，无法从根本上解决问题。

Method: 提出优化路径组织框架，实现为自动多策略PEFT架构。通过任务分组和任务排序，在固定参数预算下自动组织优化兼容的适配路径；采用独立量化的低秩适配器(QLoRA)实现组织后的优化路径，使异构任务在解耦的适配空间中优化，同时保持兼容任务间的正向迁移。

Result: 在TRACE基准上的实验表明，从传统单策略PEFT到多策略PEFT性能持续提升，提出的自动多策略框架在相同可训练容量下达到最优性能44.78，验证了优化路径组织比单纯增加适配器容量更有效。

Conclusion: 优化路径组织策略比简单增加适配器容量更能有效解决异构参数高效微调问题，为异构任务序列学习提供了新的解决思路，证明了解耦优化空间的重要性。

Abstract: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) commonly adapts large language models using a single shared Low-Rank Adapter (LoRA). This shared optimization space often suffers from interference when adapting heterogeneous task sequences, leading to poor transfer and catastrophic forgetting. Existing approaches mainly improve adapter expressiveness by increasing parameter capacity or composing multiple adapters, yet they still rely on a shared optimization path. In this paper, we propose an optimization-path organization framework for parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large language models, implemented as an automatic multi-policy PEFT architecture. Specifically, optimization-compatible adaptation paths are automatically organized through task grouping and task sequencing under a fixed parameter budget. The organized optimization paths are implemented as independent Quantized Low-Rank Adapters (QLoRA), enabling heterogeneous tasks to be optimized in decoupled adaptation spaces while preserving positive transfer among compatible tasks. Experiments on the TRACE benchmark demonstrate that performance consistently improves from conventional single-policy PEFT to multi-policy PEFT, with the proposed automatic multi-policy framework achieving the best performance of 44.78 under the same trainable capacity. This suggests that optimization-path organization is more effective than simply increasing adapter capacity for heterogeneous parameter-efficient fine-tuning.

</details>


### [82] [Freeze, Then Select: Structured Field Adapters and Stability-Validated Weak Selection for PDE Discovery from Sparse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29665)
*Juncheng Zhong,Chenghuang Shen,Jianfeng Liu,Zhengdong Xiao,Longjiu Luo,Qianrong Wang,Wenjun Xu,Wenlian Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种冻结后选择的方法来从稀疏观测数据中发现偏微分方程，通过解耦方程选择与神经优化过程，显著提高了在稀疏MDBench数据集上的精确支持恢复率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏观测数据中发现PDE需要同时重建连续场并选择正确的微分项。现有耦合神经PDE发现方法在优化路径中存在三种行为：精确支持可能持续到训练结束、仅短暂出现或根本无法出现，这表明方程选择与神经优化过程相互耦合导致问题复杂化。

Method: 提出了一种冻结后选择方法，结合结构化场适配器和稳定性验证弱选择(SVWS)。首先，适配器将场分解为学习的空间特征和由三次样条表示的时间系数，无需PDE残差即可从观测数据训练。然后冻结场后，SVWS在独立的弱形式系统中识别循环项，重新拟合候选支持，并在留出的弱形式系统上选择最终方程。该方法还可应用于遗传规划生成的表达式。

Result: 在所有六个稀疏MDBench场景中，该方法获得了最高的精确支持恢复率，尤其在具有挑战性的Kuramoto-Sivashinsky动力学问题上，相比经典和神经基线方法表现出明显优势。此外，该方法还能从稀疏噪声观测中恢复未知非线性扩散函数的幂律形式。

Conclusion: 通过将方程选择与神经优化过程解耦，冻结后选择方法有效解决了耦合优化中支持恢复不稳定的问题，为从稀疏数据发现物理方程提供了更可靠的框架，在复杂动力学系统建模方面展现出优越性能。

Abstract: PDE discovery from sparse observations requires reconstructing a continuous field and selecting the correct differential terms. Our analysis of optimization paths in coupled neural PDE discovery reveals three behaviors: the exact support can persist to the end of training, appear only transiently, or fail to emerge. To decouple equation selection from neural optimization, we develop a freeze-then-select method combining a structured field adapter with Stability-Validated Weak Selection (SVWS). Trained from observations without a PDE residual, the adapter factorizes the field into learned spatial features and temporal coefficients represented by cubic splines. After freezing the field, SVWS identifies recurrent terms across independent weak-form systems, refits candidate supports, and selects the final equation on held-out weak-form systems. Beyond fixed libraries, we apply the same principle to expressions generated by genetic programming and recover the power-law form of an unknown nonlinear diffusion function from sparse, noisy observations. Across all six sparse MDBench regimes, our method attains the highest exact support recovery rate, with its clearest gains over classical and neural baselines on challenging Kuramoto-Sivashinsky dynamics.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [83] [DeltaServe: Host-Agnostic Co-Serving of Inference and Fine-Tuning for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28848)
*Jiaxuan Chen,Jianshu She,Ye Yuan,Rajat Ghosh,Karan Gupta,Qirong Ho,Xue Liu,Oana Balmau*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DeltaServe是一种主机无关的联合服务设计，可将空闲推理容量转换为LoRA微调吞吐量，同时保持推理SLO，在vLLM上实现了比LLMStation高2.9倍的微调吞吐量且保持100% SLO合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务系统为满足严格的延迟目标而按峰值负载配置，当流量低于峰值时存在大量GPU计算空闲。这种资源浪费促使研究者寻找一种方法来利用这些空闲资源进行其他计算任务。

Method: 提出DeltaServe，通过紧凑的钩子接口与现有推理引擎集成，仅需要多LoRA批处理支持。利用推理前填充和LoRA微调前向传播的共享执行结构，使用SLO感知调度器仅在推理余量充足时接受和执行微调任务。调度器由离线校准和在线优化的CUDA图感知延迟模型驱动。

Result: 与vLLM、SGLang和S-LoRA集成。在生产追踪数据上，DeltaServe在vLLM上实现了比LLMStation高2.9倍的微调吞吐量，同时保持100%推理SLO合规性（LLMStation为85%）。相比运行vLLM+torchtune的基线，在不使用额外硬件的情况下实现了39%更高的微调吞吐量并保持完全SLO合规性。

Conclusion: DeltaServe成功地将空闲推理容量转化为有用的微调吞吐量，无需额外硬件即可显著提升资源利用率，同时完全满足推理服务级别目标，为LLM服务系统的资源优化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: LLM serving systems are provisioned for peak load to meet strict latency targets, leaving substantial GPU compute idle whenever traffic falls below peak. We present DeltaServe, a host-agnostic co-serving design that converts this idle inference capacity into LoRA fine-tuning throughput while preserving inference service-level objectives (SLOs). DeltaServe integrates with existing inference engines through a compact hook interface that requires only multi-LoRA batching support. It exploits the shared execution structure of inference prefill and LoRA fine-tuning forward passes, and uses an SLO-aware scheduler to admit and execute fine-tuning only when sufficient inference headroom is available. The scheduler is driven by a CUDA-graph-aware latency model calibrated offline and refined online. We integrate DeltaServe with vLLM, SGLang, and S-LoRA. On a production trace from Company X, DeltaServe on vLLM delivers 2.9x higher fine-tuning throughput than LLMStation at 100% inference SLO compliance, versus 85% for LLMStation. It also achieves 39% higher fine-tuning throughput than a baseline running vLLM+torchtune, using no additional hardware and maintaining full SLO compliance.

</details>


### [84] [LayoutBench: Performance Benchmarking of Cloud Storage Layouts for Multimedia Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28880)
*Debopam Sanyal,Hongjie Chen,Alexey Tumanov,Joshua Kimball*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个面向多媒体数据存储布局的基准测试LayoutBench，系统评估了三种代表性存储布局策略在数据检索时间、数据传输量和成本方面的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代多媒体机器学习工作负载越来越多地将大规模数据集存储在云对象存储服务中。存储布局直接影响数据检索的速度和成本，然而现有基准测试主要关注数据库引擎和查询处理，缺乏针对多媒体数据检索的存储布局系统性评估。

Method: 提出LayoutBench基准，评估三种代表性存储布局策略：单个样本存储为独立对象（L1）、顺序打包为tar归档文件（L2）、以及组织为Parquet文件列格式（L3）。在ImageNet数据集上使用11种不同结果集大小的查询，在六种AWS EC2实例配置（涵盖不同网络带宽和内存层级）上测量检索时间、数据传输量和成本。

Result: L2通过连接复用实现比L1和L3更低的延迟，但在检索规模很大时优势消失。L3在大规模检索时速度最快，但由于行组粒度问题在所有查询规模下传输数据量显著增加，且需要更多内存。数据传输成本在所有布局中占总支出主要部分，L3的成本比L1或L2高一个数量级。

Conclusion: 不同存储布局在不同检索规模下各有优劣，L2适合中小规模检索，L3适合大规模检索但成本高昂。LayoutBench为多媒体数据存储布局决策提供了系统性评估工具，有助于指导实际应用中的存储策略选择。

Abstract: Modern multimedia machine learning workloads increasingly store large-scale datasets in cloud object storage services such as AWS S3. How these samples are physically organized in storage (i.e.,storage layout) directly affects how quickly and cheaply they can be retrieved. Yet the benchmarks used to guide storage decisions today focus on database engines and query processing, and none systematically evaluates how different storage layouts perform for multimedia data retrieval. We present LayoutBench, the first benchmark designed to fill this gap. It evaluates three representative layout strategies: storing each sample as an individual object (L1), sequentially packing samples into tar archives (L2), and organizing samples as columns in Parquet files (L3). We measure retrieval time, data transferred, and monetary cost using 11 queries of varying result-set sizes on ImageNet across six AWS EC2 instance configurations that span different network bandwidth and memory tiers. Our experiments reveal that L2 achieves lower latency than L1 and L3 through connection reuse, but loses this advantage as retrieval sizes become very large. L3 is the fastest for very large retrievals but transfers substantially more data across all query sizes due to row-group granularity, and requires significantly more memory. Across all layouts, data transfer cost dominates total expenditure, with L3 costing an order of magnitude more than L1 or L2.

</details>


### [85] [Rethinking AI Cloud Infrastructure for Agentic Serving Systems with the Aries Experimentation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29069)
*Leonid Kondrashov,Hongrui Liu,JooYoung Park,Boxi Zhou,Zonghao Liu,Chengzhi Lu,Riccardo Mancini,Esha Choukse,Haris Javaid,German Sviridov,Tao Peng,Chen Zhao,Anastasia Avdeeva,Aleksei Gusev,Marios Kogias,Luo Mai,Dmitrii Ustiugov*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Aries框架用于自主智能体的实验研究，通过生产数据实证分析揭示了传统LLM服务系统在智能体场景下的关键瓶颈，并提出面向智能体原生的服务系统设计愿景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体通过将重复推理与持久化上下文和沙箱工具执行相结合，挑战了传统的LLM服务模式。现有服务系统缺乏针对智能体工作负载特性的优化，需要深入理解智能体在生产环境中的实际行为特征和性能瓶颈，以指导面向智能体的服务系统设计。

Method: 提出Aries全栈实验框架，实现任务语义与执行配置的分离，重构跨组件智能体轨迹并关联系统遥测数据，通过统一接口暴露异构沙箱基质上的有状态工具执行。基于该框架在开放智能体框架和基准测试上开展可复现实验，并结合商业平台的生产轨迹数据进行验证。

Result: 研究发现：(1)以token为中心的指标遗漏了非推理瓶颈；(2)保留额外上下文带来的准确性收益递减，同时降低服务能力；(3)工具沙箱在长空闲期和短资源爆发之间交替，当前基于快照的状态管理导致激进暂停成本高昂。安全分析进一步强调了减少沙箱攻击面的必要性。

Conclusion: 需要设计智能体原生服务系统，围绕轨迹级指标、自适应上下文管理、弹性沙箱资源管理以及最小化攻击面的沙箱进行构建，以更好地支持自主智能体的服务需求。

Abstract: Autonomous agents challenge conventional LLM serving by coupling repeated inference with persistent context and sandboxed tool execution. We present Aries, a full-stack experimentation framework that separates task semantics from execution configurations, reconstructs cross-component agent trajectories with correlated system telemetry, and exposes stateful tool execution through a consistent interface across heterogeneous sandbox substrates. We use Aries to conduct reproducible experiments on open agent harnesses and benchmarks. We complement these experiments with production traces from a commercial platform, grounding low-level systems research in observed production behavior. Our results show that (1) token-centric metrics miss non-inference bottlenecks, (2) retaining additional context yields diminishing accuracy benefits while reducing serving capacity, and (3) tool sandboxes alternate between long idle periods and short resource bursts, while current snapshot-based state management makes aggressive suspension costly. A complementary security analysis further highlights the need to reduce the sandbox attack surface. We then discuss the vision for agent-native serving systems designed around trajectory-level metrics, adaptive context management, elastic sandbox resource management, and sandboxes with minimized attack surface.

</details>


### [86] [Knox: Fortifying Smart Spaces With Safety Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29198)
*Rishabh Menezes,Jadon T. Schuler,Kaimeng Zhu,Oliver Rogalski,Indranil Gupta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Knox系统，这是首个能够对物联网智能空间进行安全性检查的系统，包含新的安全性规范语言和静态验证算法，在保证高准确性的同时显著减少了检查时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网设备在智能空间和建筑中的应用日益广泛，形成了一类具有关键安全需求的分布式系统。然而，现有系统缺乏对这类智能空间进行安全性检查的有效方法，存在安全隐患。

Method: 提出了Knox系统，包含两个核心贡献：(1)安全性规范语言：一种新的用于智能空间安全性条款的语言；(2)静态安全检查：两种新的静态验证算法，用于验证智能空间中多个例程运行的多个安全属性。针对静态版本问题是NP难问题，提出了新颖且可解释的算法，并设计了进一步减少运行时间的优化方法。

Result: 使用真实数据集的分析和实验结果表明，Knox相比基线方法显著减少了检查时间，同时在捕捉安全违规方面提供了高准确性。

Conclusion: Knox系统成功解决了物联网智能空间中安全性检查的关键问题，为该领域的安全验证提供了有效的解决方案，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Internet of Things (IoT) devices in smart spaces and buildings are an emerging class of distributed systems with critical safety requirements. This paper presents Knox, the first system to enable safety checking in IoT-enabled smart spaces. Knox's contributions include (i) safety specifications: a new language for safety clauses in such smart spaces, and (ii) static safety checking: two new algorithms for static verification of multiple safety properties across multiple routines running inside a smart space. Since the latter problem is NP-hard, we present and analyze novel and explainable algorithms for the static version of the problem. We also present optimizations that further reduce runtime. Our analysis and experimental results with real datasets show that Knox reduces checking time significantly compared to baselines, while providing high accuracy in catching safety violations.

</details>


### [87] [Allocation Tracking and Parameter Checking for Parallel Programming Models using Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29303)
*Yussur Mustafa Oraji,Christian Bischof*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文扩展了CoVer框架的契约语言，引入通用参数检查和分配跟踪功能，提升了HPC程序正确性检查的错误覆盖范围，同时保持了跨编程模型和语言的通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的高性能计算（HPC）程序正确性检查工具通常局限于特定的并行编程模型（如MPI或OpenSHMEM）。虽然CoVer框架通过基于契约的方法解决了这一问题，但其有效性受限于底层契约语言的表达能力，限制了能够验证的错误类型范围。

Method: 本文扩展了CoVer契约语言，引入两个关键特性：（1）通用参数检查机制；（2）分配跟踪功能。这些扩展在保持跨编程模型和语言通用性的同时，能够捕获和检查更广泛的错误类别。

Result: 实验评估表明，扩展后的分析方法在多种编程语言中保持一致的准确性，验证了框架的通用适用性。虽然额外的运行时分析带来了性能开销，但这些改进显著提升了CoVer的实用性，并带来了显著的准确性提升。

Conclusion: 通过扩展契约语言的表达能力，CoVer框架能够在保持通用性的前提下显著提升错误检测能力，为HPC程序提供了更全面和实用的正确性检查解决方案。

Abstract: Correctness checking tools for High-Performance Computing programs are typically limited to specific parallel programming models such as MPI or OpenSHMEM. The CoVer framework previously addressed this by introducing a generic, contract-based approach that decoupled API requirements from the core tool. However, CoVer's effectiveness remains bounded by the expressiveness of its underlying contract language, restricting the types of errors it can verify. This paper presents an extension to the CoVer contract language designed to capture and check a broader range of error classes. Our extensions introduce generic parameter checking and allocation tracking, while keeping generality across both programming model and language. We evaluate these extensions and demonstrate that analysis accuracy remains consistent across multiple languages, reinforcing the framework's general applicability. While the additional runtime analyses naturally incur a performance overhead, these improvements greatly enhance CoVer's utility with a significant accuracy improvement.

</details>


### [88] [CARA: Exact Local Repair with Fresh One-Action Certification for Cloud Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29465)
*Xiyang Zhang,Yuanhe Tian,Hongzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了CARA方法，一种基于现有方案的定位流水线，通过将自适应提案生成与一次性部署决策分离，解决了基于模拟器的定位流水线中存在的搜索场景重用和标量化问题。在128个独立合成环境的前瞻性冻结研究中，该方法在所有五个评估端点上均优于基准方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模拟器的定位流水线在检查多个修复方案后，仅在多个可靠性指标同时改善时才进行部署。然而存在两个关键问题：(1) 重用搜索场景来测试所选行动会使名义证据失效；(2) 标量化可能会牺牲最弱的指标。这些问题影响了定位决策的可靠性和有效性。

Method: 提出证书对齐重组(CARA)方法，这是一种基于现有方案的流水线，将自适应提案生成与一次性部署决策分离。在有界的双主机邻域中，使用打包特定的可容许界限，在混合配对二进制/固定赌注证书顺序下恢复精确的前P个不同行动。然后使用保留视图选择一个行动，在新的配对认证开始之前提交其赌注计划。在符号独立性和条件矩假设下，错误声明四向改进的概率至多为0.05。

Result: 在128个独立合成环境和四个重复上下文的前瞻性冻结研究中，完整的故障关闭终端策略在每个环境的所有五个评估端点上均优于开发选择的相同主机数基准方案。J指标下降了3.57个百分点，连续负担下降了21-28%。匹配的顺序敏感性产生的绝对平均差距低于5.3×10^-4，并未建立顺序优势。

Conclusion: CARA成功地将精确的可审计提案恢复与选择鲁棒的新鲜认证相结合，用于最后一公里的局部修复。该方法证明了在保持统计有效性的同时，可以实现可靠的定位决策，为实际部署提供了可靠的保障。

Abstract: Simulator-based placement pipelines may inspect many repairs but deploy only when several reliability criteria improve together. Reusing search scenes to test the selected action invalidates nominal evidence, while scalarization can trade away the weakest criterion. We introduce Certificate-Aligned Recomposition (CARA), an incumbent-anchored pipeline that separates adaptive proposal generation from a one-use deployment decision. In a bounded two-host neighborhood, a packing-specific admissible bound recovers the exact top-$P$ distinct actions under a mixed paired-binary/fixed-bet certificate order. Held-out views then select one action and commit its betting plans before fresh paired Certification opens. Under the stated sign-independence and conditional-moment assumptions, the probability of falsely declaring four-way improvement is at most $.05$, irrespective of the size or complexity of upstream search. In a prospectively frozen study over 128 independent synthetic environments and four repeated contexts, the complete fail-closed terminal policy improved a development-selected same-host-count incumbent on all five Evaluation endpoints in every environment. $J$ fell by 3.57 percentage points and the continuous burdens by 21--28%. A matched ordering sensitivity produced absolute mean gaps below $5.3\times10^{-4}$ and does not establish order superiority. CARA thus couples exact auditable proposal recovery to selection-robust fresh certification for last-mile local repair.

</details>


### [89] [System-Wide Termination in Distributed Betweenness Centrality Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29474)
*Siamak Abdi,Lucia Cavallaro,Giuseppe Di Fatta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个轻量级的系统级终止检测算法，用于分布式介数中心性计算，解决了现有方法无法准确判断全局收敛的问题。实验表明该方法能实现零误差的安全终止，优于基于局部收敛的终止策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模网络上计算介数中心性成本高昂，分布式算法是可行方案，但现有分布式实现缺乏轻量级的系统级终止检测机制。这会导致局部收敛后产生额外消息传递，或因配置不当导致过早停止，影响计算效率和准确性。

Method: 提出了一种轻量级的系统级全局终止检测算法，使节点能够局部判断整个系统是否已收敛。开发了自定义Python模拟器，在合成网络（Erdos-Renyi和几何网络）和真实网络（邮件网络和道路网络）上，对比评估了全局终止检测方法与局部停止策略（节点基于自身估计稳定后独立终止）。

Result: 系统级终止检测方法使节点能够在检测到全局收敛后安全停止，在所有评估网络上实现零最终误差。相反，局部停止策略在异构网络上导致过早终止并产生误差。全局终止检测相比仅基于局部收敛的独立停止更加可靠。

Conclusion: 分布式中心性计算需要协调的停止机制。系统级终止检测算法能够有效解决分布式介数中心性计算中的终止问题，确保计算准确性，避免了局部停止策略在异构网络上的过早终止和误差问题。

Abstract: Computing betweenness centrality on large networks is inherently expensive, as it requires aggregating shortest-path dependencies across all pairs of vertices and becomes increasingly difficult to scale as network size grows. Scalable distributed algorithms can facilitate such computations, particularly when centralised processing is not feasible, and message exchanges must be carefully controlled, for example, in bandwidth-limited or very large-scale networks. However, existing distributed betweenness centrality implementations do not integrate a lightweight, system-wide termination detector. As a consequence, this can lead to extra messaging after local convergence or, if misconfigured, premature stops. In this work, a lightweight, system-wide global termination detection algorithm for this task is presented. The proposed method enables vertices to decide locally when the overall system has converged. The method is evaluated against a local stopping strategy in which vertices terminate individually once their own estimates stabilise. To compare these two approaches, namely global termination detection and local stopping, a custom Python simulator is implemented, and both approaches are tested on synthetic (Erdos-Renyi and geometric) and real (Email and Road) network topologies. Our results show that system-wide termination detection lets vertices stop safely after detecting global convergence, as indicated by zero final error in the evaluated networks, rather than stopping independently based only on local convergence. The local stopping approach, on the other hand, results in premature termination and some errors on heterogeneous networks. This work emphasises the need for coordinated halting in distributed centrality computation.

</details>


### [90] [SLIM: Saturation-Aware Lightweight Performance Modeling for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29575)
*Pol G. Recasens,Ferran Agullo,Yue Zhu,Chen Wang,Jordi Torres,Josep Ll. Berral*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM服务吞吐量饱和源于解码阶段注意力内核的DRAM带宽限制，提出SLIM性能模型和BCA配置顾问，在满足延迟约束下优化批处理配置并减少GPU内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将LLM服务吞吐量饱和归因于HBM/DRAM带宽限制，但缺乏深入的硬件层面分析和实验验证，导致无法准确理解和解决该性能瓶颈问题。

Method: 1）使用硬件性能分析技术对GPU进行详细特征刻画，定位吞吐量饱和的具体来源；2）提出SLIM半解析性能模型，基于Transformer计算和内存访问的理论公式预测推理吞吐量与延迟；3）设计BCA批处理配置顾问系统，在延迟约束下选择最优批处理配置并识别可节省的GPU内存。

Result: 实验证明吞吐量饱和源于解码阶段注意力内核的DRAM带宽饱和，其算术强度随活跃上下文长度增加保持近乎恒定；SLIM模型在各项评估场景中优于代表性基线，成功泛化到未见过的运行条件；BCA识别出OPT模型中最多55GB的可节省GPU内存分配，同时吞吐量损失极小。

Conclusion: 本研究从硬件层面揭示了LLM推理性能瓶颈的本质，提出的SLIM模型和BCA系统为LLM部署提供了精确的性能预测和资源优化能力，有助于在满足延迟约束的前提下实现更高的资源利用效率。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) serving commonly increases batch size to improve throughput, but performance eventually reaches a deployment-dependent plateau beyond which larger batches provide marginal gains while increasing latency and GPU memory consumption. Previous studies have attributed this behavior to HBM/DRAM bandwidth limitations, but the underlying causes have primarily been supported by conceptual arguments or high-level performance observations. As our first contribution, we present a detailed GPU characterization using hardware profiling techniques, demonstrating that throughput saturation originates in the attention kernels during the decode phase. Specifically, we show that their nearly constant arithmetic intensity as active-context lengths increases -not merely larger batch sizes- drives DRAM-bandwidth saturation, while the achieved compute throughput remains far below the hardware limit. Building on this analysis, we present the Batching Configuration Advisor (BCA), which selects the highest-throughput batching configuration satisfying a target latency constraint and identifies up to 55 GB of GPU memory allocation that can be avoided for the evaluated OPT models with minimal throughput loss. To enable these recommendations, we introduce SLIM (Saturation-Aware Lightweight Performance Model), a semi-analytical model that predicts LLM inference throughput and latency from analytical formulations of Transformer computation and memory traffic. Across the evaluated scenarios, SLIM outperforms representative performance-modeling baselines while successfully generalizing to previously unseen operating conditions.

</details>

### [91] [OpenClaw and Ollama in Agentic AI: Toward Fully Autonomous and Scalable AI Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28629)
*Konstantinos I. Roumeliotis,Ranjan Sapkota*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Agentic AI的分层架构，以OpenClaw-Ollama为例验证了全栈自主智能体系统的有效性，证明了持久记忆、工具使用等能力源于系统集成，并讨论了可扩展性、安全性等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从反应式大语言模型（LLMs）向持久、具备行动能力的系统快速转变过程中，Agentic AI在架构理解方面暴露出关键空白，尤其是在自主AI智能体的推理、编排和执行层的分离上。尽管近期有所进展，但设计和评估全栈Agentic系统的统一框架仍然有限。

Method: 提出了一种全面的Agentic AI分层架构，概述了从反应式LLM接口到具有记忆、规划和持续执行能力的持久、目标驱动的自主AI智能体的演进。以OpenClaw和Ollama作为全栈Agentic AI系统进行分析，其中Ollama作为LLM推理层，OpenClaw实现智能体运行时编排，整合推理、工具使用和动作执行。

Result: 对OpenClaw-Ollama架构的原型实验验证表明，持久记忆、工具使用和自适应决策等能力源于系统级集成而非独立模型，且随着架构复杂度的增加，性能持续提升。

Conclusion: 本研究确立了Agentic AI的统一架构基础，验证了全栈自主AI智能体的有效性，为构建可扩展、安全、可信的智能体系统提供了路线图。研究还指出了可扩展性、安全性、隐私、治理和评估方面的挑战，强调了需要鲁棒的基准测试和系统级设计。所有模型、代码和数据集均已公开发布以支持可复现性和基准测试。

Abstract: The rapid transition from reactive large language models (LLMs) to persistent, action-capable systems has exposed critical gaps in the architectural understanding of Agentic AI, particularly in separating inference, orchestration, and execution layers for autonomous AI agents. Despite recent advances, unified frameworks for designing and evaluating full-stack agentic systems remain limited. This paper presents a comprehensive, layered architecture for Agentic AI, outlining the evolution from reactive LLM interfaces to persistent, goal-driven autonomous AI agents with memory, planning, and continuous execution. We analyze OpenClaw and Ollama as a full-stack Agentic AI system, where Ollama serves as the LLM inference layer and OpenClaw enables agent runtime orchestration, integrating reasoning, tool use, and action execution. A prototype experimental validation of the OpenClaw-Ollama architecture demonstrates that capabilities such as persistent memory, tool utilization, and adaptive decision-making emerge from system-level integration rather than standalone models, with performance improving consistently as architectural complexity increases. The study further examines challenges in scalability, security, privacy, governance, and evaluation of agentic systems, highlighting the need for robust benchmarking and system-level design. Future directions include scalable multi-agent architectures, distributed autonomous systems, and human-aware Agentic AI frameworks for responsible deployment. Overall, this work establishes a unified architectural foundation for Agentic AI, validates the effectiveness of full-stack autonomous AI agents, and provides a roadmap for building scalable, secure, and trustworthy agentic systems. All models, code, and datasets are publicly released to support reproducibility and benchmarking.

</details>


### [92] [Can AI Evaluate AI Scientists? A Benchmarking Study of Autonomous Research Generation Systems Using Automated Multi-Model Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28631)
*Vaibhava Lakshmi Ravideshik,Mayank Kejriwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究建立了首个AI科学家系统基准测试协议，通过多模型LLM自动评审发现FARS系统生成的论文质量显著优于其他框架，验证了自动化评估方法的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI科学家系统具有加速科学发现的潜力，但评估和比较AI生成论文的质量仍是一个开放性挑战，缺乏标准化的评估基准。

Method: 提出并实施了一个严格的基准测试协议，利用前沿LLM（GPT-5.4、Gemini、Claude）作为自动化同行评审系统，从原创性、科学严谨性、清晰度和重要性四个核心维度评估论文；对四个领先的AI科学家框架（Sakana AI v1/v2、CycleResearcher、Data-to-Paper）在15个统一研究提案上生成的60篇论文与15篇FARS基准论文进行对比评估。

Result: FARS基准论文显著优于所有竞争框架，在1-5分制下平均得分2.14-2.47，而其他系统仅为1.00-1.87；Gemini和Claude评估结果高度一致（ρ=0.907），且与综合得分极强相关（ρ=0.961），但GPT-5.4一致性较弱（ρ≈0.32），表明其采用不同评估标准。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了首个AI科学家系统的定量基准，证明了多模型LLM评估为评估自主研究质量提供了一个可扩展、一致的框架。

Abstract: AI Scientist systems capable of autonomous research have the potential to significantly accelerate scientific discovery. However, evaluating and comparing the quality of AI-generated papers remains an open challenge. We propose and implement a rigorous benchmarking protocol using an automated peer-review system that harnesses frontier large language models to assess scientific papers across four core dimensions: originality, scientific rigor, clarity, and significance. We evaluate four leading AI Scientist frameworks: \textit{Sakana AI (v1 & v2)}, \textit{CycleResearcher}, and \textit{Data-to-Paper}. Each framework was run on a consistent set of 15 research proposals published by a commercial autonomous AI scientist company (FARS), generating 60 papers that we evaluate alongside 15 FARS benchmark papers. Using three independent LLM reviewers (GPT-5.4, Gemini, and Claude), we find that FARS benchmark papers significantly outperform all competing frameworks, achieving mean scores of 2.14--2.47 on a 1--5 scale compared to 1.00--1.87 for other systems. Notably, FARS scores are more than 2$\times$ higher than the next-best systems on Gemini and Claude evaluations. We find strong agreement among Gemini and Claude ($ρ$ = 0.907, $p < 0.001$), and both correlate extremely strongly with the synthesis score ($ρ$ = 0.961, $p < 0.001$), validating the reliability of automated evaluation. However, GPT-5.4 exhibits weaker agreement ($ρ\approx 0.32$), suggesting it evaluates papers using different criteria. These results establish the first quantitative benchmark for AI Scientist systems and demonstrate that multi-model LLM evaluation provides a scalable, consistent framework for assessing autonomous research quality.

</details>


### [93] [LLM Framework for Discovering Major Mathematical Conjectures: AI's Quest for the Next Riemann Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28632)
*Alizer Wong,Zixin Zeng,Yi Tan,Wenyuan Li,Xuhang Chen,Xingru Lai,Yang Shi,Liangsi Lu,Yanhui Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个三阶段的数学猜想发现流水线，包含区域搜索、反思验证和形式化验证（Lean 4），成功生成并通过了20个具有高度数学潜力的候选猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前重大数学猜想仍高度依赖专家直觉，缺乏系统化的方法来生成和验证具有实质性数学潜力的猜想，作者旨在解决这一方法论空白。

Method: 提出三阶段流水线：1）基于显式局部证据模块的区域搜索；2）反思验证，评估基础性、新颖性和潜在重要性；3）在Lean 4和Mathlib中进行形式化验证。目标是发现具有高问题品味的数学问题，即其证明能够重组研究领域语言并为人类数学研究提供持久帮助的问题。

Result: 对20个候选猜想进行实验，全部通过Lean解析和类型检查，全部未被exact?直接吸收，全部未被aesop自动消解，且无显式重复或近似重复，展示了从自然语言到形式化检查的稳定转换。

Conclusion: 该流水线能够系统性地生成和验证具有高数学潜力的猜想，为数学猜想发现提供了可行的方法论框架，有望辅助人类数学研究。

Abstract: Major mathematical conjectures still depend heavily on expert intuition, so a unified method for the systematic generation and validation of conjectures with substantial mathematical potential remains unavailable. We present a three stage pipeline for major conjecture discovery, with region search from explicit local evidence modules, reflective validation for foundationality, novelty, and potential significance, and formal validation in Lean 4 and Mathlib. The objective is the discovery of mathematical problems with high problem taste, namely problems whose proofs could reorganize the language of a research area and provide durable help to human mathematical research. Experiments on twenty candidates showstable passage from natural language to formal checks, with twenty out of twenty candidates passing Lean parsing and type checking, twenty out of twenty candidates not directly absorbed by exact?,twenty out of twenty candidates not automatically discharged by aesop, and no explicit duplicates or near duplicates.

</details>


### [94] [Multi-Agent Planning with Spatio-Temporal and Topological Constraints using STL-GO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28679)
*Sheryl Paul,Vidisha Kudalkar,Anand Balakrishnan,Lars Lindemann,Alberto Speranzon,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对多智能体路径规划问题，提出了两种编码方法（混合整数规划和可满足性模理论）来解决带有时空和拓扑约束的STL-GO规范，并通过多无人机搜救基准测试验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体规划问题在工程应用（如多机器人灭火、无人机巡检）中广泛存在，但现有的时空逻辑框架难以有效处理时空约束和拓扑约束（即智能体如何交互）的同时编码问题。特别是STL-GO中固有的图操作符需要编码多个潜在时变图，这使得问题求解极具挑战性。

Method: 提出了两种编码方法来解决STL-GO约束下的多智能体路径规划问题：1）基于混合整数规划（MIP）的编码方法；2）基于可满足性模理论（SMT）的编码方法。两种方法均具有正确性保证。同时提供了统一接口来指定智能体约束、图拓扑结构和STL-GO规范，支持两种方法的无缝使用和直接比较。

Result: 在多无人机搜救基准测试上对两种编码方法进行了评估，通过对团队规模和图复杂性的消融实验，展示了所提编码方法在动态多图交互场景下的表达能力和有效性。实验验证了两种方法都能有效处理STL-GO规范的约束求解问题。

Conclusion: 所提出的MIP和SMT两种编码方法都能有效解决带有时空和拓扑约束的多智能体路径规划问题，为STL-GO规范的实际应用提供了可行的解决方案。统一接口的设计使得两种方法可以方便地应用于不同场景，为动态多图交互环境下的多智能体系统规划提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Multi-agent planning problems arise in a variety of engineering applications, such as multi-robot wildfire fighting and unmanned aerial inspection in factories. A particular challenge is the existence of spatio-temporal (i.e., when and/or where an agent should do what) and topological constraints (i.e., how agents should interact), as typically formalized via the notion of graphs. Over the last years, various frameworks have been proposed that can capture such constraints via spatio-temporal logics. We focus here on spatio-temporal logic with graph operators (STL-GO), a recent formalism that supports reasoning about multiple agents and their topologies, such as sensing, communication, and task topologies. In this paper, we consider the problem of planning multi-agent paths that satisfy constraints written in STL-GO. This problem is particularly challenging due to the need of encoding multiple, potentially time-varying graphs via the graph operators inherent to STL-GO. We present two encodings of this problem, one based on mixed-integer programming (MIP) and another based on satisfiability modulo theory (SMT), with soundness guarantees. We provide a unified interface for specifying agent constraints, their graph topologies, and the STL-GO specification, enabling seamless use of both methods and facilitating direct comparison between them. We evaluate both encodings on a multi-UAV search-and-rescue benchmark, ablating over team size and graph complexity, highlighting the expressiveness of the proposed encodings under dynamic multi- graph interactions.

</details>


### [95] [ThinkReset: Learnable Intermediate Interface Construction for Bounded-Context Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28642)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ThinkReset方法，通过构建可复用的中间接口解决长链推理中的上下文溢出问题，在固定上下文窗口下显著提升长程推理成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链推理虽能提升复杂问题性能，但面临冗余累积、上下文溢出和错误锚定问题。在有界上下文窗口下，核心瓶颈并非轨迹压缩或测试时控制，而是缺乏可复用的中间接口来替代丢弃的历史并支持持续求解。此外，基于结果奖励的长链强化学习存在关键失败模式：当窗口即将耗尽而模型尚未完成任务时，最终答案奖励会鼓励过早猜测而非继续谨慎推理。

Method: 提出ThinkReset，一种文本空间实现方案：1）通过接口回写显式构建可复用的中间接口；2）通过重置机制替换丢弃的历史；3）直接优化重置后的继续成功率，而非依赖最终结果奖励。这种方法避免了窗口耗尽时的过早猜测问题。

Result: 在多个长程推理基准测试上，该方法在固定上下文窗口约束下持续提升了成功率。

Conclusion: ThinkReset通过显式构建可复用中间接口并优化重置后继续成功率，有效解决了长链推理中的上下文溢出和过早猜测问题，为有界上下文窗口下的长程推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought reasoning improves performance on complex problems, but it also introduces redundancy accumulation, context overflow, and error anchoring. We argue that under bounded context windows, the core bottleneck is not trajectory compression or test-time control, but the absence of a reusable intermediate interface that can replace discarded history and support continued solving. We further identify a key failure mode of outcome-reward-driven long-chain reinforcement learning: when the model has not solved the task before the window is nearly exhausted, the final-answer reward encourages premature guessing rather than continued careful reasoning. We propose ThinkReset, a text-space instantiation of this view. ThinkReset explicitly constructs reusable intermediate interfaces through interface writeback and reset, and directly optimizes post-reset continuation success. Across multiple long-horizon reasoning benchmarks, this perspective consistently improves success rates under fixed context windows.

</details>


### [96] [TAPR: Enhancing LLM Performance with a Task-Aware Prompt Rewriter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28657)
*Oliver Savolainen,Emanuele Bastianelli,Hosein Azarbonyad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了TAPR模型，通过强化学习将用户提示词改写为任务优化提示词，显著提升了下游LLM在问答、摘要和推理等任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）通常需要精心设计的提示词才能充分发挥潜力，这对于非专家用户而言构成了使用障碍。现有方法缺乏针对特定任务优化提示词的系统化解决方案。

Method: 提出Task-Aware Prompt Rewriter (TAPR)模型，基于Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)强化学习算法进行训练。奖励信号来源于LLM-as-judge对改写后提示词及相应任务输出的评估。以Phi-4-mini-instruct为基础模型进行微调。

Result: 在问答、摘要和算术推理等多样化任务上相比基线模型获得一致的性能提升。在Natural Questions和GSM8K等基准测试上准确率更高，生成的提示词包含更清晰、更具指导性的语言表达。

Conclusion: TAPR能够有效地将用户提示词改写为任务优化的提示词，帮助非专家用户更好地利用LLMs的能力，同时验证了强化学习结合LLM-as-judge在提示词优化任务上的有效性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often require carefully crafted prompts to unlock their full potential, which can be a barrier for non-expert users. This work addresses the challenge by introducing a Task-Aware Prompt Rewriter (TAPR), a model that reformulates user prompts into task-optimized prompts with the explicit goal of improving downstream LLM performance. We train TAPR using reinforcement learning with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), where rewards are derived from LLM-as-judge evaluations of both the reformulated prompt and the corresponding task output. Experimental results on diverse tasks, such as question answering, summarization, and arithmetic reasoning, show that our method yields consistent gains over base models in prompt rewriting ability. Fine-tuning Phi-4-mini-instruct (as the base model for TAPR) produces prompts that contain clearer and more instructive language, leading to higher accuracy on established benchmarks such as Natural Questions and GSM8K. Our code is available at: https://github.com/OliverSavolainen/task-specific-prompt-rewriter

</details>


### [97] [Beyond Component Testing: Validating Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29405)
*Fabio Orazio Mirto,Luca D'Agati,Giuseppe Tricomi,Stefano Silvestri,Francesco Longo,Antonio Puliafito,Giovanni Merlino*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本综述分析了257篇论文，提出五维分类法来表征代理AI系统的验证问题，发现行为评估相对成熟，但时序有效性、运行时证据维护、监管可读性和多智能体系统保障仍然不足，核心观点是代理AI的可信部署依赖于上下文轨迹验证而非孤立组件评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理AI系统通过多步轨迹行动，结合规划、工具使用、记忆、交互和适应。这种行为超出了组件测试和单次输入-输出评估的范围，因为可接受的系统行为现在取决于决策如何在时间和变化的环境条件下展开，传统验证实践已无法满足需求。

Method: 综合257篇跨领域论文（涵盖智能体评估、软件保障、信息物理系统、运行时监控和监管指导），提出五维分类法（行为、安全、时序、监管、多智能体关注点），并据此映射当前方法、揭示覆盖缺口。通过三个跨领域案例研究（医疗护理、工业运营、智能出行系统）说明五个维度在安全关键场景中的重现。

Result: 分析表明：行为评估相对成熟；时序有效性、运行时证据维护、监管可读性和开放式多智能体系统保障仍发展不足。文献中记录的失败模式为案例研究提供了基础。

Conclusion: 提出以生命周期为导向的研究议程，聚焦于有界自主性规范、对抗性轨迹生成、运行时监控和审计就绪证据结构。核心主张是代理AI的可信部署依赖于在上下文中验证轨迹，而非仅评估孤立组件。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems act through multi-step trajectories that combine planning, tool use, memory, interaction, and adaptation. This behavior stretches validation practice beyond component testing and one-shot input--output evaluation, because acceptable system behavior now depends on how decisions unfold over time and under changing environmental conditions. This survey synthesizes 257 papers spanning agent evaluation, software assurance, cyber-physical systems, runtime monitoring, and regulatory guidance in order to characterize the validation problem for agentic systems. The review is organized around a five-dimension taxonomy covering behavioral, safety, temporal, regulatory, and multi-agent concerns, and uses that taxonomy to map current approaches and expose recurrent coverage gaps. The analysis shows that behavioral evaluation is comparatively mature, while temporal validity, runtime evidence maintenance, regulatory legibility, and open-ended multi-agent systems assurance remain under-developed. Three cross-domain case studies (medical care, industrial operations, smart-mobility systems) provide operational illustrations of how the five taxonomy dimensions recur in safety-critical settings, grounded in the failure patterns documented in the reviewed literature. The paper concludes with a lifecycle-oriented research agenda centered on bounded-autonomy specifications, adversarial trajectory generation, runtime monitoring, and audit-ready evidence structures. The central claim is that trustworthy deployment of agentic AI depends on validating trajectories in context rather than assessing isolated components alone.

</details>


### [98] [Empowering Cross-Domain Sequential Recommendation with Hybrid Tokenization and Serial-Parallel Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28659)
*Yuxuan Hu,Yuhao Wang,Tianbo Huang,Chao Zhang,Ziwei Liu,Lihua Zhang,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GenCDSR框架，通过跨域混合分词机制和串并行解码策略，解决了现有生成式跨域序列推荐中跨域协作信息缺失和推理效率低的问题，在三个数据集上实现了1.5%的准确率提升和85.1%的推理延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐方法在处理跨域序列推荐时存在两个关键问题：(1)在分词过程中忽略了跨域协作关联性；(2)在生成阶段采用如束搜索等低效解码策略，阻碍了实时部署。这些局限性促使研究者开发更有效且高效的跨域生成式推荐框架。

Method: 提出GenCDSR框架，包含两个核心组件：(1)设计跨域混合分词机制，采用多塔架构和分层共享-特定及细粒度码本，联合捕获跨域共性和域特定差异；(2)开发跨域串并行解码策略，利用分层语义标识符结构实现部分并行化生成，在保持生成一致性的同时显著降低推理延迟。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上的实验表明，GenCDSR相比最先进基线方法平均准确率提升1.5%，平均推理延迟降低85.1%，证明了该方法的有效性和高效性。

Conclusion: GenCDSR成功解决了跨域序列推荐中跨域协作建模和实时推理的挑战，通过创新的分词机制和解码策略实现了准确性与效率的双重提升，为生成式推荐在实际场景中的部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Cross-domain sequential recommendation (CDSR) aims to model users' dynamic interest transitions and sequential patterns across multiple domains. Recently, generative recommendation (GR) has emerged. It first learns semantic identifiers (SIDs) from item semantics and formulates recommendation as autoregressive generation. However, existing methods face two critical issues: (1) they ignore collaborative correlations across domains during tokenization, and (2) they adopt inefficient decoding strategies, such as beam search, during generation, which hinders real-time deployment. To address these limitations, we propose GenCDSR, an effective and efficient generative framework for CDSR. Specifically, we design a cross-domain hybrid tokenization mechanism with a multi-tower architecture to jointly capture cross-domain commonalities and domain-specific distinctions through hierarchical shared-specific and fine-grained codebooks. Furthermore, we develop a cross-domain serial-parallel decoding strategy that leverages the hierarchical SID structure to partially parallelize generation, significantly reducing inference latency while preserving generation consistency. Experiments on three public datasets show that GenCDSR achieves an average accuracy improvement of 1.5 percent and an average inference latency reduction of 85.1 percent compared with state-of-the-art baselines. The implementation code and datasets are available online: https://github.com/Applied-Machine-Learning-Lab/RecSys2026_GenCDSR.

</details>


### [99] [An Ontology-Guided, Deduplication-Aware Extraction Layer for Knowledge Graph Construction from Heterogeneous Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28662)
*Vaibhav Dangaich,Kevin Lewis,Kundeshwar Pundalik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个生产级知识图谱抽取系统，通过本体引导的LLM抽取和多阶段精炼流水线，解决了实体关系抽取中的一致性问题，将搜索召回率从70%提升至95%，并修复了七类静默质量问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在从非结构化文档中抽取实体和关系时存在一致性问题：类型词汇表在不同文档间断裂、同一人物以多种名称变体出现、关系重复、以及同名不同个体面临静默混淆的风险，这些缺陷阻碍了知识图谱的构建质量。

Method: 系统采用两阶段抽取策略：首先使用本地部署的Qwen3.5-9B模型（基于本体调优）进行抽取，关键创新是本体引导抽取——通过嵌入相似度从图数据库实时检索相关本体切片并注入抽取提示词，相比静态域切片减少约94%的目录开销。随后执行五阶段精炼流水线：确定性清洗、跨块合并、关系二次抽取、六种无需模型推理的去重算法，以及具有冲突保护机制的嵌入解析子系统。

Result: 在情报语料库上的评估表明，搜索召回率从约70%提升至95%，无错误合并，并修复了七类静默质量缺陷，包括截断源文本的单字符bug和携带标题前缀实体的系统性重复问题。

Conclusion: 该生产抽取层成功将实时文档流转换为与形式本体对齐的验证知识图谱，证明了本体引导的LLM抽取结合多阶段精炼流水线能有效解决知识抽取中的一致性和质量问题，为实际应用提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: Large language models extract entities and relationships from unstructured documents fluently but inconsistently: type vocabularies fracture across documents, the same person surfaces under several name variants, relationships duplicate, and distinct individuals who share a name risk silent conflation. This paper presents the design, implementation, and empirical refinement of a production extraction layer that converts a live document stream into a validated knowledge graph aligned to a formal ontology. The system consumes document metadata from Kafka, routes PDF, spreadsheet, Office, and image content through handlers built for each format, and extracts entities and relationships in two passes using a locally hosted Qwen3.5-9B model tuned on the ontology. Its distinguishing component is ontology-guided extraction: the relevant slice of a curated ontology is retrieved live from a graph database by embedding similarity and injected into the extraction prompt, reducing catalog overhead by about 94 percent relative to static domain slices. Extracted results then pass through a refinement pipeline of five stages: deterministic cleaning, merging across chunks, a second pass for relationships, six deduplication algorithms that require no model inference, and an embedding resolution subsystem whose conflict guard no similarity score can override. Evaluation on intelligence corpora improved search recall from roughly 70 to 95 percent with no false merges, and corrected seven classes of silent quality defect, ranging from a bug that truncated source text by a single character to the systematic duplication of entities that carried title prefixes.

</details>


### [100] [How Hard Does It Think? Analyzing Step-Aware Reasoning Energy in LLM Chain-of-Thought Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28674)
*Hui Wei,Junda Wu,Sheldon Yu,Sizhe Zhou,Yizhu Jiao,Ming Zhong,Bowen Jin,Tong Yu,Shijia Pan,Jiawei Han,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了步骤感知推理能量(SARE)框架，通过CKA量化思维链各步骤的计算投入，发现推理能量分布高度不均且能预测错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可解释性方法依赖输出级信号或将处理深度压缩为单一轨迹级标量，导致思维链推理中各个步骤的计算投入分配情况不透明，无法理解步骤级别的计算努力。

Method: 提出步骤感知推理能量(SARE)几何框架，通过计算相邻transformer层之间token隐藏状态Gram矩阵的中心核对齐(CKA)来量化各个CoT步骤的努力程度，捕获token间关系结构；同时将CoT轨迹建模为潜在语义状态间的转移，将能量置于推理语义进程中进行分析。

Result: 在6个推理基准和3个开源LLM上的实验表明：推理能量在不同步骤类型间高度不均匀，呈现轨迹级指标不可见的类相变；错误轨迹在关键推理节点处系统性地显示较低能量；SARE特征在大多数设置中匹配或优于基于输出的置信度基线。

Conclusion: 内部几何动力学编码了超越表面信号的预测性信息，SARE框架成功揭示了思维链推理中步骤级别的计算分配规律，为理解和预测LLM推理过程提供了新的几何视角。

Abstract: Understanding how computational effort is allocated across individual chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning steps remains an open challenge: existing interpretability methods rely on output-level signals or collapse processing depth into a single trajectory-level scalar, leaving step-wise effort opaque. We propose Step-Aware Reasoning Energy (SARE), a geometric framework that quantifies effort at the granularity of individual CoT steps via Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) between Gram matrices of token hidden states across adjacent transformer layers, capturing inter-token relational structure without requiring eigenvector alignment or cluster correspondence. SARE further contextualizes this energy within reasoning's semantic progression by modeling CoT trajectories as transitions among latent semantic states. Across six reasoning benchmarks and three open-weight LLMs, we find that reasoning energy is highly non-uniform across step types, exhibiting phase-like transitions invisible to trajectory-level metrics; incorrect trajectories show systematically lower energy at critical reasoning junctions; and SARE-based features match or outperform output-based confidence baselines in most settings, indicating that internal geometric dynamics encode predictive information beyond surface-level signals.

</details>


### [101] [Reasoning in Real World Clinical Care: Why Large Language Models Are Not Yet Safe for Autonomous Clinical Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28677)
*Shayndhan Sivanathan,Shravan Nageswaran,Mehdi Zadem,Ryaan Sultan,Nicolas von Mallinckrodt,Max Solovyev,Alexey Matyushkin,Sumon Sadhu,Gabriele C DeLuca,Sanjeeva Jeyaretna,James Hillis,Manoj Ramachandran,Prakash Jayakumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM在医疗分诊应用中存在安全隐患，主要原因是模型缺乏在不确定性下收集信息的能力，容易遗漏罕见但致命的诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已通过医疗执照考试并在诊断推理中表现优异，推动了其在症状评估和临床决策支持中的应用。然而，在自主分诊（几乎没有临床医生参与）这一关键应用上，安全性的证据仍然缺失。作者旨在分析LLM在自主分诊任务中的核心缺陷和潜在风险。

Method: 通过概念性分析，指出LLM的核心问题：模型优化目标是生成最可能的文本，而非在安全答案为低概率但不可漏诊的诊断时采取安全行动。分析了安全分诊的本质特征——不对称成本下的序贯决策，其中单一灾难性漏诊的代价远超多次误报。

Result: 在病史不完整的情况下，LLM系统可能无法展现安全分诊所需的行为模式：扩大鉴别诊断范围、寻找缺失的红旗征、降低升级阈值、推迟判断直至获得充分信息、对高危害诊断未排除时提升关注级别。这些失败模式难以检测，因为现有评估多采用完整、精心策划、置信度把关的模拟场景。此外，LLM的助手式行为和正向偏差（轻信、顺从性、校准失误）可能放大这些风险。

Conclusion: LLM在医疗知识方面已具备能力，但在自主分诊这一高风险场景中存在根本性缺陷——不确定性下的信息收集能力不足。在缺乏临床分诊逻辑约束的情况下，应谨慎推进LLM的自主分诊应用，需要开发专门的安全评估框架。

Abstract: LLM now pass medical licensing examinations and, in curated cases, can rival physicians at diagnostic reasoning. These developments have accelerated the use of LLMs for symptom assessment and clinical decision support in diagnostic and treatment guidance, administrative documentation, and rules-based alert enhancement. This Perspective concerns the most consequential of these applications: the autonomous triage of self-presenting, undifferentiated patients, with little or no clinician in the loop. For that task, the evidence of safety does not yet exist. The gap is not in medical knowledge but in the fidelity of clinical evaluation: a model optimized to continue the most probable text is not optimized to act safely when the safe answer is the improbable must-not-miss diagnosis. Safe triage is not the selection of the most likely diagnosis; it is a sequential decision under asymmetric cost, in which the single catastrophic miss outweighs many false alarms, and the decisive signal may be one the patient has not volunteered - and that the model has not been trained to seek. The core deficit is therefore one of information gathering under uncertainty. Under incomplete histories, LLM systems may fail to show the behaviors safe triage requires: broadening the differential; seeking the missing red flag; lowering the threshold for escalation; deferring judgement until sufficient information is obtained; and escalating concern where high-harm diagnoses remain unexcluded. These modes of failure for LLMs can be difficult to detect considering that evaluations to date often use complete, well-curated, confidence-gated simulations. The application of LLMs under these conditions may be amplified by assistant-like behaviors and positive bias, including credulity, agreeableness, and miscalibration - when these are not constrained by clinical triage logic.

</details>


### [102] [ViSAGE: Constructing Self-Correcting Memories for Long-Form Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28678)
*Xinkui Zhao,Enbo Chen,Yifan Zhang,Chang Liu,Guanjie Cheng,Naibo Wang,Yueshen Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ViSAGE框架，通过跨模态绑定、双向记忆优化和多智能体交叉验证构建以实体为中心的多模态智能体记忆，解决实体混淆和幻觉问题，准确率提升5.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆方法在激进压缩和分段处理下丢失细粒度实体线索，且过度依赖向量相似性检索，导致检索到语义相关但身份不匹配的证据，引发实体混淆、错误传播和幻觉回答。

Method: ViSAGE框架包含三个核心组件：1) 通过跨模态绑定锚定实体身份，覆盖长时序范围；2) 应用双向记忆优化传播延迟的身份证据，回溯统一历史记录以改善未来推理；3) 引入多智能体交叉验证，在身份-证据对齐约束下评估检索证据，支持在证据缺失时拒绝回答而非给出无依据答案。

Result: 在广泛实验中，ViSAGE持续超越最强基线，准确率提升5.9%。

Conclusion: ViSAGE有效解决了多模态智能体记忆中的实体一致性问题，通过自纠正、以实体为中心的记忆构建机制显著提升了长时序环境下的推理性能。

Abstract: Multimodal agents operating in long-horizon environments must build and continually update multimedia memories to support entity-consistent, temporally grounded reasoning. However, existing agentic memory approaches often discard fine-grained dentity cues under aggressive compression and segment-wise processing. They also rely heavily on vector similarity retrieval, which can surface semantically related yet identity-mismatched evidence, leading to entity confusion, error propagation, and hallucinated answers.
  We propose ViSAGE, a multimodal agentic memory framework that constructs self-correcting, entity-centric memories. Specifically, ViSAGE anchors entity identity via cross-modal binding over long temporal ranges. It then applies bidirectional memory refinement to propagate delayed identity evidence, retroactively unifying historical records and improving future reasoning. We also introduce multi-agent cross-verification to assess retrieved evidence under an identity-evidence alignment onstraint, enabling abstention instead of unsupported answers when evidence is missing. Extensive results demonstrate that ViSAGE consistently outperforms the strongest baseline, achieving 5.9% higher accuracy.

</details>


### [103] [Library Reachability in LSR-Synth: How Anti-Memorization Design Changes the Measurement of Symbolic Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28684)
*Zhan'ao Yao,Liang Yin,Zhihao Gao,Boxuan Zhang,Xiaoyu Wu,Linjing Li,Rongyan Wang,Tingwei Chen,Youwei Wang,Xiaolin Zhao,Jiahui Shi,Jianjun Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究评估LSR-Synth基准测试中语言模型先验知识的贡献，发现固定词汇表已覆盖大部分任务，语言模型候选仅在词汇覆盖被破坏时有显著边际贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学方程发现基准使用公开领域的已知方程，难以判断模型是真正从数据发现规律，还是仅从训练语料中回忆答案。虽然LSR-Synth通过引入新颖合成术语缓解此问题，但需要进一步验证这些任务能否区分语言模型提供的科学先验知识与不访问任务语义的传统算子搜索方法。

Method: 构建一个不使用语义信息的基线方法，采用具有公开文档来源的固定词汇表；通过语义遮蔽、库弱化和匹配算子族敲除来评估候选覆盖率的作用；在严格的分布外评估设置下比较不同方法的性能。

Result: 在当前任务快照、搜索预算和评分协议下，固定词汇表已覆盖大部分任务，语言模型生成的候选很少扩展可求解实例集合；只有当词汇覆盖率被选择性破坏时，语言模型的边际贡献才变得显著；严格的分布外评估降低所有方法的绝对成功率，但不改变这一关系。

Conclusion: 研究结果既不否定LSR-Synth对完整公式记忆的控制，也不意味着语言模型先验知识普遍无帮助。结论更有限：当前大部分任务仍适合评估先前未见表达式的拟合和重组，但仅凭这些任务不足以识别超出固定搜索空间的先验知识贡献。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks for scientific equation discovery are largely composed of well-known equations available in the public domain, making it difficult to determine whether a model is discovering laws from data or merely recalling answers from its training corpus. LSR-Synth mitigates this problem by introducing novel synthetic terms into established scientific mechanisms and filtering the resulting tasks for novelty, solvability, and scientific plausibility. This paper examines a narrower measurement question: can these tasks further distinguish scientific priors supplied by language models from conventional operator search that does not access task semantics? We construct a semantics-free baseline using a fixed vocabulary with publicly documented provenance, and assess the role of candidate coverage through semantic blinding, library weakening, and matched operator-family knockouts. Under the current task snapshot, search budget, and scoring protocol, the fixed vocabulary already covers most tasks, while language-model-generated candidates rarely expand the set of solvable instances. Their marginal contribution becomes substantial only when vocabulary coverage is selectively disrupted. Strict out-of-distribution evaluation lowers the absolute success rates of all methods but does not alter this relationship. These findings neither invalidate LSR-Synth's controls against memorization of complete formulas nor imply that language-model priors are generally unhelpful. Rather, they support a more limited conclusion: most current tasks remain suitable for evaluating the fitting and recombination of previously unseen expressions, but are insufficient on their own to identify contributions from priors beyond a fixed search space.

</details>


### [104] [Safety, or Just Capability? A Validity Audit of Agent-Safety Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28685)
*Youting Wang,Xiao Han,Dingyan Shang,Yuan Tang,Bowen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统验证了四个AI代理安全基准测试的有效性，发现存在严重的测量问题：基准测试之间排序不一致、样本量依赖性、能力与安全性负相关，呼吁对安全声明建立最低标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI代理安全评估存在基准测试分数被互换引用的问题，缺乏对这些基准测试有效性的系统性验证。作者指出这些基准测试测量不同行为，却被统一视为"代理安全性"，这种做法的科学基础未经充分检验。

Method: 将四个主流安全基准（R-Judge、InjecAgent、AgentHarm、AgentDojo）作为待验证的测量工具，使用官方实现和作者提供的评分器在最多22个模型上测试；同时使用MMLU和GPQA作为能力组合指标；通过相关性分析、留一验证、bootstrap分析等方法评估基准测试的有效性。

Result: 发现三个关键问题：（1）基准测试排序不一致：R-Judge特异性与AgentHarm安全性在小样本（n=7）时相关-0.64，大样本时几乎为零；（2）"总是肯定"策略在R-Judge上达到F1=0.690，超过5个真实模型；（3）能力与误对齐安全性负相关（ρ=-0.44），在配对面板上差异显著（Δ=-1.00，p<0.001）；AgentHarm与越狱安全性在控制能力后相关+0.72，但这反映的是收敛效度而非通用安全性。

Conclusion: 当前安全基准测试存在严重的有效性问题，包括排序不一致、样本量依赖性、能力-安全性负相关等。安全声明必须至少包括：基准测试名称、度量指标、目标行为和模型面板，这是最低标准。研究结果质疑了现有安全评估方法的可靠性，强调需要更严格的验证程序。

Abstract: Agent-safety benchmarks measure different behaviors, and their scores get quoted interchangeably as an agent's safety. We treat four of them (R-Judge, InjecAgent, AgentHarm, AgentDojo) as measurements to be validated, running each under its official implementation and author-provided scorer on up to 22 models, with MMLU and GPQA measured by us under one protocol as a capability composite. The metric is the first problem.
