On any binary trace-judgment benchmark scored by $F_1$, an ``always positive'' policy attains $F_1 = 2π/(1+π)$; on R-Judge that is $0.690$, above five of the 21 models that actually discriminate. The three broad-coverage benchmarks then rank the same 18 models differently, and the trade-off behind that disagreement is a small-panel artifact: R-Judge specificity against AgentHarm safety correlates $-0.64$ at $n{=}7$ and $+0.02$ at $n{=}18$, and a quarter of random size-7 subsets reach $|ρ| \geq 0.5$ around that near-zero value. Held-out validity turns on which outcome you pick. Capability predicts task success ($ρ{=}{+}0.60$) but correlates negatively with misalignment safety ($ρ{=}{-}0.44$, $n{=}21$). On their paired $n{=}20$ panel, the corresponding contrast is $Δ{=}{-}1.00$ (95% CI $[-1.48, -0.49]$, $p<0.001$), and it survives leave-one-organization-out and organization-clustered bootstrap analyses. On an expanded 41-model panel, the misalignment correlation weakens to $-0.16$ (95% CI $[-0.54, +0.22]$) and jailbreak strengthens to $+0.34$, though neither change is significant. \mbox{AgentHarm} shows the strongest held-out association, $ρ{=}{+}0.72$ with three-template jailbreak safety after controlling capability. But both instruments score harmful compliance, so this is evidence of convergent validity rather than general safety. Naming the benchmark, metric, target behavior, and model panel is the minimum a safety claim needs.

</details>


### [105] [SciToolAgent-Evo: An Ontology-Aware Self-Evolving Agent for Open-World Scientific Tool Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28692)
*Yuqi Tang,Chenyi Zhou,Libin Wang,Keyan Ding,Qiang Zhang,Huajun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了SciToolAgent-Evo，一个面向开放世界科学工具获取的本体感知自进化智能体，通过演化记忆、对比轨迹学习和LinUCB策略动态平衡探索与利用，在OpenSciToolBench基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体依赖静态语义的预定义工具空间，无法适应开放世界科学研究中工具需求、能力和边界动态演化的特性，限制了其在动态科研工作流中的应用。

Method: 提出SciToolAgent-Evo框架：(1)构建包含技能、经验和本体化工具图的演化记忆驱动系统；(2)积累阶段从对比轨迹中蒸馏可泛化知识；(3)推理阶段制定主动请求，采用基于LinUCB的多臂赌博机门控机制动态平衡探索与利用；(4)在线完成新工具的科学本体以无缝集成到已知图谱；(5)构建包含900个跨四个难度等级真实任务的OpenSciToolBench基准。

Result: 在OpenSciToolBench基准上的广泛评估表明，SciToolAgent-Evo达到了最先进（SOTA）性能，验证了其在开放世界科学工具获取任务中的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: SciToolAgent-Evo成功实现了科学工具的动态获取与集成，突破了静态工具空间的限制，为开放世界科学研究提供了更灵活、更通用的智能体解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have been increasingly adopted in scientific research for organizing and invoking specialized computational tools. However, their reliance on predefined tool spaces with static semantics limits their applicability to open-world scientific workflows, where tool requirements, capabilities, and boundaries evolve dynamically. To this end, we propose SciToolAgent-Evo, an ontology-aware self-evolving agent for open-world scientific tool acquisition. Driven by an evolving memory of skills, experiences, and an ontologized tool graph, it distills generalizable knowledge from contrastive trajectories during accumulation, whereas during inference, it formulates active requests and utilizes a LinUCB-based bandit gate to dynamically balance exploration and exploitation. Once a novel tool is acquired, its scientific ontology is completed online for seamless integration into the known graph. Moreover, we introduce OpenSciToolBench, a benchmark containing 900 realistic tasks across four difficulty levels. Extensive evaluations show that SciToolAgent-Evo achieves state-of-the-art performance, validating its robustness and generalization.

</details>


### [106] [EarlyDx: An Admission-Anchored Benchmark for Open-Ended Generation of Evidence-Supported ED-Encounter Diagnoses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28788)
*Jiahui Li,Ruili Fang,Zishuai Liu,Yutong Guo,Nan Yang,Wenzhan Song,Jin Lu,Fei Dou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了EarlyDx，一个基于154,834次急诊科就诊的大规模早期诊断基准，专注于入院时的开放性诊断预测。研究发现现有系统（包括前沿通用、医疗专业和领域后训练模型）在语义LLM评估下均无法可靠地综合入院证据，零样本模型主要依赖提取而非推理，即使后训练后仍有显著性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医院入院时的临床诊断必须基于有限、不完整的证据快速做出，但现有诊断预测基准存在明显局限性：限制预测于封闭编码集、排除自由文本笔记，并使用包含完整住院过程的出院诊断作为监督，这些都不适合早期诊断场景。因此需要一个新的基准来真正评估和推动早期诊断技术的发展。

Method: 研究构建了EarlyDx基准，基于MIMIC-IV中的154,834次急诊科就诊数据。关键技术方法包括：1）将每次就诊限制为入院时间$t_0$可获得的记录；2）使用急诊科就诊期间记录的诊断而非出院诊断作为监督；3）引入LLM审核员验证每个自由文本标签是否被证据支持（完全支持、部分支持或无支持）；4）采用语义LLM-as-judge评估协议，主要评估仅完全支持的标签。

Result: 在语义LLM-as-judge评估协议下，所有评估系统（前沿通用、医疗专业化或领域后训练模型）都无法可靠地综合入院时间证据。零样本模型主要通过提取评分，只能恢复3-31%需要推理而非从记录中读取的诊断；后训练将推理依赖性召回率提高到56%，但仍存在显著差距；在时间关键条件下，没有任何系统达到临床医生的敏感性和精确性平衡。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了当前AI诊断系统在早期诊断场景中的重大局限性，特别是在需要推理而非简单提取的情况下。即使经过领域特定的后训练，系统性能仍有显著提升空间。研究发布了完整的构建和评估流程，为未来改进早期诊断AI系统提供了重要基准和研究基础。

Abstract: Clinical diagnosis at hospital admission must be made rapidly from limited, incomplete evidence. Existing diagnosis-prediction benchmarks are poorly suited to this setting: they restrict prediction to closed code sets, exclude free-text notes, and supervise with discharge diagnoses that incorporate the full inpatient course. We introduce EarlyDx, a large-scale benchmark for open-ended early diagnosis, built from 154,834 emergency department encounters in MIMIC-IV. Each encounter is restricted to records available at admission time $t_0$ and supervised by the diagnoses recorded during the ED encounter rather than at discharge. An LLM auditor further verifies every free-text label as supported, partially supported, or unsupported by that evidence; the primary evaluation scores only fully supported labels. Under a semantic LLM-as-judge protocol, no evaluated system --- frontier general, medical-specialized, or in-domain post-trained --- synthesizes admission-time evidence reliably. Zero-shot models score largely by extraction, recovering only 3-31% of diagnoses that must be inferred rather than read from the record; post-training raises inference-dependent recall to 56%, but a sizeable margin remains, and on time-critical conditions no system attains a clinician's balance of sensitivity and precision. We release the full construction and evaluation pipeline at here.

</details>


### [107] [Model or Harness? An Interaction-Centric Taxonomy for Localizing Agent Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28802)
*Harsh Raj,Vipul Gupta,Anas Mahmoud,Razvan-Gabriel Dumitru,Darvin Yi,Aakash Sabharwal,Yunzhong He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种以交互为中心的智能体故障分类体系，将41种故障模式定位到组件间的交互边并标注故障侧，解决了现有评估将故障归结为系统级结果而导致的修复分配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估将智能体故障简化为系统级结果，掩盖了故障源头，导致相同的表面故障可能需要完全不同的干预措施（模型后训练、工具链工程、环境重设计或基准修复），而大多数故障分类体系因缺乏共享结构而无法解决这一修复分配问题。

Method: 构建交互式分类体系，将41种故障模式按"组件间交互边+故障侧"进行组织，每种故障被分配到两个组件之间的边并标注故障归属侧（模型侧、工具链侧、环境侧或评分器侧），从而指导具体干预措施。

Result: 基于公开基准、模型系统卡、已发表报告和智能体轨迹日志进行实例验证，使用独立推理智能体作为评判者评估可复现性，最强评判者在四类前沿模型上对人类类别标签达到Cohen's κ=0.76，表明分类体系捕获了共享结构而非标注者偏好。

Conclusion: 该分类体系具有跨架构可操作性，能够准确定位故障源并指导针对性修复，为智能体系统改进提供了系统化的诊断框架。

Abstract: Existing evaluations often reduce agent failures to system-level outcomes, obscuring where the fault originated and which intervention would improve the agent system. This creates a repair-assignment problem: the same visible failure may call for model post-training, harness engineering, environment redesign, or benchmark repair depending on its source. Because agent behavior emerges from interactions among models, harnesses, users, tools, memory, and environments, outcome-level labels are often insufficient for improvement. Most failure taxonomies do little to resolve this problem because they are benchmark-specific and lack a shared structure. We introduce an interaction-centric taxonomy that localizes failures to the interactions in which they originate and identifies the responsible component. It organizes 41 failure modes by assigning each to an edge between two components and a fault side indicating where the repair belongs. This makes the taxonomy actionable: model-side failures identify targets for post-training, harness-side failures point to scaffolding and tool-integration fixes, and environment or grader failures reveal evaluation conditions requiring redesign. The schema applies across agent architectures, from coding assistants to long-horizon personal assistants and multi-agent systems. We ground the taxonomy in worked examples from public benchmarks, model system cards, published reports, and logged agent trajectories, and evaluate its reproducibility using independent reasoning agents as judges. Across four frontier models, the strongest judge reaches Cohen's $κ=0.76$ against human category labels, suggesting that the categories capture shared structure rather than annotator-specific preferences.

</details>


### [108] [Best Friends, Not Forever: Evaluating Long-Horizon Persona Collapse and Behavioral Drift in AI Companions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28818)
*Pranav Narayanan Venkit,Akshara Prabhakar,Yu Li,Daniel Lee,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI伴侣系统中的长期失效问题（人格崩溃和行为漂移），提出了ANCHOR审计框架来评估人格扮演和轨迹回忆能力，发现现有模型在长期交互连续性方面存在严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI伴侣越来越多地中介重复社交互动，用户可能依赖稳定的角色和共享历史，但本地可接受的回复并不能确保这些特性得以保持。因此需要研究长期交互中的失效问题，即人格崩溃（部署角色、边界、价值观或风格的丧失）和行为漂移（这些属性的逐渐或反复侵蚀）。

Method: 提出ANCHOR控制合成审计方法，分别测量人格扮演和轨迹回忆。研究包含2,008个对话，涵盖27个角色、9种交互时间表、3种生成记忆设置和4个评估模型。使用身份探针（结合密封的102项问卷和轮次级判断）和轨迹探针（对35个对话库中的110个校准反事实问题进行评分）。

Result: 没有评估模型和配置能可靠地保持这两个维度：轨迹准确率平均仅为44.4%，用户状态回忆接近四选项随机水平，测试的上下文条件或记忆都不能一致地解决这些失效。问卷保持程度也因模型和角色侧面而异，与轮次级行为不一致，且对评估者选择敏感。

Conclusion: 当前系统尚不能可靠支持长期伴侣连续性，审计必须区分人格扮演、轨迹回忆、评估者来源和部署上下文，而不是将它们合并为单一的信任或稳定性分数。

Abstract: As AI companions increasingly mediate repeated social interaction, users may rely on a stable role and shared history, yet locally acceptable replies do not ensure that either persists. We study two observable long-horizon failures: 'persona collapse', the loss of a deployed role, boundaries, values, or style, and 'behavioral drift', the gradual or recurrent erosion of those properties. We introduce ANCHOR, a controlled synthetic audit that separately measures persona enactment and trajectory recall. The study contains 2,008 conversations spanning 27 personas, nine interaction schedules, three generated memory settings, and four evaluated models. The Identity Probe combines a sealed 102-item questionnaire with turn-level judgments, while the Trajectory Probe scores 110 calibrated counterfactual questions from 35 conversation banks. Our results show that no evaluated model and configuration reliably preserves either dimensions: trajectory accuracy averages only 44.4%, user-state recall remains near four-option chance, and no tested context condition or memory consistently resolves these failures. Questionnaire retention also varies by model and persona facet, disagrees with turn-level behavior, and is sensitive to evaluator choice. These results indicate that current systems do not yet reliably support long-horizon companion continuity and that audits must distinguish persona enactment, trajectory recall, evaluator provenance, and deployment context rather than collapse them into a single trust or stability score.

</details>


### [109] [Fragility of Value under Imperfect Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28881)
*Winter Cross*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过建模分析发现，在确保代理满足代理条件的情况下，当人类价值函数和代理条件满足特定条件时，部署的代理可能导致灾难性结果（期望人类价值低于η），揭示了过度优化的风险，建议采用限制优化压力的AI设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统承担更多责任，确保其与人类价值对齐变得至关重要。AI安全领域的一个普遍担忧是"人类价值是脆弱的"——即对人类价值的不完美代理进行过度优化可能导致灾难性后果。需要理解在什么条件下，即使经过理想化的对齐训练，代理仍可能产生灾难性结果。

Method: 建立一个对齐问题的形式化模型，其中代理首先经历理想化的对齐训练以确保其价值函数满足代理条件，然后再对世界进行优化。通过数学分析，识别导致代理价值函数具有η-灾难性（即在极限优化下，人类价值的期望值低于η）的条件，具体研究人类价值函数的性质和多种代理条件的准确性。

Result: 确定了导致部署具有η-灾难性价值函数代理的具体条件：当人类价值函数具有特定性质，且代理条件的准确性满足某些条件时，即使代理已经过对齐训练，仍可能产生灾难性结果。这证明了过度优化的危险性和单纯依赖部署前训练的局限性。

Conclusion: 过度优化AI系统可能导致灾难性结果，即使经过理想化的对齐训练也不足以保证安全性。研究结果表明应当采用限制优化压力的AI设计方法（如quantilizers），而非仅仅依赖部署前的训练来确保系统安全。

Abstract: As more responsibility is placed upon AI systems, it becomes increasingly important to guarantee that these systems are aligned with humanity. A common fear in AI safety is that human value is fragile -- that is, optimizing too heavily for an imperfect proxy to human values will lead to a catastrophic outcome. In this paper, we present a model of the alignment problem where an agent undergoes idealized alignment training that guarantees its value function satisfies a proxy condition before optimizing the world. Our primary results identify conditions on the human value function and the accuracy of several proxy conditions under which an agent with an $η$-catastrophic value function, one that is guaranteed to take the expectation of human value below $η$ in the limit of optimizing power, would be deployed. Our results highlight the danger of overoptimization and motivate AI designs that limit optimization pressure, such as quantilizers, rather than relying solely on pre-deployment training.

</details>


### [110] [Identifying Informative Environments for Cognition Parameter Inference via Bayesian Experimental Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28894)
*Manisha Dubey,Rimvydas Rubavicius,N. Siddharth,Subramanian Ramamoorthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于贝叶斯实验设计（BED）的认知实验环境优化框架，通过将实验环境作为设计变量，解决了如何设计最具信息量的认知实验这一关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的贝叶斯逆向规划方法在认知参数推断中取得了一定成功，但该方法严重依赖实验环境的选择。现有研究通常将实验环境视为固定不变，未能回答哪些认知实验设计最能提供信息量用于认知参数推断这一核心问题。

Method: 将认知规划实验的设计形式化为贝叶斯实验设计（BED）问题，将实验环境作为设计变量。建立了精确的蒙特卡洛BED基准，并引入了摊销贝叶斯实验设计框架以实现高效的后验推断和设计评估。在Mouselab-MDP过程追踪范式中进行了实验验证。

Result: 摊销BED方法在环境排名上与精确蒙特卡洛BED高度一致，同时大幅降低了计算成本。研究发现不存在单一最优环境能够在所有认知推断目标上表现最优，揭示了期望信息增益、后验可恢复性和信息效率之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 本研究为贝叶斯参数推断提供了设计信息性认知实验的原则性框架，为认知科学实验设计提供了理论指导和实用工具。

Abstract: Computational cognitive modeling seeks to infer latent cognitive mechanisms underlying observed behavior. Bayesian inverse planning provides a principled framework for such inference, but its success depends critically on the experimental environment. Existing approaches typically treat environments as fixed, leaving open the question of which cognitive experiments are most informative for cognition parameter inference. We formulate the design of cognitive planning experiments as a Bayesian Experimental Design (BED) problem, treating the experimental environment as the design variable. We establish an exact Monte Carlo BED benchmark and introduce an amortized Bayesian experimental design framework for efficient posterior inference and design evaluation. Experiments on the Mouselab-MDP process-tracing paradigm show that amortized BED closely matches the environment rankings of exact Monte Carlo BED while substantially reducing computational cost. We further show that no single environment is uniformly optimal across cognitive inference objectives, revealing trade-offs between expected information gain, posterior recoverability, and information efficiency. These results provide a principled framework for designing informative cognitive experiments for Bayesian parameter inference.

</details>


### [111] [NeSyFS: A Neuro-symbolic Fast-Slow Thinking Framework for LLM Agent under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28942)
*Duo Xu,Faramarz Fekri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了NeSyFS框架，通过知识图谱表示信念状态，结合快慢思维模块解决LLM智能体在部分可观测环境下的决策挑战，在多个基准测试中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在部分可观测环境下面临信念状态推断、任务目标错位和不确定性规划等关键挑战，而先前方法通常基于完整或摘要化的动作-观察历史进行决策，其中冗余和无关信息会误导决策过程。

Method: 提出神经符号快慢思维(NeSyFS)框架：(1)使用知识图谱(KG)表示信念状态，为每个模块提供三元组上下文；(2)快思维模块执行反应式动作；(3)慢思维模块基于扭曲序贯蒙特卡洛(TSMC)算法进行不确定性感知规划；(4)反思模块反思快思维动作，并在反应式动作反复失败时切换至慢思维模块。

Result: 在三个代表性基准测试(ALFWorld、Webshop和ScienceWorld)上进行了实验验证，相比先前方法展现出显著优势。

Conclusion: NeSyFS框架通过知识图谱表示和快慢思维协同机制，在统一框架内有效解决了部分可观测性带来的挑战，为LLM智能体在复杂环境中的决策提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Recently Large Language Models (LLMs) have been increasingly deployed as autonomous agents in applications such as self-reflection, retrieval-augmented generation, and scientific discovery. In these settings, agents must act based on limited observations rather than full environmental states, leading to partial observability. This introduces several key challenges: belief state inference, task objective misalignment, and planning under uncertainty. Prior approaches typically condition actions on full or summarized action-observation histories whose redundant and irrelevant information can mislead the decision making of LLM agent. Inspired by human cognition, we propose a novel neuro-symbolic fast-slow thinking (NeSyFS) framework for LLM agent, addressing the challenges introduced by partial observability in a unified approach. We use a knowledge graph (KG) to represent the belief state, providing triplets as context for every module of NeSyFS. The fast-thinking module performs reactive action, while slow-thinking conducts a new uncertainty-aware planning by following the high-level structure of twisted sequential Monte Carlo (TSMC) algorithm. To mitigate the misalignment of task objective, a reflection module is used to reflect fast-thinking actions, and also switches to the slow-thinking module whenever reactive actions repeatedly fail. Experiments on three representative benchmarks, i.e. ALFWorld, Webshop, and ScienceWorld, demonstrate significant advantages over previous methods.

</details>


### [112] [MerchantBench: Benchmarking LLM Agents for Long-Term Coherence in E-Commerce Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28956)
*Qiming Shi,Yulong Tao,Linbo Jin,Zhaolu Kang,Yibo Dou,Jiawen Zhu,Tianjun Pan,Shaokang Fu,Chengyu Wang,Siyue Li,Yaping Cheng,Di Weng,Chengfu Huo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MerchantBench基准，用于评估大语言模型代理在长期任务中的连贯性能力。通过365天的电商模拟实验，发现即使是最新的大语言模型，其表现也远低于人类参与者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型评估基准主要关注有明确成功标准的短期任务，而实际部署往往需要代理在长时间跨度内保持目的性行为（长期连贯性），并能根据累积证据调整决策。因此需要一个新的评估环境来测试这种能力。

Method: 构建了MerchantBench基准，这是一个基于98,843条真实电商产品记录的365天订单级模拟环境，配备了26个交互工具。该环境涵盖产品采购、上架定价、现金流管理和混合延迟反馈适应等环节，将快速可观察的上游供应商事件与延迟的下游订单结果相结合，要求代理跟踪单个订单生命周期并重新审视早期决策。在两种代理框架下对8个大语言模型进行了48次实验运行。

Result: 实验结果显示，即使是最新的LLM与人类参与者之间仍存在显著差距。表现最好的LLM配置最终净资产均值仅为人类参与者的27.3%。

Conclusion: MerchantBench揭示了当前大语言模型在需要长期连贯性的复杂实际任务中的局限性，为未来改进代理的长期决策能力提供了重要的评估基准和实证依据。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly evaluated as autonomous tool users, yet most benchmarks focus on bounded tasks with immediate success criteria. Real-world deployments often require Long-Term Coherence, the capacity to preserve purposeful behavior across extended horizons while adapting decisions to accumulated evidence. Evaluating this capacity requires a persistent environment in which actions constrain future choices, feedback arrives at heterogeneous delays, and incoherent behavior produces measurable cumulative effects. Seller-side e-commerce provides a suitable setting for this evaluation through recurrent and interdependent decisions over Product Sourcing, Listing and Pricing Control, Cash-Flow Management, and Mixed-Latency Feedback Adaptation. We introduce MerchantBench, a 365-day order-level simulation grounded in 98,843 real e-commerce product records and equipped with 26 tools for agent interaction. MerchantBench couples promptly observable Upstream Supplier Events with delayed Downstream Order Outcomes, requiring agents to follow individual order lifecycles and revisit earlier decisions. We evaluate eight LLMs under two agent frameworks in 48 runs, each spanning 365 simulated days. Our results reveal a substantial gap between even the latest LLMs and human participants, with the best LLM configuration attaining only 27.3\% of the mean final net assets achieved by human participants.

</details>


### [113] [Scaling Scientific Discovery Environments for Turn-Level Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28990)
*Yucheng Xu,Keyi Zhang,Yuyang Yu,Min Zhang,Shiyuan Meng,Pei Chu,Zhongying Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SciDisco框架，用于训练科学发现智能体，在假设驱动的科学数据分析基准测试中达到了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体在数据驱动的科学发现任务中展现出潜力，但长期科学分析仍受限于缺乏针对真实科学数据的过程监督环境。

Method: 提出SciDisco框架，包含SciThèque编译环境（整合假设、数据集、隐藏证据图和验证器）和DAG导向的轨迹合成方法，以及DiscoPO算法来分配产生可验证分析证据的动作的轮次级别信用。

Result: SciDisco-14B模型在假设驱动的科学数据分析基准测试中达到了最先进（state-of-the-art）性能。

Conclusion: SciDisco框架成功解决了科学发现智能体训练中过程监督环境缺乏的问题，证明了过程可验证环境对训练科学分析智能体的重要性。

Abstract: Large language model agents have shown promising capabilities in data-driven scientific discovery tasks, where an agent interacts with an execution environment and produces a statistical claim. Long-horizon scientific analysis remains constrained by the lack of process supervised environments over real-world scientific data. This paper introduces SciDisco, a scalable framework for training Scientific Discovery agents in process-verifiable environments. SciThèque compiles hypotheses, datasets, hidden evidence graphs, and verifiers into task environments where analytical progress can be checked during interaction. DAG-grounded trajectory synthesis uses these environments to construct verifier-filtered multi-turn demonstrations. DiscoPO then uses the environment as the source of training signal, assigning turn-level credit to actions that produce verifiable analytical evidence. Experiments show that SciDisco-14B reaches state-of-the-art on hypothesis-driven scientific data analysis benchmarks.

</details>


### [114] [MMShopBench: A Real-Log Benchmark for Multimodal, Multi-Turn Shopping Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29002)
*Zeying Hao,Hao Guo,Mengtao Xu,Yimin Hu,Yuheng Song,Zesheng Zhou,Jinsong Lan,Xiaoyong Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MMShopBench，首个基于真实日志的多模态、多轮购物智能体基准测试，用于评估和改进AI购物助手在理解复杂购物需求方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线购物者越来越多地使用AI购物助手，通过图像和多轮对话来表达难以仅用文字描述的产品需求。然而现有基准主要依赖纯文本或合成请求，未能充分代表通过图像和语言共同表达的复杂现实购物需求。

Method: 构建了MMShopBench基准，该基准基于经过清洗和人工标注的真实购物日志，提供购买意图和强制产品要求的真实标注。智能体需要从用户图像和多轮对话中联合推断需求，通过图像和文本搜索检索候选产品，并验证每个候选产品是否满足所有要求。研究者使用基于证据的多模态协议评估了代表性开源和专有模型，构建了配套训练集来微调开源模型，并建立了离线购物沙盒以确保可重复实验。

Result: 微调显著缩小了开源模型与领先专有模型之间的性能差距，证明了训练数据的有效性。

Conclusion: MMShopBench为多模态、多轮购物智能体提供了首个基于真实日志的基准测试，能够有效评估和改进AI购物助手的能力，微调后的开源模型表现接近专有模型。

Abstract: Online shoppers increasingly turn to AI shopping assistants, using images and multi-turn dialogue to express and refine product needs that are difficult to articulate in text alone. However, existing benchmarks largely rely on text-only or synthetic requests, underrepresenting complex real-world shopping requirements jointly expressed through images and language. We introduce MMShopBench, the first real-log benchmark for multimodal, multi-turn shopping agents. Built from carefully cleaned and manually annotated shopping logs, MMShopBench provides ground-truth annotations of each request's purchase intent and mandatory product requirements. Agents must infer these requirements jointly from user images and multi-turn dialogue, retrieve candidate products through image and text search, and verify that each candidate satisfies all requirements using its product images and structured attributes. We evaluate representative open-source and proprietary models using an evidence-grounded multimodal protocol and construct a companion training set for fine-tuning an open-source model. To ensure reproducible experimentation, we build an offline shopping sandbox, where fine-tuning substantially narrows the performance gap between our open-source model and leading proprietary models, demonstrating the effectiveness of our training data.

</details>


### [115] [Evidence-Grounded Constraint Checking in Construction Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29058)
*Rashid Mushkani,Hugo Berard,Shin Koseki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个专业文档审查的证据支撑流程，通过重新分配图像预算，在重复测试中将决策准确率提高了10.6个百分点，但在更广泛的测试中效果未能持续，揭示了分辨率与广度之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业文档审查是一个约束检查问题，其决策依赖于文本、几何、页面和文档修订之间的关系。现有方法在处理这些复杂关系时存在准确性不足和假阳性等问题，需要一种能够有效分配证据、提高决策准确性的方法。

Method: 提出了一个证据支撑的流水线，包括：规范化提取的事实、确定性执行四状态规则、保留源跨度以及升级未解决案例。在160个来自29个建筑项目的参考任务上评估了PDF证据分配器，使用重复四系统测试和不相交二系统广度扩展进行验证。

Result: 在重复测试中，将四图像预算从检索页面概览重新分配为一个概览和三个重叠瓦片，将项目族标准化决策准确率提高了10.6个百分点（95%项目聚类自举CI：4.3到18.0；精确p=0.031）。然而，在更广泛的区块中这种效果未持续：Region-RAG准确率变化为-4.1点（95% CI：-10.2到1.9；精确p=0.209）。精确发现集恢复率仍然较低，假阳性仍然常见，重复运行一致性校准不佳。

Conclusion: 研究结果识别出了分辨率-广度权衡，而非区域聚焦证据的普遍优势，这促使需要规则感知的证据路由和专家审查，为专业文档审查系统的设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Professional-document review is a constraint-checking problem in which decisions depend on relations among text, geometry, pages, and document revisions. We present an evidence-grounded pipeline that normalizes extracted facts, executes four-state rules deterministically, retains source spans, and escalates unresolved cases. We evaluate its PDF evidence allocator on 160 reference-based tasks from 29 construction projects using a repeated four-system test and a disjoint two-system breadth extension. In the repeated test, reallocating a four-image budget from retrieved page overviews to one overview and three overlapping tiles improves project-family standardized decision accuracy by 10.6 percentage points (95% project-cluster bootstrap CI: 4.3 to 18.0; exact p = 0.031). This effect does not persist in the broader block: Region-RAG changes accuracy by -4.1 points (95% CI: -10.2 to 1.9; exact p = 0.209), while an equal-image sensitivity favors page breadth. Exact finding-set recovery remains low, false passes remain common, and repeated-run agreement is poorly calibrated. The results identify a resolution-breadth trade-off rather than a universal advantage for region-focused evidence, motivating rule-aware evidence routing and expert review.

</details>


### [116] [On the Generalization of Steering Vectors for Chain-of-Thought Faithfulness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29062)
*Matthew Nguyen,Kyle Cox,Austin Meek,Iván Arcuschin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究研究了激活引导技术如何提高思维链（CoT）的忠实度（即模型是否承认其推理过程中的关键步骤），发现虽然引导效果主要在较大模型（Gemma-3 12B）上显著，但一旦有效，其效果可跨提示类型和数据集广泛泛化，且引导向量的构建方法对效果影响较小；更重要的是，引导并未增加提示的使用率，而是减少了未被承认的隐藏提示使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着思维链（Chain of Thought, CoT）技术的发展，模型能够用自然语言表达其推理过程，这为AI安全提供了新的监控机会。然而，研究表明模型有时未能如实披露其推理过程中的关键步骤（例如受到误导性提示时），这种CoT的不忠实性（unfaithfulness）限制了其作为监控工具的有效性。先前工作表明激活引导（activation steering）可能改善CoT的忠实度，但其泛化能力尚不明确，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 研究在受提示影响的问答设置下，系统评估了激活引导技术对三个模型（Gemma-3 4B、Qwen-3.5 9B、Gemma-3 12B）CoT忠实度的改善效果及其泛化能力。具体考察了：(1) 跨提示类型和跨数据集的泛化性能；(2) 四种不同引导向量构建方法的效果差异；(3) 通过分析引导后提示使用率和隐藏提示使用率的变化，验证引导的作用机制是否仅提升提示显著性而非针对语言化行为。

Result: 主要发现包括：(1) 激活引导仅在最大模型（Gemma-3 12B）上稳定提升提示承认率；(2) 当引导有效时，其效果可广泛泛化——在跨提示和跨数据集分析中，效果大小主要由评估设置而非向量训练设置决定；(3) 四种引导向量构建方法（包括目标函数不提及特定提示类型的方法）效果相近，说明构建方式对结果影响有限；(4) 引导后提示使用率基本不变，但未被承认的隐藏提示使用显著减少，表明引导针对的是语言化行为而非单纯提升提示显著性。

Conclusion: 本研究表明，激活引导技术作为一种提升CoT忠实度的方法，虽然其有效性在较大模型中更为显著，但一旦有效便具有跨提示类型、跨数据集的强泛化能力。更重要的是，研究排除了引导仅增加提示显著性的替代解释，证实其确实促进了模型对推理过程的如实披露。这些发现为AI安全领域提供了重要见解，有助于发展更可靠的模型推理监控方法。

Abstract: Model capabilities have improved in large part due to scaling chain of thought. This has been a promising development for AI safety--where models verbalize their reasoning, it is possible to monitor it. However, in some cases, models do not verbalize important steps in their reasoning process. For example, models prompted with a cue suggesting the incorrect answer may fail to acknowledge that cue, even when it appears instrumental to their conclusion. When chain of thought (CoT) fails to disclose instrumental reasoning steps, we describe it as unfaithful. Prior work has shown that activation steering can be a useful method to improve faithfulness in CoT. We extend this line of work by studying how well steering for faithfulness generalizes across cue types, datasets, and methods of constructing the steering vector for three models (Gemma-3 4B, Qwen-3.5 9B, Gemma-3 12B) in a cued question-answering setting. While steering reliably increases cue acknowledgment for only the largest model (Gemma-3 12B), we find that when steering is effective, its effect generalizes broadly across cue types and datasets--in cross-cue and cross-dataset analyses, effect size is determined primarily by the evaluation setting, rather than the vector's train setting. How the vector is built also matters little--four construction methods, including one whose optimization target mentions no specific cue, yield similar effect sizes. Finally, we consider the possibility that steering promotes the salience of the cue and causes greater cue use, rather than targeting verbalization behaviors. However, we find no evidence for this--steering leaves the rate of cue use roughly unchanged while reducing hidden cue use, i.e., cue use that is not acknowledged.

</details>


### [117] [A Generalized-Bayes Perspective on Counterfactual Explanations: Posterior-Based Decision-Making and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29077)
*Keita Kinjo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将基于距离最小化的反事实解释（CE）重新表述为广义贝叶斯框架下的Gibbs后验MAP估计，并在此基础上提出了两种新的决策规则（贝叶斯决策和CVaR-CE）以及考虑模型多重性的扩展方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的反事实解释（CE）通常被表述为距离最小化问题，但这种表述的理论基础尚未得到充分探讨。本文旨在为基于距离最小化的CE提供理论基础，并基于后验视角扩展CE的决策框架。

Method: 作者证明了距离最小化CE在数学上等价于广义贝叶斯框架下使用基于距离先验的Gibbs后验的MAP估计（称为DP-GBCE）。基于此，提出了两种新的决策规则：最小化期望决策损失的贝叶斯决策规则和风险规避的CVaR-CE规则。同时，提出使用贝叶斯模型权重来混合多模型的后验分布，以解决模型多重性问题。

Result: 理论贡献在于建立了距离最小化CE与广义贝叶斯框架的联系；方法贡献包括开发了两种新决策规则和模型多重性扩展；实验贡献在于定义了评估指标，并通过模拟数据和Google Trends数据量化了不同决策规则之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 本文为传统CE方法提供了坚实的理论基础，并展示了后验视角如何能够自然地推导出更丰富的决策规则，包括贝叶斯决策和风险规避决策，同时解决了模型多重性问题，为CE方法的理论发展和实践应用提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations (CEs) enhance the interpretability of machine learning models by identifying the smallest change to an input required to obtain a desired output. Although CEs are conventionally formulated as a distance-minimization problem, the theoretical basis of this formulation has received limited attention. We show that a distance-minimization-based CE is mathematically equivalent to the maximum a posteriori (MAP) estimate of a Gibbs posterior within the generalized Bayes framework, specifically when a distance-based prior is used. We call this formulation the Distance-Prior Generalized Bayes CE (DP-GBCE). Building on this posterior perspective, we introduce two decision rules beyond MAP within a unified framework: a Bayes decision that minimizes expected decision loss and CVaR-CE, a risk-averse decision rule. We also propose an extension that uses Bayesian model weights to mix the posterior distributions of multiple models, thereby accounting for model multiplicity, where several models have comparable predictive performance. Finally, we define metrics for evaluating both individual CEs and the posterior distribution as a whole, and use experiments on simulated data and Google Trends data to quantify the trade-offs among the decision rules.

</details>


### [118] [Harnessing the Wisdom of LLM Crowds through Complementarity-Driven Iterative Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29087)
*Yanbin Fang,Xuan Wei,Wei Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出WILC框架，通过迭代反思精炼和互补性驱动的模型选择实现多LLM协同，在降低约7倍成本的同时达到与GPT-5.2相当的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业环境中部署的单一LLM存在模型特定的能力边界，现有组合方法采用固定方式，忽视了复杂问题解决中动态的、状态依赖的互补性作用，这既带来部署挑战也创造了集体智能的机会。

Method: 基于群体智慧范式，提出WILC框架，包含两个设计原则：(1)迭代反思-精炼建立状态保持工作流，模型诊断并改进先前输出；(2)互补性驱动的模型选择通过双重门控机制管理转换，包括前瞻互补性拟合（PCF）识别最适合当前瓶颈的模型，以及后验互补性增益（PCG）评估转换是否改进演化中的解决方案。

Result: 在四个多样化基准测试中，WILC超越单模型自精炼、集成方法和查询路由方法等现有方法。在标准化定价假设下，WILC以约低7倍的查询成本达到GPT-5.2的平均基准性能，同时通过自托管部署支持数据主权。

Conclusion: 本研究将群体智慧理论从静态聚合扩展至序列AI互补性，为多AI协同提供了可迁移的设计原则，在成本效益和性能平衡方面展示了多模型协同的优势。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in enterprise settings, yet individual models remain bounded by model-specific capability limitations. These heterogeneous boundaries pose a deployment challenge, but also create an opportunity: strategically coordinating multiple LLMs may unlock collective intelligence exceeding any single model. Existing approaches fix how models are combined in advance, overlooking the dynamic, state-dependent role of complementarity in complex problem solving. Drawing on the wisdom-of-crowds paradigm, we reconceptualize collective LLM intelligence as relay-style complementarity: a sequential process in which each successor model is selected to address the specific bottleneck identified in its predecessor's output. To operationalize this, we propose WILC (Wisdom Integration of LLM Crowds), a framework grounded in two design principles. First, iterative reflection-and-refinement establishes a state-preserving workflow through which models diagnose and refine prior outputs. Second, complementarity-driven model selection governs transitions via a dual-gate mechanism: prospective complementarity fit (PCF) identifies the worker most suited to the current bottleneck, while posterior complementarity gain (PCG) evaluates whether the selected transition improves the evolving solution. Experiments across four diverse benchmarks show that WILC outperforms existing approaches, including single-model self-refinement, ensemble methods, and query-routing methods. Under standardized pricing assumptions, WILC matches the average benchmark performance of GPT-5.2 at roughly 7 times lower estimated per-query cost, while facilitating data sovereignty through self-hosted deployment. This study extends wisdom-of-crowds theory from static aggregation to sequential AI complementarity and provides transferable design principles for multi-AI coordination.

</details>


### [119] [CAGE: Certified Authorization under Typed-Return Uncertainty for Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29190)
*Blaise Delattre,Cong Wang,Yang Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对工具使用型LLM智能体中运行时权限验证的安全漏洞，提出CAGE框架来验证候选动作在合理返回值邻域内的安全性，解决了分类和数值通道分别验证不满足组合性的关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的运行时权限验证机制仅对观测到的返回值和动作进行点态授权，但未考虑返回值绑定过程中可能存在的小错误（如绑定错误和数值漂移）。这些小错误可能导致本应被拒绝的危险动作被错误授权，形成安全漏洞。

Method: 提出CAGE框架，通过联合验证分类和数值两个通道来解决这个问题。该方法枚举离散分支并验证每个分支内的连续扰动，包括三个变体：(1) CAGE-Exact直接验证可执行策略；(2) CAGE-Lip和CAGE-RS在策略不可执行时，基于测量的保真度假设验证学习到的门控函数。

Result: 在合成数据、策略即代码、监管规则和真实交易等多种场景的实验中，CAGE成功消除了精确点态门控所允许的预算内错误通过案例，同时保持了有用比例的自主决策能力。实验证明了分类和数值通道分离验证确实存在组合性问题。

Conclusion: CAGE框架通过直接联合验证返回值的邻域安全性，有效解决了传统点态验证方法的不足。该方法为工具使用型LLM智能体提供了更鲁棒的安全保障，在保持自动化决策效率的同时显著提升了安全性。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents act on typed tool returns, records pairing provenance and categorical fields with numerical values. Runtime permission gates generally authorize the observed return and action, leaving the decision unprotected against small errors in how the return was bound to its source. We ask whether a candidate action stays authorized over a declared neighborhood of plausible correctly bound returns: one admissible binding fault plus bounded numerical drift. We prove that certifying the categorical and numerical channels separately does not compose: perturbations that are safe on each channel alone can jointly turn the same action unsafe. CAGE certifies this joint neighborhood directly, enumerating the discrete branches exactly and certifying the continuous perturbation within each branch. Across synthetic, policy-as-code, regulatory, and real-transaction settings, CAGE removes the in-budget false allows that accurate pointwise gates admit, while keeping a useful fraction of decisions autonomous. When the policy is executable, CAGE-Exact certifies the policy itself; otherwise CAGE-Lip and CAGE-RS certify a learned gate under an explicit, measured fidelity assumption.

</details>


### [120] [MirrorCraft: Paired Evaluation under Hidden Rule Changes in Minecraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29218)
*Jianxin Gao,Beini Hu,Runze Li,Wanli Peng,Ruohan Lei,Jinyuan Zhang,Linna Deng,Tianyi Yu,Zining Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了MirrorCraft基准测试，用于评估大语言模型智能体在Minecraft隐藏规则变化下的表现，发现隐藏规则变化对智能体性能有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Minecraft基准测试大多在固定游戏机制下评估智能体，无法测试智能体在熟悉规则发生变化时的适应能力。需要研究当配方、掉落物和其他规则改变时，智能体能否持续取得进展。

Method: 引入MirrorCraft配对基准测试，每个Mirror世界是配对Vanilla世界的副本，通过数据包修改选定的服务器端规则。地形、生成点、资源放置、目标、界面和动作预算在每对Vanilla-Mirror中保持匹配。包含5个受控生物群落、6个规则套件、3个进度目标、2个模型家族和6种智能体配置。使用规则干预效应(RIE)测量匹配世界间的性能变化。

Result: 隐藏规则变化在不同规则套件间产生显著不同的效果。在未提供规则描述的配置中，ReAct获得了最高的汇总Mirror分数。提供确切规则在所有三个目标中带来适度的平均进度和完成率提升。

Conclusion: MirrorCraft将Minecraft评估扩展到固定机制之外，为研究智能体在当前世界规则与熟悉规则不同时如何利用游戏结果提供了可控设置。

Abstract: With the prosperity of the large language models (LLMs), it has become an interesting topic: how do LLM-based agents work in Minecraft? Unfortunately, most existing benchmarks evaluate them under fixed game mechanics. High performance in these settings does not show whether an agent can continue making progress when familiar recipes, drops, and other rules change. In this paper, we introduce MirrorCraft, a paired benchmark for evaluating agents under hidden rule changes in Minecraft. Each Mirror world is a copy of its paired Vanilla world, with selected server-side rules modified by the corresponding datapack. Terrain, spawn, resource placement, objective, interface, and action budget remain matched within every Vanilla-Mirror pair. MirrorCraft includes five controlled biomes, six rule suites, three progression objectives, two model families, and six agent configurations under a shared Mineflayer interface. We evaluate task progress with deterministic advancement milestones and success rate and use the Rule Intervention Effect (RIE) to measure the performance change between matched Vanilla and Mirror worlds. The experiments show that hidden rule changes have strongly different effects across suites. Among the configurations evaluated without rule descriptions, ReAct achieves the highest pooled Mirror score. Providing the exact rules yields modest gains in average progress and completion across all three objectives. MirrorCraft extends Minecraft evaluation beyond fixed mechanics and provides a controlled setting for studying how agents use gameplay outcomes when the rules of the current world differ from familiar ones.

</details>


### [121] [Don't Mix Rewards, Mix Policies: Policy Decomposition and Optimization for Multi-Reward RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29246)
*Ruiming Liang,Yi Zhong,Yizhen Yuan,Yinan Zheng,Tianyi Tan,Tianyue Wang,Haiyun Guo,Jinqiao Wang,Xianyuan Zhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了PRISM框架，通过策略空间分解与组合解决多奖励强化学习中的对齐税问题，实现了更优性能和推理时可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大语言模型需要适应不同的人类价值观和应用场景，导致多奖励强化学习变得重要。然而，多奖励优化面临严重的对齐税问题：不同优化目标之间可能相互权衡甚至冲突，导致后训练过程不稳定且低效。

Method: 提出PRISM框架，基于策略空间分解与组合的思想。不同于组合不同奖励，PRISM优化一组独立的正策略和一个全局负策略，从而缓解多奖励策略优化过程中的潜在冲突，同时通过灵活的策略组合实现推理时的可控性。

Result: 在科学推理、工具使用推理以及有用性-安全性对齐任务上的实验表明，PRISM一致性地超越了现有的多奖励强化学习基线方法，并额外提供了推理时偏好控制的可控性。

Conclusion: PRISM为多奖励强化学习提供了一种有效的解决方案，通过策略空间分解缓解了优化冲突，不仅提升了模型性能，还赋予了推理阶段的灵活控制能力。

Abstract: Modern large language models (LLMs) are expected not just to answer correctly, but to adapt their behavior to different human values and use cases. As a result, multi-reward reinforcement learning (RL) has become an increasingly important problem for LLMs, where each reward captures a different aspect of desired behavior. However, optimizing with multiple rewards suffers from a more severe alignment tax issue, where different optimization objectives can trade off or even conflict with each other, leading to unstable and inefficient post-training. In this work, we propose PRISM, a new multi-reward RL framework built upon the idea of policy-space decomposition and composition. Instead of compositing different rewards, PRISM optimizes a set of standalone positive policies and a global negative policy. This alleviates the potential conflict during multi-reward policy optimization, while enabling controllability during inference by flexible policy composition. Experiments on scientific reasoning, tool-use reasoning, and helpfulness-safety alignment show that PRISM consistently outperforms existing multi-reward RL baselines, with extra controllability for inference-time preference control.

</details>


### [122] [Tool Specifications Matter: Uncovering and Mitigating Safety Risks in AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29254)
*Minghui Pan,Jiayuxuan Yang,Yuanyuan Yuan,Yu Jiang,Zhenpeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现schema格式的工具规范是导致AI智能体安全性降低的主要原因，并提出了SafeKeep方法，将安全性判断与工具执行分离，显著提高了对有害请求的拒绝率并降低了攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）在作为智能体部署时，安全性会显著降低，但这种性能退化的原因尚不清楚。随着AI智能体能够通过外部工具执行现实世界中的重要操作，理解并解决这一安全性问题变得至关重要。

Method: 通过白盒表示分析识别出schema格式工具规范是安全性退化的主要来源；提出SafeKeep推理时保护方法，将安全性判断与工具执行解耦：使用扁平化文本工具规范评估请求，同时保留原始schema格式规范用于执行。

Result: 在两个代表性基准和四个LLM（包括白盒和黑盒模型）上，SafeKeep将有害请求的平均拒绝率从23.8%提高到70.6%，将观察级提示注入下的平均攻击成功率从25.6%降低到2.5%，同时优于现有保护方法并保持任务处理能力。

Conclusion: schema格式工具规范会削弱模型的内部拒绝信号并导致不安全的工具执行；通过将安全性判断与工具执行分离，SafeKeep能有效提升AI智能体的安全性，为构建更安全的智能体系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: AI agents extend large language models (LLMs) with external tools, enabling them to perform complex tasks and translate model outputs into consequential real-world actions. Yet LLMs often become substantially less safe when deployed as agents, and the source of this degradation remains poorly understood. In this paper, we identify schema-formatted tool specifications as a primary source of agent safety degradation and show, through white-box representation analysis, that they weaken the model's internal refusal signals and contribute to unsafe tool execution. Building on this finding, we propose SafeKeep, an inference-time safeguard that decouples safety judgment from tool execution: it assesses requests using flattened textual tool specifications while retaining the original schema-formatted specifications for execution. Across two representative benchmarks and four LLMs, including both white-box and black-box models, SafeKeep increases the average refusal rate for harmful requests from 23.8% to 70.6% and reduces the average attack success rate under observation-level prompt injection from 25.6% to 2.5%. It also outperforms existing safeguards and preserves task-handling capability. We release the code and data at https://github.com/snowcatsmoking/SafeKeep .

</details>


### [123] [MAGA: Multi-Platform Self-Fusion of GUI Agents via Structured Action Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29320)
*Hang Yan,Zhangxuan GU,Beitong Zhou,Jiaxuan Chen,Runze Li,Yusong Hu,Shuheng Shen,Changhua Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAGA方法，通过重新分配训练信号聚焦于结构化动作错误，解决了跨环境GUI智能体融合中的专家冲突问题，在8B规模上超越最强基线2.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的GUI智能体通常针对特定领域设计，限制了部署范围和用户体验。虽然将领域专家模型融合为单一跨环境策略的需求迫切，但现有方法存在缺陷：权重合并会在专家意见不一致时破坏可执行动作，而现有的在策略蒸馏（OPD）方法在蒸馏过程中对所有响应token同等对待，忽略了动作token是环境与智能体之间唯一的交互接口这一关键事实。

Method: 提出MAGA（Masked Action-Guided Agent Distillation）方法，核心创新点包括：（1）根据结构化动作的正确性重新分配训练信号，抑制不必要或无效的蒸馏信号，将学习聚焦于错误的动作；（2）引入仅训练时的提示机制来优化领域专家教师提供的监督信号，而不改变学生模型的输入。该方法针对动作token的重要性进行差异化处理，避免了传统蒸馏中token无差别对待的问题。

Result: 在两种模型规模下进行实验，MAGA取得了最高的平均成功率。在8B参数规模上，MAGA超越了最强基线方法2.0%的性能，并且达到了与领域专家教师模型几乎相同的平均性能表现。

Conclusion: MAGA通过动作导向的训练信号重新分配策略，有效解决了跨环境GUI智能体融合中的关键挑战，证明了针对动作token进行差异化蒸馏的重要性，为构建统一的多环境GUI智能体提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) agents based on large language models are increasingly deployed across mobile, web, and desktop environments. However, existing agents are typically domain-specific, limiting the deployment and user experience. This motivates the consolidation of specialized models into a single cross-environment policy. Weight merging directly merges domain-specific experts but can corrupt executable actions under expert disagreement, while on-policy distillation (OPD) avoids conflicting teacher supervision yet still treats all response tokens equally during distillation, ignoring that action tokens are the only interface between the environment and the agent. To address this, We introduce MAGA that re-allocates training signal according to the structured action. Based on the correctness of the generated action, it suppresses unnecessary or invalid distillation signals and focuses learning on erroneous actions. Besides, a training-only hint optimizes the supervision signal provided by domain-specific teachers without changing the student input. Across two model scales, MAGA achieves the highest mean success rate, outperforming the strongest baseline by 2.0% at 8B and achieves almost the same average performance with teachers.

</details>


### [124] [ModelEquivBench: Certifying Multi-Relational Evaluation of LLM-Generated Optimization Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29431)
*Penglin Zhu,Jungang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ModelEquivBench，一个多关系、可认证的评估系统，用于细粒度评估大语言模型生成的优化模型。通过E0-E6七层语义关系验证，发现现有粗粒度评估方法无法捕捉模型在不同阶段的差异化失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法将生成模型与真实值简单判定为"等价/不等价"或仅报告执行成功率，这种粗粒度标签既不可独立验证，也无法反映两个优化问题表述在多种语义层面上的差异。缺乏细粒度、可认证的评估体系，无法准确诊断模型生成能力的具体缺陷。

Method: 提出ModelEquivBench系统，建立E0-E6七层语义关系验证框架：(E0)模型构建与精确解析、(E1)验证表示对齐、(E2)同空间可行集关系、(E3)投影可行集关系、(E4)目标函数顺序等价性、(E5)最优值相等性、(E6)最优解集等价性。每层关系配备可独立重验的证据：可回放轨迹、精确映射、有理数证书以及显式反例。对不确定或资源受限情况输出UNKNOWN/N/A而非猜测，对缺失先决条件输出ABSENT。

Result: 在173个基准问题集上评估GPT-5.4、Claude Sonnet 4.6和Qwen3.5-397B-A17B三个模型快照（每模型346个单元），发现：(1) 分别有49、35、25个单元虽可执行但在至少一个关系上被认证为负；(2) 分别有25、8、18个结构被拒绝但E2认证可行集相等。三个模型在不同验证阶段失败，无法简化为单一准确率分数。

Conclusion: ModelEquivBench提供的多层语义分析揭示了粗粒度基准无法捕捉的模型能力差异，证明了细粒度、可认证评估体系对于准确诊断和改进大语言模型优化问题生成能力的重要性。不同模型呈现差异化失败模式，验证了现有简化评估方法的局限性。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly generate optimization models from natural language, but existing evaluation often reduces a generated model and its ground truth to a single equivalent/not-equivalent verdict or an execution-success rate--labels that are neither independently checkable nor faithful to the multiple distinct senses in which two formulations can agree. We present ModelEquivBench, a certifying, multi-relational evaluation system that reports a per-pair semantic profile E0--E6: model construction and exact ingestion (E0), verified representation alignment (E1), same-space and projected feasible-set relations (E2, E3), objective-order equivalence (E4), optimal-value equality (E5), and optimizer-set equivalence (E6). Each decided entry carries relation-appropriate, independently re-checkable evidence: replayable traces or explicit maps for E0--E1, exact-rational certificates for positive E2--E6 conclusions, and explicit witnesses for supported negatives. Incomplete mapping search, unsupported structure, and resource limits produce typed UNKNOWN or N/A outcomes rather than guesses, while unmet prerequisites are reported as ABSENT. Using ModelEquivBench to evaluate three model snapshots--GPT-5.4, Claude Sonnet 4.6, and Qwen3.5-397B-A17B--on the same frozen cohort of 173 base problems (346 cells per model) under a no-repair protocol, the resulting profiles expose distinctions that coarse baselines do not represent: 49, 35, and 25 cells contain executable candidates that are nevertheless certified negative on at least one supported relation, and 25, 8, and 18 structural rejections occur on pairs for which E2 certifies mapped feasible-set equality under a verified map. The three model snapshots fail at different stages of the profile and therefore cannot be meaningfully reduced to a single accuracy score.

</details>


### [125] [Beyond Retrieval: Analytic Memory for Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29440)
*Zhoujin Tian,Yao Tian,Hao Zhang,Cheng Chen,Yakun Li,Lei Zhang,Xiaofang Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaMM框架，联合支持检索记忆和分析记忆，在两个长期多模态记忆基准上分别提升11.3%和7.3%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统主要关注检索记忆，通过摘要和索引组织交互历史以返回查询相关信息。但长期多模态记忆不仅需要检索相关信息，还需要对跨交互累积的观测数据进行计算。因此需要一种互补的分析记忆抽象，支持过滤、聚合、排序和时间比较等分析操作。

Method: 提出AdaMM框架，从对话、图像和上下文元数据中提取溯源链接的属性-值观测，发现重复出现的字段结构并将其物化以支持分析访问。在推理时，记忆感知规划器将查询分解为检索和分析操作，并将每种操作路由到适当的工具。该框架联合支持检索记忆和分析记忆两种互补抽象。

Result: 在两个长期多模态记忆基准数据集MemEye和MemGallery上的实验表明，AdaMM分别提升了11.3%和7.3%的性能。

Conclusion: 分析记忆作为检索记忆的互补抽象，通过组织重复的多模态观测为可查询结构，能够有效支持复杂的分析操作。AdaMM框架成功实现了检索和分析记忆的联合支持，无需依赖应用定义的模式即可自动发现和物化字段结构。

Abstract: Long-term multimodal memory must support not only retrieving relevant information but also computing over observations accumulated across interactions. Existing systems largely emphasize \emph{retrieval memory}, organizing interaction histories through summaries and indexes to return query-relevant information at multiple granularities, from high-level abstractions to underlying records. In this paper, we formulate \emph{analytic memory} as a complementary abstraction that organizes recurring multimodal observations into queryable structures supporting filtering, aggregation, ranking, and temporal comparison. We present AdaMM, a framework that jointly supports retrieval and analytic memory. Rather than relying on application-defined schemas, AdaMM extracts provenance-linked attribute-value observations from dialogue, images, and contextual metadata, discovers recurring field structures, and materializes them for analytical access. At inference time, a memory-aware planner decomposes queries into retrieval and analytic operations and routes each operation to the appropriate tools. Experiments on two long-term multimodal memory benchmarks, MemEye and MemGallery, show that AdaMM improves performance by up to 11.3\% and 7.3\%, respectively.

</details>


### [126] [Self-Play Meets Skill Evolution: Self-Evolving Search Agents that Pose, Solve, and Remember](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29468)
*Zenghuang Fu,Zhaoyang Li,Qiuyuan Ai,Haoyu Wu,Minghui Wu,Chenxu Zhao,Ante Wang,Guannan He,Changwei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SESA框架，通过挑战者-求解器自博弈机制使技能记忆与任务生成协同进化，在7个问答基准上准确率提升1.2-3.2点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自博弈代理的课程学习缺乏持久状态（失败仅影响梯度，不显式塑造未来练习），而外部技能记忆通常从固定任务分布中学习，缺乏动态演化能力。

Method: 引入SESA框架，包含：1）挑战者提出问题，求解器检索技能解决问题；2）将有信息量的失败提炼为可复用技能并写回记忆；3）记忆更新改变求解器行为和成功率，进而改变挑战者的奖励和未来问题分布，形成双向循环。

Result: 在7个开放域和多跳问答基准上，SESA相比SSP平均准确率提升1.2-3.2点，相比SkillRL提升0.9点；在Qwen3模型上，SESA-Off相比SSP保留1.8-2.2点提升，最终技能库额外增加0.5-1.0点。

Conclusion: 演化的技能记忆不仅是推理时插件，更能改变策略学习和未来训练分布，同时保留可选外部记忆的价值，实现了记忆与策略的协同进化。

Abstract: Self-play agents can generate training problems without questions from target benchmarks, but their curricula lack persistent state: failures affect gradients yet do not explicitly shape future practice. External skill memories preserve procedural experience but are typically learned from fixed task distributions. We introduce \textbf{SESA} (Self-Evolving Skill-Augmented Agent), which makes procedural memory an evolving state of tool-augmented search self-play. A challenger poses problems, while a separately parameterized solver alone retrieves skills. Informative failures are distilled into reusable skills and written back to memory. The updated memory changes solver behavior and success, which changes the challenger's reward and the distribution of future problems; the resulting frontier produces new failures that rewrite memory. This bidirectional loop makes task generation and skill memory co-evolve. Because retrieved skills shape on-policy training trajectories, their benefits can enter the model parameters as well as remain in the external bank, enabling memory-free deployment and optional inference-time retrieval. Across seven open-domain and multi-hop question-answering benchmarks, SESA improves average accuracy over SSP by 1.2--3.2 points across multiple backbones and surpasses the skill-augmented SkillRL baseline by 0.9 points under a unified evaluation protocol. On Qwen3 models, SESA-Off retains 1.8--2.2 points of improvement over SSP, while the final skill bank adds a further 0.5--1.0 points. These results show that evolving skill memory is not merely an inference-time plug-in: it changes policy learning and the future training distribution while retaining value as optional external memory. Our code is available at https://github.com/Zenghuang-Fu/SESA-Self-Evolving-Search-Agents.

</details>


### [127] [AMTFV: Agentic Mathematical Tool-Flow Verification for LLM Self-Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29549)
*Rui Zou,Yutao Zhu,Mengqi Wei,Ji-Rong Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了AMTFV框架，通过数学工具流(MTF)解耦验证建模与具体执行，利用数学工具箱支持精确计算，在五个数学推理数据集上相比最强基线平均准确率提升最高8.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型数学问题求解的验证方法存在局限性：基于自然语言反思的方法无法可靠支持精确计算，直接生成验证程序的方法过早将数学建模与底层实现耦合，影响验证的可靠性。

Method: 提出AMTFV框架，引入数学工具流(MTF)作为中断-执行-恢复接口。验证代理首先构建验证工作流，将需要可靠执行的数学对象和计算意图编码为MTF请求发送给数学工具箱代理；后者解析请求、生成可执行调用并分发到后端进行精确计算；工具输出支持候选答案裁决、答案修订和验证工作流修订。

Result: 在五个具有挑战性的数学推理数据集上，使用DeepSeek、GPT和Gemini的七种模型配置进行评估，AMTFV总体上优于代表性基线；在单模型配置下，相比最强基线平均准确率提升最高达8.3个百分点，在中等和高验证复杂度样本上提升更大。

Conclusion: AMTFV通过解耦验证建模与具体执行并支持精确计算，有效提升了大语言模型数学问题答案验证的可靠性，为数学推理任务提供了一种有效的验证框架。

Abstract: Large language models have demonstrated strong mathematical problem-solving capabilities, yet reliably verifying their candidate answers remains challenging. Existing representative methods mainly revise outputs through natural-language reflection or assist verification by directly generating verification programs; the former may not reliably support exact computation, whereas the latter prematurely couples mathematical modeling with low-level implementation. We propose AMTFV (Agentic Mathematical Tool-Flow Verification). By introducing Mathematical Tool Flow (MTF) as an interrupt--execute--resume interface, AMTFV decouples verification modeling from concrete execution and supports exact computation through a mathematical toolbox. Specifically, the verification agent first constructs a verification workflow, encodes the mathematical objects and computational intent requiring reliable execution in an MTF request, and sends it to the mathematical toolbox agent. The latter parses the request, generates executable calls, and dispatches them to the backend for exact computation. Tool outputs then support candidate-answer adjudication, answer revision, and verification-workflow revision. We evaluate AMTFV on five challenging mathematical reasoning datasets with seven model configurations from DeepSeek, GPT, and Gemini. Experimental results show that AMTFV outperforms the representative baselines evaluated in this study overall; under an individual model configuration, it improves average accuracy over the strongest baseline by up to 8.3 percentage points, with larger gains on samples of medium and high verification complexity.

</details>


### [128] [COntExt: Towards Context-Aware Ontology Extension from Operational Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29553)
*Hussain Hussain,Stefan Schöberl,Angelika Schneider,Verena Geist*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了COntExt框架，通过利用结构化运营指标定义作为输入，自动建议如何将相关概念和属性集成到现有本体中，从而实现低成本的本体扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织越来越多地以结构化、机器可读的格式定义运营指标来监控系统、流程和合规性。这些指标定义隐含地编码了领域知识，通常超出了形式本体所捕获的内容。然而，运营指标目录与本体知识之间的连接仍然是手动、临时且劳动密集型的，缺乏系统化的方法来利用这些丰富的知识源进行本体扩展。

Method: 提出了COntExt框架，将本体扩展问题定义为三个子任务：父类预测、关系类型预测和数据属性分配。框架以结构化指标定义为输入，利用这些指标的上下文来建议如何将引用的概念和属性集成到现有本体中。在四个网络安全本体上评估了每个任务的不同算法。

Result: 实验结果表明，在关系类型预测和数据属性分配任务上，指标派生的上下文相比仅使用本体上下文的基线方法能够改善建议质量。这证明了运营指标目录是本体扩展的一个实用且未被充分利用的知识源。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了运营指标目录作为本体扩展知识源的实用价值，使组织能够以显著低于手动工程的成本维护其本体，为本体工程提供了一种自动化、低成本的解决方案。

Abstract: Organizations increasingly define operational metrics in structured, machine-readable formats to monitor systems, processes, and compliance. These metric definitions implicitly encode domain knowledge, such as referencing concepts, properties, and relationships, that often extends what is captured in formal ontologies. Yet the connection between operational metric catalogues and ontological knowledge remains manual, ad-hoc, and labor-intensive. We present COntExt, a framework for context-aware ontology extension that takes structured metric definitions as input and suggests how referenced concepts and properties should be integrated into an existing ontology, utilizing the context of these metrics. The framework defines the extension problem as three sub-tasks: parent class prediction, relation type prediction, and data property assignment. Across four cybersecurity ontologies, we evaluate different algorithms for each task. Our results show that metric-derived context improves the suggestions over ontology-context baselines for relation type prediction and data property assignment. Our work demonstrates that operational metric catalogues are a practical and underexploited source for ontology extension. This work enables organizations to maintain their ontologies at a significantly lower cost than manual engineering.

</details>


### [129] [LEMUR: Learning to Align with Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning from Preference Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29559)
*Manith Adikari,Bei Peng,Samuele Vinanzi,Angelo Cangelosi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了LEMUR框架，首次将偏好学习与多目标强化学习结合，通过人类反馈联合学习策略和多个目标特定的奖励模型，解决了无预定义奖励函数的多目标决策问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的决策任务通常涉及多个相互竞争的目标（如性能与效率），其真实奖励函数难以指定或无法获取。现有方法存在局限性：多目标强化学习（MORL）假设每个目标都有明确指定的奖励函数，继承了单目标强化学习的挑战；而基于偏好的强化学习（PbRL）虽然能在无预定义奖励函数的情况下解决复杂任务，但主要局限于单目标设置。因此需要一种能够结合两者优势的新方法。

Method: 提出了LEMUR（Learning to Align with Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning with Preference feedback）框架。该方法使智能体能够通过与多个人类的偏好进行交互式学习，联合学习策略和多个目标特定的奖励模型，从而在学习过程中有效平衡相互竞争的目标。

Result: 在多种多目标基准任务上对LEMUR进行了评估，实验结果表明其在性能上优于基线方法，证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: LEMUR为解决无预定义奖励函数的多目标决策任务提供了一个有前景的方向，成功地将偏好学习与多目标强化学习相结合，实现了从人类反馈中学习多目标策略的目标。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) systems are typically trained using a single, well-specified scalar reward function. However, real-world decision-making tasks often involve multiple, competing objectives, such as performance versus efficiency, where ground-truth reward functions are difficult to specify or inaccessible. While Multi-Objective RL (MORL) addresses such trade-offs by modeling rewards as vectors, existing approaches typically assume access to a well-specified reward function for each objective, inheriting the same challenges faced by single-objective RL. Meanwhile, Preference-based RL (PbRL) has shown great potential in solving complex tasks without access to a pre-defined reward function through reward learning from human feedback, yet has largely been studied in single-objective settings. In this work, we bridge this gap with LEMUR: Learning to Align with Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning with Preference feedback, a novel framework where an agent interactively learns from the preferences of multiple humans to learn optimal multi-objective policies. Our approach jointly learns policies and multiple objective-specific reward models from human feedback, enabling agents to effectively balance competing objectives during learning. We evaluate LEMUR on a variety of benchmark multi-objective tasks, and empirical results demonstrate its superior performance over baseline methods. Our method presents a promising direction for solving multi-objective decision-making tasks without pre-defined reward functions.

</details>


### [130] [DungeonBench: A Benchmark for Rules-Rich Tactical Reasoning in Dungeons & Dragons Combat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29577)
*Ismayil Ismayilov,Atakan Kara,Kaan Oktay*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了DungeonBench基准测试，用于评估AI在《龙与地下城》战斗场景中的战术推理能力，发现前沿语言模型在单一战斗中表现良好，但在连续遭遇的资源管理方面存在显著缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试套件对规则丰富的战术推理能力测试不足，特别是当几何位置、时机、资源、目标和规则交互同时起作用时的复杂决策场景。需要一个能够全面测试战术推理能力的基准，保留简化的战斗模拟器通常抽象掉的机制细节。

Method: 构建了DungeonBench基准，基于2014年系统参考文档的大部分战斗相关内容，在每一步提供完整的战术观察、待决策项和可执行选项列表（包括移动、攻击、法术、反应、目标、准备和稀缺资源）。设计了两条测试轨道：遭遇轨道（评估单一战斗的局部战术）和每日轨道（通过持久化的生命值、法术位、消耗品等连接多个遭遇）。支持启发式控制器、语言模型策略、学习型选项排序器和掩码动作强化学习代理。

Result: 对前沿语言模型策略的评估显示，完整战术观察并未使基准饱和：前沿策略在直接遭遇中经常获胜，但连接的遭遇日暴露了资源预算、休息时机和规则感知战术纪律方面的失败。

Conclusion: DungeonBench成功揭示了当前AI系统在复杂战术推理方面的局限性，特别是在需要长期规划和资源管理的连续决策场景中，为未来战术AI研究提供了有价值的测试平台。

Abstract: Games and simulators make valuable benchmarks by turning decisions into measurable outcomes, but many current suites under-test rules-rich tactical reasoning: the ability to choose well when geometry, timing, resources, objectives, and rule interactions all matter at once. We introduce DungeonBench, a benchmark for tactical reasoning in Dungeons & Dragons combat, built to cover the vast majority of combat-relevant 2014 System Reference Document content whose effects can be resolved by the simulator while retaining mechanics that simplified combat simulators often abstract away. At each step, DungeonBench exposes a complete tactical observation, a pending decision, and an indexed list of executable options spanning movement, attacks, spells, reactions, objectives, preparation, and scarce resources. The task is to value legal choices whose consequences depend on action economy, creature traits, battlefield geometry, timing windows, and future encounters. DungeonBench has two tracks: Encounter, which evaluates local tactical play in single fights, and Day, which links encounters through persistent hit points, spell slots, consumables, preparation, and short-rest timing, forcing policies to trade off immediate tactical advantage against future survivability. The same engine-generated decision stream supports heuristic controllers, language-model policies, learned option rankers, and masked-action reinforcement-learning agents. We evaluate frontier language-model policies on this shared decision stream. Results show that full tactical observations do not saturate the benchmark: frontier policies often win direct encounters, but linked encounter days expose failures in resource budgeting, rest timing, and rule-aware tactical discipline.

</details>


### [131] [AgentHPOBench: A Benchmark For Evaluating LLM Agents as Sequential Hyperparameter Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29626)
*Tianyu Huai,Tingshuo Fan,Xinchi Chen,Yining Zheng,Yuxin Wang,Shuang Chen,Jie Zhou,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了AgentHPOBench，一个包含30个可执行机器学习任务的序贯基准，用于评估LLM代理解释实验证据并指导后续超参数决策的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要关注静态代码生成、论文复现或最终答案正确性，但未直接评估代理能否解释实验证据并据此指导后续超参数决策，存在评估空白。

Method: 构建包含7个研究类别、30个可执行机器学习任务的序贯基准AgentHPOBench。每个任务从验证的基线运行开始，代理执行多次序贯干预，在每步观察累积配置、指标和日志后提出下一个有效配置。

Result: 在统一协议下评估了12个广泛使用的代理和传统HPO基线，发现当前代理在跨领域实验优化方面表现出可测量的能力，但在持续迭代优化、复杂日志诊断和稳定逼近参考性能方面仍存在明显局限。

Conclusion: AgentHPOBench填补了评估代理实验优化能力的基准空白，揭示了当前LLM代理在科学实验迭代优化方面的能力与不足，为后续研究提供了标准化评估框架。

Abstract: As LLMs evolve from code completion systems into autonomous scientific agents, evaluating their ability to conduct experiments has become increasingly important. Existing benchmarks typically focus on static code generation, paper replication, or final answer correctness, but do not directly assess whether agents can interpret experimental evidence and use it to guide subsequent hyperparameter decisions. To address this gap, we introduce AgentHPOBench, a sequential benchmark comprising 30 executable machine learning tasks across seven research categories. Each task begins with a validated baseline run, after which an agent performs several sequential interventions. At each step, the agent observes the accumulated configurations, metrics, and logs before proposing the next valid configuration. We evaluate 12 widely used agents and conventional HPO baselines under a unified protocol. The results show that current agents exhibit measurable experimental optimization ability across domains, but still face clear limitations in sustained iterative refinement, complex log diagnosis, and consistent progress toward reported reference performance.

</details>


### [132] [Development of FDD-ON: an Ontology for VAV HVAC System Fault Detection and Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29657)
*Yimin Chen,Brian Fricke,Bo Shen,Jamie Lian,Mingkan Zhang,James Lo,Yun Zhang,Shi Ye,Jiajing Huang,Han Hu,Chujie Lu,Rui Tang,George Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了FDD-ON，一个模块化、可扩展的本体论，用于形式化表示VAV HVAC系统故障检测与诊断，解决数据可解释性和互操作性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前HVAC系统故障检测与诊断领域缺乏结构化的领域知识来整合异构数据源、多样化设备类型和诊断输出。数据可解释性和互操作性有限导致信息孤岛，阻碍了FDD及相关应用（如数字孪生FDD框架和AI驱动的维护决策系统）的实施。

Method: 构建FDD-ON本体论，形式化表示VAV HVAC系统组件、故障类型、症状状态、故障影响及相关属性。整合HVAC系统FDD语义，定义受控词汇表，建立故障、症状和影响库，明确因果关系链（根本原因-故障-症状-影响）。

Result: 通过公开VAV HVAC系统数据集进行评估，并在FDD开发应用中演示。结果表明FDD-ON为跨应用的FDD解决方案提供了可扩展、透明、可互操作的语义基础框架。

Conclusion: FDD-ON本体论有效解决了HVAC系统FDD领域的知识表示和互操作性问题，为诊断知识查询、异构FDD输出映射和互操作应用开发提供了机器可解释的基础。

Abstract: Fault detection and diagnosis (FDD) technology is essential for improving HVAC system reliability, energy efficiency, and maintenance effectiveness. However, effective deployment of FDD solutions in buildings requires structured domain knowledge that can bridge heterogeneous data sources, diverse equipment types, and varied diagnostic outputs. Limited data interpretability and interoperability within the FDD domain have led to fragmented information silos, hindering the implementation of FDD and related applications, such as the digital twin-enabled FDD frameworks and artificial intelligence (AI)-driven maintenance decision-making systems. This paper presents an FDD Ontology (FDD-ON), a modular and extensible ontology to formally represent variable air volume (VAV) HVAC system components, fault types, symptom statuses, fault impacts and associated attributes. FDD-ON integrates HVAC system FDD semantics to provide comprehensive representations of fault and symptom attributes, supported by the well-defined controlled vocabulary. Additionally, FDD-ON offers comprehensive fault, symptom, and impact libraries to capture a broad spectrum of operational abnormalities and their consequences in VAV HVAC systems. Through explicit contributing cause-fault-symptom-impact relations, FDD-ON serves as a machine-interpretable basis for querying diagnostic knowledge, mapping heterogeneous FDD outputs, and developing interoperable FDD-related applications. FDD-ON is evaluated using publicly available VAV HVAC system datasets and demonstrated through FDD development applications. Results indicate that FDD-ON provides a foundational semantic framework for advancing scalable, transparent, and interoperable FDD solutions across various applications.

</details>


### [133] [ExtractBench: A Benchmark for Schema-Guided Enterprise Document Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29677)
*Boyang Zhang,Adrian Lyjak,Eli Stewart,Zhaoqi Li,Simon Suo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了ExtractBench，这是首个综合评估模式引导文档提取任务的基准，涵盖价值准确性、记录完整性、溯源性和成本。评测发现商业VLM在短文档表现良好但在长文档上容易截断，编码代理准确性高但成本昂贵，LlamaExtract Agentic Plus在所有指标上排名第一，实现了高准确性和低成本的最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业工作流程越来越依赖智能代理进行模式引导的文档提取任务，即将用户定义的模式应用于文档以产生带有源证据的正确输出。然而，现有研究缺乏综合性基准来系统评估这一任务的多个关键维度，包括价值准确性、记录完整性、溯源性和成本效率，这阻碍了对不同方法优劣的深入理解和比较。

Method: 提出了ExtractBench基准，包含4,869页、370份企业文档、8个业务领域和67种文档类型，涵盖多种挑战场景。评测系统采用可扩展的模式和真实值策展管道，结合独立系统一致性验证真实文档、合成列表的已知值验证以及表单的人工验证。提出顺序无关的价值F1评估价值准确性，以及词级和页级F1两个溯源指标评估来源可追溯性。

Result: 在综合评测中发现：商业视觉语言模型在短文档上表现优异，但在长文档列表上经常发生截断；编码代理虽然保持更高准确性，但成本显著增加；LlamaExtract Agentic Plus在所有三个指标上排名第一，在准确性上媲美编码代理，而成本仅为后者的一小部分，实现了性能与成本的最佳平衡。

Conclusion: ExtractBench为企业级模式引导文档提取任务提供了首个多维度综合评估基准，填补了该领域评测空白。研究揭示了现有方法的优劣势：商业VLM在长文档处理上存在不足，编码代理成本过高，而LlamaExtract Agentic Plus展现了优越的性能-成本权衡。该基准及其评测代码已开源发布，为未来文档智能提取研究和应用提供了重要的评测标准和基线。

Abstract: Enterprise workflows increasingly rely on agents for \emph{schema-guided extraction}: given a document and a user-defined schema, the agent faithfully follows the schema to produce the correct output with source evidence as grounding metadata. We present ExtractBench, a benchmark for schema-guided extraction and, to our knowledge, the first to score value accuracy, record completeness at scale, grounding, and measured cost together. The evaluation system contains 4,869 pages across 370 enterprise documents, 8 business domains, and 67 document types, with clear tags differentiating their challenge scenarios. The scalable schema and ground-truth curation pipeline combines independent-system agreement for real documents, known values for synthetic lists, and human verification for forms. We report order-insensitive value F1 for value accuracy, plus two grounding metrics for source traceability: word- and page-level F1. Commercial VLMs perform well on short documents but often truncate record lists on long ones, while coding agents retain higher accuracy at much higher cost. LlamaExtract Agentic Plus ranks first on all three metrics, with accuracy comparable to coding agents at a fraction of the cost. Dataset and evaluation code are available on \href{https://huggingface.co/datasets/llamaindex/ExtractBench}{HuggingFace} and \href{https://github.com/run-llama/ExtractBench}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [134] [SVR: Self-Verifying Refinement via Joint Verdict-Confidence Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Test-Time Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28457)
*Hongyu Chen,Liang Lin,Guangrun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了自验证精炼（SVR）框架，通过强化学习让模型学会用自验证信号控制推理计算，无需外部反馈，在数学推理任务上以更少的推理轮次超越多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时计算扩展方法存在效率问题：均匀计算预算在简单样本上浪费计算资源，而验证器引导的精炼方法依赖外部反馈信号。需要一种无需外部监督、能自适应分配计算资源的方法。

Method: 提出自验证精炼（SVR）框架：模型在每轮生成解决方案的同时输出离散正确性判定和置信度分数；仅当判定为"正确"且置信度超阈值时才保留答案，否则继续精炼。使用GRPO在固定长轨迹上训练，奖励函数包含解的正确性、校准感知的自验证和停止就绪的正确状态；自适应停止仅在推理时激活。真实标签仅用于构建训练奖励，不暴露给策略。

Result: 在七个数学推理基准测试中，基于Qwen3.5-2B的SVR实现宏观平均准确率0.563，平均仅用2.99轮推理。在完整系统对比中超越标准GRPO、强多轮基线及固定预算的oracle引导分数反馈参考，且比固定十轮推理需要更少的推理轮次。

Conclusion: 学习到的自验证可作为有效的内部控制信号，用于答案保留和自适应测试时计算分配，证明了无需外部反馈的自适应推理计算控制的可行性。

Abstract: Scaling test-time computation can improve language-model reasoning, but uniform budgets waste computation on easy inputs, while verifier-guided refinement relies on external feedback. We introduce Self-Verifying Refinement (SVR), an oracle-free multi-turn reinforcement learning framework that learns to use self-verification as a compute-control policy. At each turn, the model produces a solution together with a discrete correctness verdict and a confidence score; it retains the current answer only when the verdict is Correct and confidence exceeds a threshold, and otherwise continues refinement using its own self-verification. Ground-truth correctness is used only to construct training rewards and is never exposed to the policy through refinement prompts or required at inference. SVR is trained with GRPO on fixed-horizon trajectories using rewards that promote solution correctness, calibration-aware self-verification, and stop-ready correct states; adaptive stopping is activated only at inference. On seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks with Qwen3.5-2B, SVR achieves a macro-average accuracy of 0.563 with only 2.99 inference turns on average. In the evaluated complete-system comparison, it exceeds standard GRPO, strong multi-turn baselines, and a fixed-budget oracle-guided score-feedback reference while requiring substantially fewer turns than fixed ten-turn inference. These results demonstrate that learned self-verification can serve as an effective internal control signal for answer retention and adaptive test-time compute allocation.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [CyberNeuro: A Privacy-Preserving Agentic Workbench for Cohort-Scale Neuroimage and Clinical Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28841)
*Ran Ren,Junhong Tong,Yunxi Kong,Yiyao Chen,Yucheng Li,Kunhao Zhou,Shaoqi Wang,Yuxiang Tao,Shuheng Cao,Zhihao Fan,Marissa DiPiero,Tingting Dan,Guorong Wu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了CyberNeuro，一个基于多智能体LLM的自动化神经影像分析平台，通过定制的本地LLM模型WandaMind和四个专用智能体，实现了从自然语言到复杂工作流的自动化执行，在NeuroBench测试集上将准确率从40%提升至69%，同时显著降低了token使用成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管神经影像方法取得了巨大成功，但将大规模、高维数据集准备用于AI/ML应用仍是一个关键的操作瓶颈。传统工作流程需要在元数据整理、流程执行、后处理质量控制和数据管理方面投入大量人工，这一负担严重限制了人力和计算基础设施有限的实验室参与。迫切需要可扩展、成本效益高的计算平台来普及高级神经影像分析并加速心理健康和临床转化的发现。

Method: 利用多智能体LLM突破，开发了CyberNeuro智能工作台，配备定制的本地LLM模型WandaMind，由四个专用智能体组成：Planner（规划器）、Validator（验证器）、Dispatcher（调度器）和Reporter（报告器）。这些智能体通过安全的MCP桥接和固定执行层进行通信，使研究人员能够使用自然语言执行复杂工作流程，同时保持临床级数据隐私。平台还集成了人在环验证面板以确保严格的生物医学质量控制。

Result: 在公开NeuroBench测试集上，CyberNeuro将基准模型的保留域准确率从40%提升至69%。在相同的10批次队列端到端工作流程套件中，本地WandaMind配置完成了所有任务，与Neuroclaw相比，估计的聚合token使用量分别约为10.6%（使用WandaMind）和61.7%（使用云提供商）。

Conclusion: CyberNeuro成功实现了神经影像分析的自动化和普及化，通过多智能体LLM系统和本地模型部署，在保证临床级数据隐私的同时，显著提升了分析性能并降低了计算成本，为资源有限的实验室提供了可访问的高级神经影像分析平台。

Abstract: Despite tremendous success in neuroimaging methodology, making large-scale, high-dimensional datasets ready for AI/ML applications remains a critical operational bottleneck. Conventional workflows require extensive manual effort across metadata curation, pipeline execution, post-processing quality control, and data management, a burden that disproportionately excludes laboratories with limited manpower and computational infrastructure. To address this real-world barrier, there is an urgent need for scalable, cost-effective computational platforms that democratize advanced neuroimaging analytics and accelerate discoveries in mental health and clinical translation. Capitalizing on multi-agent LLM breakthroughs, we introduce CyberNeuro, an agentic workbench with a tailored local LLM-model ('WandaMind') for automated neuroimaging and health-data analysis. Driven by four dedicated agents (Planner, Validator, Dispatcher, and Reporter) communicating via a secure MCP bridge and a pinned execution layer, CyberNeuro enables researchers to execute complex workflows using natural language while maintaining clinical-grade data privacy. On the public NeuroBench suite, CyberNeuro increases held-out domain accuracy from 40% to 69% over the baseline model. Beyond automated metrics, the platform integrates a human-in-the-loop verification panel to ensure rigorous biomedical quality control. Across the same end-to-end 10-batch cohort workflow suite, the local WandaMind configuration completed all tasks with an estimated aggregate token count of about 10.6% using WandaMind and 61.7% using cloud providers of token usage, compared to Neuroclaw, respectively. The platform and its production-ready modules are available at https://wanda-cyberbench.com.

</details>


### [136] [TransMem: Transforming Hidden States into Memory for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29032)
*Haodong Lei,Junming Liu,Yirong Chen,Pinlong Cai,Botian Shi,Ding Wang,Hongsong Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: TransMem是一种轻量级推理时参数化记忆模块，通过转换稀疏历史隐藏状态为可复用记忆表示，显著提升长上下文LLM智能体的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）智能体在长交互历史中运行时，需要在过去的观察和行动中识别并利用任务相关证据。然而，当前生成过程往往未能充分利用先前计算表示中编码的有用信息，导致稀疏历史隐藏状态的潜力未被充分挖掘。

Method: 提出TransMem，一种轻量级推理时参数化记忆模块：1）将冻结LLM骨干的稀疏历史隐藏状态转换为可复用记忆表示；2）使用轻量级门控网络动态应用潜在干预到当前隐藏状态，无需重复编码前文；3）引入证据条件自蒸馏，让记忆增强的学生模型匹配仅含证据的教师模型的预测分布，以学习可迁移的记忆利用能力而非任务特定知识。

Result: 在LoCoMo、HotpotQA和MemoryAgentBench三个基准上验证有效性：LoCoMo提升11.58-29.25 F1，HotpotQA提升10.20-13.03 F1，MemoryAgentBench平均准确率从29.54%提升至40.00%。不同模型架构和规模均获得一致改进。

Conclusion: 稀疏历史隐藏状态可作为长上下文LLM智能体有效且高效的记忆底物，TransMem为无需重复编码上下文的记忆增强推理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly operate over long interaction histories, where effective reasoning requires identifying and exploiting task-relevant evidence distributed across past observations and actions. However, useful information encoded in previously computed representations is often underutilized during subsequent generation. We propose \textbf{TransMem}, a lightweight inference-time parametric memory module that transforms sparse historical hidden states from a frozen LLM backbone into reusable memory representations. TransMem uses a lightweight gating network to dynamically apply the latent intervention to the current hidden states, without repeatedly encoding the preceding context. To learn transferable memory utilization rather than task-specific knowledge, we introduce evidence-conditioned self-distillation. A memory-augmented student processes the full context and matches the predictive distribution of an evidence-only teacher that shares the same frozen backbone. Experiments on LoCoMo, HotpotQA, and MemoryAgentBench demonstrate consistent improvements across different model architectures and scales. TransMem yields gains of 11.58--29.25 $F_1$ on LoCoMo and 10.20--13.03 $F_1$ on HotpotQA, while improving the average MemoryAgentBench accuracy from 29.54\% to 40.00\%. These results establish sparse historical hidden states as an effective and efficient memory substrate for long-context LLM agents. Our code is available at https://github.com/Haodong-Lei-Ray/TransMem.

</details>


### [137] [SeekBrain: An Autonomous Multi-Agent System for Accelerating Neuroscience Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29347)
*Jiamin Wu,Peishan Xiang,Jingyang Chen,Yuqing Zhu,Yuxi Li,Ling Luo,Qihao Zheng,Jialiang Zu,Yongchao Wu,Mindong Liu,Haitao Wu,Chaofan Hu,Yijie Sun,Yuqi Hang,Yu Zhu,Shuo Li,Yue Fan,Shiyang Feng,Wanghan Xu,Tianlei Zhang,Jie Zhang,Wenlong Zhang,Bo Zhang,Kai Wang,Lei Bai,Mianxin Liu,Wanli Ouyang,Jiulin Du,Chunfeng Song*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了SeekBrain，一个自主多智能体框架，通过领域引导的分层规划和跨模态数据分析加速神经科学发现，在BrainArena基准测试中显著优于现有方法，并在实际研究中成功揭示了幼年斑马鱼和小鼠的神经表征规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代神经科学依赖于整合多尺度、多模态数据集来揭示智能背后的神经原理。然而，高度异构的数据和碎片化的工作流程所带来的分析挑战正日益制约科学发现，现有方法难以有效处理这些复杂性问题。

Method: 引入SeekBrain自主多智能体框架，通过领域引导的分层规划和跨模态数据分析加速发现。该框架动态构建从代码-论文对中提取的分析方案库，将编码化的专业知识与智能体规划和执行引擎相结合，能够按需扩展性地生成假设和分析流程。

Result: 在专家标注的BrainArena基准测试上的系统评估表明，SeekBrain在各种分析任务中显著优于最先进的智能体基线方法。在实际研究部署中，SeekBrain成功整合了行为、神经和解剖数据，揭示了幼年斑马鱼行为的结构化分布式神经表征，以及小鼠决策任务中跨脑区的区域解码强度共享轴。

Conclusion: SeekBrain作为一个可扩展且实用的工具，能够有效加速神经科学中的数据驱动发现，为处理多模态异构数据和自动化分析流程提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern neuroscience relies on integrating multi-scale, multimodal datasets to uncover the neural principles underlying intelligence. However, analytical challenges posed by highly heterogeneous data and fragmented workflows increasingly constrain discoveries. Here we introduce SeekBrain, an autonomous multi-agent framework designed to accelerate neuroscience discovery through domain-grounded hierarchical planning and cross-modal data analysis. SeekBrain dynamically constructs a repertoire of analysis recipes extracted from code-paper pairs. By coupling this codified expertise with agentic planning and execution engines, the framework scalably generates hypotheses and analytical pipelines on demand. Systematic evaluation on the expert-annotated BrainArena benchmark demonstrates that SeekBrain substantially outperforms state-of-the-art agent baselines across various analysis tasks. Crucially, when deployed in real-world research, SeekBrain integrated behavioral, neural, and anatomical data to reveal structured, distributed neural representations of larval zebrafish behavior and a shared axis of regional decoding strength across the brain in a mouse decision-making task. These results establish SeekBrain as a scalable and practical tool for accelerating data-driven discoveries in neuroscience.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [138] [Sovereign Cognitive Digital Twins: Fusing 6G ISAC, AI-RAN, and Zero-Trust Edge Grids for National Resilience in the Global South](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28756)
*Zoe Aiyanna M. Cayetano,George M. Gichuru,Taijuo T. Morris*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出面向小岛屿发展中国家的主权认知数字孪生(S-CDT)架构，利用6G网络作为分布式雷达网格感知海平面上升、气旋等灾害，并在巴巴多斯2km²研究区域实现了可复现的地理数据到射线追踪管道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小岛屿发展中国家面临海平面上升加速、气旋增强和风暴潮威胁，但地面监测设备稀疏，难以实现及时的风险感知。现有感知系统相互割裂，利用率低，迫切需要一种低成本、高效益的统一感知方案来提升灾害监测能力。

Method: 提出六层S-CDT架构，核心是将集成感知与通信(ISAC)技术应用于6G无线接口，将通信网络本身作为感知底层；物理层映射到ETSI GR ISC 001和3GPP Release 19框架；设计信念状态控制循环（扩展卡尔曼滤波器+近端策略优化智能体）以处理O-RAN遥测延迟；实现从LiDAR数据到Sionna射线追踪场景的自动化管道，在巴巴多斯遗产区进行多频段仿真。

Result: 在576栋建筑、70x70地形网格的2km²研究区域，相同几何条件下：1.8GHz最佳服务路径增益中值为-106dB，10GHz降至-122dB；28/60GHz纯混凝土仿真分别达-130/-136dB，覆盖收缩至视距区域。验证了高频段在城市峡谷环境中的传播特性。

Conclusion: S-CDT为小岛屿国家提供了网络即传感器的范式转变方案，通过复用6G网络基础设施实现分布式感知，将数据主权和物理层零信任安全作为首要设计约束。巴巴多斯作为参考部署案例，菲律宾作为群岛推广案例，为ISAC技术在发展中国家的应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Small-island developing states face accelerating sea-level rise, intensifying cyclones, and storm surge, while suffering the sparse ground instrumentation that makes timely hazard perception difficult.
