This paper argues for a shift from passive cellular connectivity to the Network as a Sensor, realized through a Sovereign Cognitive Digital Twin (S-CDT): a federated national digital twin whose perceptual substrate is the 6G radio interface itself. Instead of disjoint sensing systems, a nation can reuse the Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC) waveforms of its own network as a distributed radar mesh. We specify a six-layer S-CDT stack in which ISAC collapses the boundary between the dynamic-data and communication layers; we map the physical layer to the ETSI GR ISC 001 and 3GPP Release 19 sensing frameworks; and we formulate a belief-state control loop, an Extended Kalman Filter feeding a Proximal Policy Optimization agent, designed to absorb O-RAN telemetry delay. That loop is specified but not evaluated here. Beyond the reference architecture, we implement a reproducible, CPU-only geodata-to-ray-tracing pipeline over a 2 km study area at the Barbados Heritage District, Newton Plantation: 576 LiDAR-height buildings, a 70x70 terrain grid, and the government tower register become a Sionna RT scene. On identical geometry, median best-server path gain falls from -106 dB at 1.8 GHz to -122 dB at 10 GHz; concrete-only 28/60 GHz runs yield -130/-136 dB, with coverage contracting to line-of-sight lobes. These uncalibrated, 1x1 V-polarized simulations are framed as a towards-6G site model of the background channel, not an operational ISAC deployment. We treat data sovereignty and physical-layer zero-trust security as first-order design constraints. Barbados (166 km2, ~280k population) is the reference deployment, with the Philippines as an archipelagic generalization.

</details>


### [139] [When Unlearning Fails: Reliable Data Deletion under Post-Training in Agent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28829)
*Zihao Ding,Jun Huang,Liang Dong*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 针对自改进联邦智能体网络中的"影响回声"问题，提出MUTE方法，通过服务器账本估计下游影响、隔离高影响轨迹和审计机制，在保持任务性能的同时实现可靠的数据删除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自改进联邦智能体网络在部署后持续收集新轨迹进行训练，形成闭环。当数据所有者请求删除数据时，目标数据可能已经影响后续保留的轨迹，导致传统重训练或模型侧遗忘方法留下"影响回声"，随着网络继续运行而重新出现，使得遗忘比一次性模型修复更困难。

Method: 提出MUTE（Muting Unlearned Trajectories' Echoes）方法：1）从轻量级服务器账本估计下游影响；2）通过遗忘-保留更新移除当前残留；3）通过隔离或降权处理高影响保留轨迹；4）审计后续行为以在上行链路预算下安排额外擦除。

Result: 在LIBERO上的实验表明，MUTE能够有效降低行为泄露和影响再生，同时保持任务实用性，且通信开销远低于完全重训练。实验覆盖了两种视觉-语言-动作骨干网络、三种删除粒度和基于Jetson的物理边缘测试平台。

Conclusion: MUTE成功解决了自改进联邦智能体网络中的影响回声问题，实现了可靠的数据删除，证明了在闭环学习系统中有效清除数据影响的可行性，为联邦学习系统的数据隐私保护提供了新思路。

Abstract: Self-improving federated agent networks keep training after deployment by collecting new trajectories with the current policy and feeding them back into later rounds. This closed loop makes unlearning harder than a one-time model repair. When a data owner requests deletion, the target data may have already shaped later retained trajectories, so retraining or model-side unlearning can leave an influence echo that returns as the network continues to operate. We show that this echo survives retained-data retraining, grows with the amount of forget-shaped retained data, and can be traced from deployment, collection, and aggregation records. To address this problem, we propose MUTE, a Muting Unlearned Trajectories' Echoes method for reliable deletion in self-improving federated agent networks. MUTE estimates downstream influence from a lightweight server ledger, removes the current residue through a forget-retain update, contains high-influence retained trajectories through quarantine or down-weighting, and audits later behavior to schedule additional erasure under an uplink budget. Experiments on LIBERO with two vision-language-action backbones, three deletion granularities, and a physical Jetson-based edge testbed show that MUTE keeps behavioral leakage and influence regeneration low while preserving task utility and using much less communication than full retraining.

</details>


### [140] [Point2Radio: A Foundation Model for Cross-Scene Radio Fields from Material-Aware Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28994)
*Chaozheng Wen,Chenghong Bian,Hongze Chen,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Point2Radio，一个跨场景迁移的无线传播基础模型，利用材质感知点云在毫秒级预测路径增益和功率角谱，相比基线模型误差降低76.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的高保真无线场仿真方法需要对每个场景-发射器配置单独仿真或拟合，未能利用不同环境间共享的传播结构，导致效率低下且泛化能力不足。

Method: 提出Point2Radio基础模型，使用共享编码器从多环境学习可迁移的传播先验。输入材质感知点云和发射器设置，生成可查询的场景表示；任务特定解码器将其映射为路径增益场和功率角谱等无线量。推理时仅需点云和收发器查询，无需网格或显式路径追踪。

Result: 在包含337个场景、86,272个TX条件场的数据集上评估，Point2Radio路径增益预测达到0.871 dB平均绝对误差，相比同数据集UNet基线误差降低76.7%。同一编码器通过任务特定解码器支持功率角谱预测。少量目标场景微调可进一步提升特定环境的适应性。

Conclusion: Point2Radio成功学习到跨环境共享的传播先验，实现了快速、高精度的无线场预测，为无线传播建模提供了一种高效的解决方案，具有良好的迁移能力和实用价值。

Abstract: High-fidelity radio fields are typically simulated for every scene--transmitter configuration or fitted separately to each scene, failing to exploit propagation structures shared across environments. We present Point2Radio, a foundation model that learns a transferable propagation prior from multiple environments. Given a material-aware point cloud and a transmitter (TX) setting, a common encoder produces a TX-conditioned scene representation that can be queried at arbitrary receiver (RX) locations. Task-specific query decoders map this representation to different radio quantities, e.g., three-dimensional (3D) path-gain (PG) fields and power angular spectra (PAS). At inference for a new scene, the model uses only a material-aware point cloud and transceiver queries, running in milliseconds on a single GPU without meshes or explicit path tracing. We evaluate PG prediction on a scene-disjoint split of a 337-scene corpus containing 86,272 TX-conditioned fields. Point2Radio achieves 0.871 dB mean absolute error (MAE), reducing error by 76.7% relative to a same-split UNet-style baseline. The same encoder also supports PAS prediction via a task-specific decoder. Experiments further show that light target-scene fine-tuning improves adaptation to a specific environment.

</details>


### [141] [Mind the Gap: Policy vs Reality in Post-Quantum TLS Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29005)
*Nimesha Wickramasinghe,Frank Li,Sanjay Jha,Arash Shaghaghi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文首次对后量子TLS（PQ-TLS）采用情况进行纵向测量研究，分析了来自11个全球分布观测点的100万个域名的20多亿次TLS握手。研究发现政策指导与实际部署存在显著差距：PQ-TLS部署高度集中于单一混合构造，主要由托管基础设施提供商推动，国家时间表和部门优先级与实际部署模式对应有限。PQ-TLS在实际互联网环境中未引入明显的延迟增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着NIST标准化PQC并发布过渡路线图，各国政府和标准机构也发布了迁移时间表、优先领域和部署策略。然而，这些政策在技术规定和紧迫性方面存在显著差异，不清楚PQC在实践中被广泛采用的程度，以及政策差异如何转化为可观察的部署结果。为弥补这一知识空白，需要进行大规模的实证研究来了解PQ-TLS的实际部署情况。

Method: 研究人员建立了首个PQ-TLS采用的纵向测量研究，通过建立超过20亿次TLS握手，分析来自11个全球分布观测点的100万个域名的密码协商行为。这种大规模实证方法使研究人员能够观察和量化实际的PQ-TLS部署模式和配置行为。

Result: 研究发现配置趋同现象：PQ-TLS部署绝大多数集中在单一混合构造上，大部分表观进展由托管基础设施提供商推动。国家时间表和部门优先级与观察到的部署模式对应有限。与早期实验研究表明的可测量开销相反，PQ-TLS在互联网环境中未引入有意义的延迟增加，尽管经常与传统TLS配置一起部署。

Conclusion: 研究结果突出了政策期望与早期部署现实之间的差距，为更接地气的PQ-TLS转型提供了实证洞察。这表明需要根据实际部署情况调整政策和期望，并为未来的PQ-TLS转型提供基于实证的指导。

Abstract: Post-quantum cryptography (PQC) has evolved from a long-term planning concern into an operational priority. Following NIST's standardization of PQC, governments and standard bodies published transition roadmaps outlining migration timelines, priority sectors, and deployment strategies. However, our survey of these policies reveals substantial divergence in technical prescriptions and urgency. It remains unclear how widely PQC has been adopted in practice and how policy differences translate into observable deployment outcomes.
  To address this gap, we present the first longitudinal measurement study of post-quantum TLS (PQ-TLS) adoption. By establishing more than 2 billion TLS handshakes, we analyze cryptographic negotiation behavior across 1 million domains from 11 globally distributed vantage points. Despite varied policy guidance, we observe configuration convergence: PQ-TLS deployment overwhelmingly centers on a single hybrid construction, and much of the apparent progress is driven by managed infrastructure providers. National timelines and sectoral priorities show limited correspondence with observed deployment patterns. Contrary to early experimental studies suggesting measurable overhead, we find that PQ-TLS introduces no meaningful latency increase in Internet settings, although it is frequently deployed alongside legacy TLS configurations. Together, these findings highlight a gap between policy expectations and early deployment reality, and provide empirical insight to inform more grounded PQ-TLS transition.

</details>


### [142] [METIS: A Declarative Slice Orchestrator for Application-Centric 5G/6G Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29282)
*Arman Divband,Ali Yaghoubian,Navid Nikaein*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了METIS，一个声明式网络切片编排器，通过级联协调循环管理网络切片实例的全生命周期，解决了跨无线接入网和核心网的QoS协调问题，并揭示了上行链路需要无线侧强制执行的关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络切片编排器依赖网络中心化数据模型、命令式工作流和静态切片模板，而O-RAN和3GPP分别独立处理无线侧和核心侧切片控制，导致跨域切片级QoS强制执行缺乏协调。动态生命周期管理和端到端QoS保障仍未解决。

Method: METIS采用声明式编排方法，定义应用中心化数据模型，通过级联协调循环管理Day-0/1/2切片生命周期。系统从应用语义和QoE需求导出符合3GPP标准的切片配置，遵循5G QoS模型进行分层聚合，并联合协调O-RAN和3GPP切片机制。

Result: 在5G云原生测试床的校园活动场景中，METIS切片创建、更新、升级、删除时间分别为22.4、5.1、52.2、32.1秒；在多切片过载下保持SLA完全满足；支持63个切片实例跨9个区域，总CPU消耗低于0.03核；可在19秒内从注入故障中恢复切片。

Conclusion: 核心发现是端到端切片控制的结构不对称性：下行可在核心网整形，但上行离开UE时无监管，仅靠核心网切片无法可靠满足上行SLA，无线侧强制执行是必要的而非补充性的。METIS证明了声明式编排和跨域协调的有效性。

Abstract: Network slicing is the cornerstone of application-aware 5G and 6G networks, yet dynamic lifecycle management of network slice instances with coordinated quality-of-service enforcement across the radio access network and core network remains unresolved. Existing orchestrators rely on network-centric data models, imperative workflows, and static slice templates, while O-RAN addresses radio-side slice control independently of 3GPP core-side control, leaving slice-level quality-of-service enforcement uncoordinated across domains. This paper introduces METIS, a declarative slice orchestrator that manages the Day-0/1/2 lifecycle of network slice instances through cascaded reconciliation loops. METIS defines an application-centric data model for service profiles, enabling customers to describe the semantics and quality-of-experience requirements of their applications. From these, METIS derives 3GPP-aligned slice profiles via hierarchical aggregation following the 5G quality-of-service model, eliminating static templates, and jointly coordinates O-RAN and 3GPP slicing for slice instantiation and enforcement. Our central finding is a structural asymmetry in end-to-end slice control: downlink traffic can be shaped at the core before reaching the radio access network, but uplink leaves the user equipment unregulated, so core-only slicing cannot reliably satisfy uplink service-level agreements - radio-side enforcement is necessary, not merely complementary. Evaluated on a 5G cloud-native testbed in a campus-event scenario, METIS completes slice creation, update, upgrade, and deletion within 22.4, 5.1, 52.2, and 32.1 seconds, respectively; sustains full service-level-agreement satisfaction under concurrent multi-slice overload; scales to 63 slice instances across nine zones consuming under 0.03 processor cores total; and recovers slices from injected failures across four levels in under 19 seconds.

</details>


### [143] [RIGEL: Real-time Optical Anomaly Diagnosis with Stateful In-Network Inference based on Distributed On-switch GNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29306)
*Zhen Wei,Yidong Wang,Yufan Zhu,Xuefeng Yan,Binjun Tang,Xiaoliang Chen,Zuqing Zhu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了RIGEL，这是首个基于Tofino交换机实现有状态分布式网络内推理的实时光异常诊断系统，通过图神经网络（GNN）协同工作，在真实测试平台上验证了其高准确性和实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据密集型应用的快速增长，光网络管理变得复杂，实时光异常诊断成为必需功能。现有方法主要基于集中式数据分析，难以避免数据平面与控制平面之间消息交换带来的延迟和开销。

Method: 提出软硬件协同设计方案预处理高维光谱数据，使其适合硬件网络内推理；开发结合自编码器与GraphSAGE图神经网络的有效模型；提出通用方法将模型适配到Tofino交换机。

Result: 在真实的包交换光网络测试平台上验证，RIGEL实现了高准确度的诊断，能够及时检测和定位光异常，展现了超越现有最先进方法的优势。

Conclusion: RIGEL成功实现了首个完全在网络内进行的实时光异常诊断系统，通过分布式图神经网络协同推理，有效解决了集中式方案的延迟和开销问题，为光网络管理提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: The recent booming of data-intensive applications has complicated optical network management, making real-time optical anomaly diagnosis a must-have feature. However, existing approaches are mostly based on centralized data analytics and thus can hardly avoid the latency and overhead due to message exchanges between data and control planes. In this work, we propose and prototype RIGEL, which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first real-time optical anomaly diagnosis system that realizes stateful distributed in-network inference through collaborative graph neural networks (GNNs) on Tofino switches. The system is designed to be fully in-network, and a software-hardware co-design is proposed to preprocess high-dimensional spectral data for being suitable for hardware-based in-network inference. Next, we first develop an effective model to combine an autoencoder with a GraphSAGE-based GNN, and then propose a generalizable method to adapt the model to Tofino switch. The effectiveness of RIGEL is showcased in a realistic packet-over-optical network testbed, verifying that it achieves highly accurate diagnosis to detect and locate optical anomalies timely and highlighting its benefits over the state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [Optical Flow Sensor: A Direction-Selective Bionic Retina Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28686)
*Juchen Zhou,Bonan Yan,Yuchao Yang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种像素级光流传感器（OFS）集成电路，通过结合DVS事件比较与时间差测量实现片上全并行光流计算，功耗降低303倍，延迟达到微秒级，输出数据量减少3.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于帧的光流计算依赖密集采样和全局计算，导致高延迟和高功耗。生物视网膜通过局部ON/OFF通路和并行处理高效提取运动信息，本文旨在仿生这一机制以克服传统方法的局限性。

Method: 设计像素级光流传感器（OFS）集成电路，将动态视觉传感器（DVS）ON/OFF事件比较与时间差测量相结合，实现片上全并行光流计算；采用光流专用地址事件表示（OF-AER）接口支持低功耗、高吞吐量读出；进一步提出基于光学忆阻器的OFS以降低功耗和面积开销。

Result: 提出的OFS功耗相比FPGA加速的DVS系统降低303倍，同时保持微秒级延迟；通过直接输出光流向量，输出数据量减少约3.3倍；实验验证了其在超高速、低功耗视觉感知应用中的潜力。

Conclusion: 像素级OFS成功实现了高效、低延迟的片上光流计算，显著降低了功耗和数据输出量，为超高速、低功耗视觉感知应用提供了有力解决方案，光学忆阻器版本的提出进一步展示了未来优化的可能性。

Abstract: Optical flow characterizes motion in the visual field and is fundamental to motion perception and tracking in biological and artificial vision systems. Biological retinas extract motion efficiently through local ON/OFF pathways and parallel processing, while conventional frame-based optical flow relies on dense sampling and global computation, resulting in high latency and power consumption. To overcome these limitations, we present a pixel-level Optical Flow Sensor (OFS) integrated circuit. The design combines Dynamic Vision Sensor (DVS) ON/OFF event comparison with time-difference measurement to enable fully parallel optical flow computation on-chip. An optical-flow-specific Address-Event Representation (OF-AER) interface supports low-power, high-throughput readout. \rev{Based on the CMOS-based OFS, we further propose optical memristor-based OFS to reduce sensor power consumption and area overhead.} Experimental results show that the proposed OFS achieves a 303$\times$ reduction in power consumption compared with FPGA-accelerated DVS systems while maintaining microsecond-level latency. Moreover, by directly outputting optical flow vectors, the OFS reduces output data size by approximately 3.3$\times$, demonstrating strong potential for ultra-high-speed, low-power vision sensing applications.

</details>


### [145] [WitCert: Sound Runtime Risk Observability and Gating for KV-Cache Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28699)
*Fanzhe Wei,Li Liu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文为KV缓存量化提供了首个运行时误差界限监测器，能实时检测压缩是否损害当前请求，并通过门控机制恢复模型质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存量化仅通过离线基准平均值验证，部署系统无法判断压缩是否损害当前正在服务的请求，缺乏实时质量保障机制。

Method: 提出两层运行时监测器：1）确定性band-norm-witness界限，适用于任何缓存保留黑盒量化器和查询；2）更紧的概率证书，针对受控减抖INT8量化器。核心定理在Lean 4中机器验证。监测器通过环境守卫补丁集成到SGLang中。

Result: 观察到跨层误差抵消现象（28层扫描中单层污染均无损失）；原始FP8在困难RULER任务上从22.8恢复至79.7；认证INT8缓存在相同内存下可服务1.88倍KV令牌。

Conclusion: 证明了实时可观测性在KV缓存量化中的必要性和可行性，激进的量化方案依赖跨层误差抵消而非单步保真度，为LLM服务提供了安全、高效的量化部署方案。

Abstract: KV-cache quantization is validated today by offline benchmark averages; a deployed system cannot tell whether compression is damaging the request it is serving right now. We give it a provably sound runtime meter, a "DTrace for KV quantization": a per-(layer, head, step) upper bound on the total variation between exact and compressed attention. The meter has two tiers: a deterministic band-norm-witness bound, sound for any cache-preserving black-box quantizer and for any query (adaptive-safe, worst-case Cauchy-Schwarz plus RoPE band-unitarity), and a tighter probabilistic certificate for a controlled subtractively-dithered INT8 quantizer under an explicit request-level failure budget (stated for non-adaptive queries; core theorems machine-checked in Lean 4). Three results. Observability: the meter enters SGLang through an environment-guarded patch, and any scheme registered as one tensor function is measured in live serving. Repair: meter-driven gating, risk-ranked where the witness is saturated and certified where it is informative, empirically restores the quality floor at benchmark scale. For example, raw-cast FP8 improves from 22.8 back to 79.7 on hard RULER tasks, with the difference from uncompressed bounded at [+0.0, +0.8] by a paired test. Analysis: aggressive schemes survive on cross-layer error cancellation, not per-step fidelity. In a 28-layer sweep, no single layer's pollution alone loses anything (0/28), and the certified INT8 cache serves 1.88 times more KV tokens at the same memory in SGLang.

</details>


### [146] [Characterizing LLM Kernel Access and Memory Interaction in Multi-Partition NUMA GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28824)
*Donghyeon Joo,Sooraj Puthoor,Nuwan Jayasena,Bahar Asgari*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文针对多分区GPU上大语言模型（LLM）工作负载的非均匀内存访问和跨分区通信问题，提出了内存追踪分析方法，将LLM内核数据访问模式分为三类，并提出了相应的优化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）工作负载推动了对多分区GPU的需求，以扩展计算和内存容量。然而，多分区GPU的非均匀内存访问特性以及跨分区通信会加剧争用并降低局部性，导致内核延迟次优。因此，需要深入理解LLM内核在多分区GPU上的数据访问模式，以指导优化策略。

Method: 本文首先引入了内存追踪分析方法，用于推导工作组级别的数据访问和共享行为。随后，利用周期级模拟器评估局部性对延迟的影响。基于这些工具，作者将LLM内核的操作数分为三种跨工作组共享模式：全局共享、部分共享和私有。最后，针对不同类别提出了相应的优化策略，从简单的工作组级别固定到子组感知的协同调度。

Result: 研究发现，LLM内核操作数可以划分为三种不同的跨工作组共享模式，每种模式需要的优化策略各不相同。简单的每工作组固定策略适用于某些模式，而其他模式则需要更复杂的子组感知协同调度。实验结果表明，这些优化策略能够有效改善多分区GPU上的内核延迟。

Conclusion: 本研究强调了在多分区GPU上进行位置感知的内核编程的必要性，并指出需要更智能的架构支持来改善工作和数据局部性，从而提升LLM工作负载在多分区GPU上的性能。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) workloads motivate multi-partition GPUs as a path to scaling compute and memory capacity, but their non-uniform memory access characteristics and inter-partition communication can amplify contention and degrade locality, leading to suboptimal kernel latency. To address this, we analyze performance-critical LLM kernel implementations spanning weight projection, mixture-of-experts, and attention variants of state-of-the-art serving engines to present a characterization of data access patterns in multi-partition GPUs. First, we introduce memory trace analysis methodology to derive workgroup-level data access and sharing behavior, then evaluate the locality implications on latency using a cycle-level simulator. Using these tools, we categorize LLM kernel operands into three inter-workgroup sharing patterns (global, partial, or private) and show that the required optimization strategies differ across categories, from simple per-workgroup pinning to subgroup-aware co-scheduling. Our findings highlight the need for placement-aware kernel programming and smarter architectural support for work and data locality in multi-partition GPUs.

</details>


### [147] [Open-Source LLM-Driven Formal Verification: A Multi-Agent Pipeline for RTL Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28877)
*Ha Trung Tran*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个将LLM与开源形式化验证工具结合的多智能体流水线，通过反例引导迭代来修复RTL设计，实现了形式化证明保证的正确性修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代芯片设计中验证消耗了大部分设计工作量，但提供数学正确性保证的形式化验证工具仍然昂贵且许可限制严格。虽然LLM在硬件设计方面显示出潜力，但现有的RTL修复方法要么通过仿真验证结果（仅测试部分输入），要么依赖商业工具，很少有将形式化证明与完全开源工具链结合的方法。

Method: 提出一个多智能体流水线，将LLM与开源形式化后端（Yosys、SymbiYbosys和Z3）结合，通过反例引导迭代修复RTL。框架生成形式化属性，验证设计，并将反例反馈给LLM，直到通过k-induction证明设计正确或达到迭代预算上限。

Result: 通过ALU案例研究显示流水线能够检测并修复真实的功能性错误并提供正确性的形式化证明。在六个基准测试集中，一个设计被可靠修复，并识别出四种不同的失败模式：有界覆盖空洞性、规范歧义性、时序逻辑错误和多属性压力。

Conclusion: 这项工作被定位为可行性研究，并提供了详细的失败分析，同时报告了Yosys bind指令的实践限制，这与开源形式化验证社区相关。研究为将LLM与开源形式化验证工具结合进行RTL修复提供了初步验证和深入分析。

Abstract: Verification consumes the majority of modern chip design effort, yet the formal verification tools that provide mathematical guarantees of correctness remain expensive and restrictively licensed. While large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for hardware design, existing approaches to RTL repair validate their results through simulation - which exercises only a subset of inputs - or rely on commercial tools, and few combine formal proof with an entirely open-source toolchain. In this paper, we present a multi-agent pipeline that couples an LLM with an open-source formal backend (Yosys, SymbiYosys, and Z3) to repair RTL through counterexample-guided iteration: the framework generates formal properties, verifies the design, and feeds counterexamples back to the LLM until the design is proved correct by k-induction or an iteration budget is exhausted. Through an ALU case study, we show that the pipeline can detect and repair a real functional bug with a formal proof of correctness. Across a six-benchmark suite, one design is repaired reliably, and we characterize four distinct failure modes: bounded-cover vacuity, specification ambiguity, temporal-logic bugs, and multi-property pressure. We frame this work as a feasibility study with a detailed failure analysis, and additionally report a practical limitation of the Yosys bind directive relevant to the open-source formal verification community.

</details>


### [148] [Selective KV Cache Protection for Noise-Resilient LLM Inference on Analog Compute-In-Memory Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29076)
*Yuannuo Feng,Wenyong Zhou,Yuang Ma,Yizhe Chen,Wenshuai Yao,Yuxin Xie,Ngai Wong,Wang Kang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了模拟存内计算（CIM）阵列上的动态注意力计算，发现硬件噪声对初始和近期令牌影响严重。提出了分层令牌保护策略，将关键令牌通过高精度数字路径处理，同时利用模拟CIM处理大量KV缓存，有效降低了困惑度并提高了资源利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟存内计算（CIM）阵列在大语言模型推理中具有能效优势，但将其扩展到注意力机制面临核心挑战：KV缓存操作需要频繁的片上权重更新，与权重固定范式不匹配，导致动态计算暴露于显著的硬件噪声之下。这一问题在先前研究中未被充分探索。

Method: 提出分层令牌保护策略：将sink令牌和滑动近期令牌窗口保持在更高精度的数字路径上，同时在模拟CIM上处理大量KV缓存。设计了协同调度器，结合模拟编程、所有权转移和批量MVM瓦片形成，以控制数字路径的开销。

Result: 在九个大语言模型上的评估表明，该方法将模拟噪声下的平均困惑度从33.91降至11.95，接近11.06的纯净基线；同时将动态KV编程行利用率从23.1%提升至91.2%。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了动态注意力计算在模拟CIM上的噪声敏感性，提出了有效的分层保护策略，在保持模型性能的同时显著提升了硬件资源利用率，为模拟CIM在LLM注意力机制中的实际应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Analog compute-in-memory (CIM) arrays have emerged as a promising substrate for energy-efficient LLM inference, particularly for weight-stationary computations in linear layers. However, extending analog CIM to attention mechanisms introduces a fundamental challenge: KV cache operations demand repeated in-situ weight updates, and the resulting mismatch with the weight-stationary paradigm exposes dynamic computations to significant hardware noise, a critical problem that remains largely unexplored. In this paper, we present the first systematic study of dynamic attention computation on analog CIM arrays, revealing that initial and recent tokens exhibit disproportionate vulnerability to hardware noise. Motivated by this token-level insight, we propose a hierarchical token protection strategy that keeps sink tokens and a sliding recent-token window on a higher-precision digital path while processing the bulk KV cache on analog CIM. A co-designed scheduler combines analog programming, ownership transition, and bulk-MVM tile formation to bound digital overhead. Evaluations on nine LLMs show that our approach lowers average perplexity under analog noise from 33.91 to 11.95, close to the clean baseline of 11.06, while improving dynamic-KV programming-row utilization from 23.1\% to 91.2\%.

</details>


### [149] [RTLCurator: Label-Efficient Data Curation for RTL Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29283)
*Siyang Cai,Cangyuan Li,Wenjing Chang,Kun Wang,Haoyu Gao,Yinhe Han,Ying Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出RTLCurator方法，通过学习行为感知的兼容性先验来筛选RTL语料库，在仅验证10%数据的情况下保留80%语料库，相比使用完整语料库训练在所有指标上均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练大语言模型生成RTL需要大量规范-代码配对数据，但此类数据稀缺，大多数公开语料库为合成生成。合成数据存在正确性问题，两个广泛使用的RTL数据集中仅24.4%和53.5%的配对通过功能测试。仅按正确性筛选会导致语料库仅包含短小简单模块，且正确性本身难以获得和验证。

Method: 提出RTLCurator方法：(1)通过对比每个规范与仿真失败的实现来学习行为感知的兼容性先验；(2)使用少量验证过的配对将先验校准到新语料库；(3)通过平衡对齐性、表示覆盖率和RTL结构丰富度来构建保留子集。

Result: 在CodeV和RTLCoder数据集上，使用RTLCurator保留80%语料库，在所有报告指标上均优于使用完整语料库训练，且仅需验证10%的数据池。相比之下，仅按分数排序的效果低于随机选择，对整个数据池进行仿真筛选也没有更好效果。

Conclusion: RTLCurator提供了一种有效的RTL语料库筛选方法，能够在保证数据质量的同时避免过度筛选导致的结构多样性损失，为LLM的RTL代码生成训练提供了更优的数据选择策略。

Abstract: Training large language models (LLMs) to write register-transfer level (RTL) requires large corpora of paired specifications and code, and such data is scarce enough that most public corpora are now synthesized. Synthesis provides scale but not correctness, and in two widely used RTL datasets only 24.4% and 53.5% of pairs pass generated functional tests. This raises the question of how much of such a corpus to keep and which part of it. Correctness alone is a poor answer. A pair that misbehaves in one corner case still shows valid syntax and interface conventions, and complex sequential designs are both harder to generate and harder to validate, so filtering by correctness leaves a corpus of short and simple modules. Correctness is also hard to obtain, since behavior leaves little trace on the surface in RTL, and validating an entire corpus only sorts pairs into passed and failed. We present RTLCurator, which learns a behavior-aware compatibility prior by contrasting each specification with implementations that fail simulation, and calibrates it to a new corpus using a small number of validated pairs. It then constructs the retained subset by balancing alignment, representation coverage, and RTL structural richness. On CodeV and RTLCoder, keeping 80% of the corpus this way improves on training with the full corpus across all reported metrics while validating only 10% of the pool, whereas ranking by the score alone falls below random selection and filtering the whole pool by simulation does no better.

</details>


### [150] [Low-Power PLL-Based Clock Stabilization for Flexible IGZO AMS Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29357)
*Paula Carolina Lozano Duarte,Georgios Zervakis,Mehdi Tahoori*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出首个针对n型非晶IGZO TFT技术的PLL架构，作为低带宽时域稳定器，在柔性电子时钟生成中实现了功率降低400倍以上、面积缩小超过1500倍的突破性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性电子平台中的模拟混合信号电路主导着系统功能和功耗，片上时钟生成成为关键且功耗敏感的功能。现有基于振荡器的解决方案存在无界的工艺-电压-温度(PVT)漂移问题，导致信号完整性下降；而替代时钟源可能消耗高达90%的系统总功耗预算，使其不适用于柔性电子平台。因此时钟生成成为主要的功耗和能效设计约束。

Method: 提出首个专为n型非晶氧化铟镓锌(a-IGZO)薄膜晶体管(TFT)技术设计的锁相环(PLL)架构，解决柔性电子特有的约束：缺少p型器件、载流子迁移率有限、强PVT变化性。设计作为低带宽时域稳定器运作：自由运行的环振荡器压控振荡器(VCO)通过最小电荷泵反馈环路进行软调节，以限制长期频率漂移，无需持续的高质量外部参考信号。

Result: PLL支持1 kHz至300 kHz频率范围，占用0.0115-0.0233 mm²面积，功耗0.115-0.153 mW。与现有柔性电子时钟方案相比，功耗降低超过400倍；与柔性VCO相比面积减小超过1500倍，与环振荡器方案相比减小超过390倍。在四个代表性已发表IGZO AMS系统中验证，实现2.24 ns的均方根周期抖动和1000 ppm以内的长期频率精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的PLL架构成功解决了柔性电子平台中时钟生成的功耗和面积瓶颈问题，在保持良好时钟稳定性的同时，相比现有方案实现了数量级的功耗和面积优化，为柔性电子系统提供了一种可行的参考锚定时钟稳定解决方案。

Abstract: Flexible electronics (FE) platforms rely on analog and mixed-signal (AMS) circuits - biosensors, readout front-ends, and analog-to-digital converters - that dominate both functionality and energy consumption, making on-chip clock generation an essential yet power-critical function. Existing oscillator-based solutions suffer from unbounded process, voltage, and temperature (PVT) drift that degrades signal integrity, while alternative clock sources can consume up to 90% of the total system power budget, rendering them inapplicable to FE platforms and elevating clock generation to a primary power and energy-efficiency design constraint. This paper presents the first phase-locked loop (PLL) architecture designed for n-type-only amorphous indium-gallium-zinc oxide (a-IGZO) thin-film transistor (TFT) technology, addressing FE-specific constraints such as the absence of p-type devices, limited carrier mobility, and strong PVT variability. Rather than targeting high-precision frequency synthesis, the proposed design operates as a low-bandwidth temporal stabilizer: a free-running ring-oscillator-based voltage-controlled oscillator (VCO) is softly regulated by a minimal charge-pump feedback loop to bound long-term frequency drift without requiring a continuous high-quality external reference. The proposed PLL supports frequencies from 1 kHz to 300 kHz while occupying 0.0115-0.0233 mm2 and consuming 0.115-0.153 mW. Compared with prior oscillator-based FE clocking solutions, our architecture reduces power by more than 400x while achieving footprint reductions exceeding 1500x compared to flexible VCOs, and more than 390x with respect to ring-oscillator solutions. Validated across four representative published IGZO AMS systems, the proposed PLL achieves an rms period jitter of 2.24 ns and a long-term frequency accuracy within 1000 ppm, providing reference-anchored clock stability in FE platforms.

</details>
